*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_HELP_FIELDS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_help_fields .

  DATA: $x030l               TYPE x030l,
        $subrc               TYPE sy-subrc.

  REFRESH nametab.

* Get the structure of the Header table.
  CALL FUNCTION 'NAMETAB_GET'
    EXPORTING
      langu               = sy-langu
      only                = ' '
      tabname             = 'PA9001'
    IMPORTING
      header              = $x030l
      rc                  = $subrc
    TABLES
      nametab             = nametab
    EXCEPTIONS
      internal_error      = 0
      table_has_no_fields = 0
      table_not_activ     = 0.

  SELECT * FROM zp902_activity
         INTO TABLE tb_activity.

  SELECT * FROM zp902_cause
         INTO TABLE tb_cause.

  SELECT * FROM zp902_where
         INTO TABLE tb_where.

  SELECT f~location_code f~active_status f~sort_order f~other_text
         j~location_desc
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE tb_location
         FROM zp902_location AS f
         INNER JOIN zp902_locationt AS j
         ON    f~location_code = j~location_code.
  SORT tb_location BY sort_order.

  SELECT f~nature_code f~active_status f~sort_order f~complaint_popup
         f~bullying f~other_text j~nature_desc
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE tb_nature
         FROM zp902_nature AS f
         INNER JOIN zp902_naturet AS j
         ON    f~nature_code = j~nature_code.
  SORT tb_nature BY sort_order.

  SELECT * FROM zp902_treatopt
         INTO TABLE tb_treatopt.

  SELECT * FROM zp902_currfit
         INTO TABLE tb_currfit.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_HELP_FIELDS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  DROP_DOWN_INC_INJ
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM drop_down_inc_inj .

  REFRESH gt_ddval.

  LOOP AT tb_location INTO ls_location.
    IF w_1000_action = 'DISP' OR
       ls_location-active_status = 'A'.
      gs_ddval-handle = '1' .
      CONCATENATE ls_location-location_code+1(2)
                  ls_location-location_desc
                  INTO gs_ddval-value SEPARATED BY ' '.
      APPEND gs_ddval TO gt_ddval .
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " DROP_DOWN_INC_INJ

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  DROP_DOWN_INC_NAT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM drop_down_inc_nat .

  LOOP AT tb_nature INTO ls_nature.
    IF w_1000_action = 'DISP' OR
       ls_nature-active_status = 'A'.
      gs_ddval-handle = '2' .
      CONCATENATE ls_nature-nature_code+1(2)
                  ls_nature-nature_desc
                  INTO gs_ddval-value SEPARATED BY ' '.
      APPEND gs_ddval TO gt_ddval .
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " DROP_DOWN_INC_NAT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_1002A
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_GT_OUTTAB1  text
*      -->P_ER_DATA_CHANGED  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM handle_data_changed_1002a  USING ir_data_changed
                         TYPE REF TO cl_alv_changed_data_protocol.

*  DATA: ls_mod_cell       TYPE lvc_s_modi,
*         lv_value          TYPE lvc_value,
*         lv_ucomm          TYPE sy-ucomm,
*         l_code(2)         TYPE n,
*         l_amount          TYPE zotm_meal_rate-meal_rate,
*         l_field(20)       TYPE c,
*         l_date            TYPE d.
*
*  FIELD-SYMBOLS : <fs>.
*
*  LOOP AT ir_data_changed->mt_mod_cells
*          INTO ls_mod_cell
*          WHERE fieldname = 'MAIN_INJ'.
*    CALL METHOD ir_data_changed->get_cell_value
*      EXPORTING
*        i_row_id    = ls_mod_cell-row_id
*        i_fieldname = ls_mod_cell-fieldname
*      IMPORTING
*        e_value     = lv_value.
*
*    IF lv_value = abap_true.
*      LOOP AT gt_outtab1 INTO gr_outtab1.
*        IF sy-tabix NE ls_mod_cell-row_id.
*          IF gr_outtab1-main_inj IS NOT INITIAL.
*            CLEAR gr_outtab1-main_inj.
*            MODIFY gt_outtab1 FROM gr_outtab1.
*          ENDIF.
*        ENDIF.
*      ENDLOOP.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDLOOP.
*
*
** If there is less than 2 blank lines, add more
*  DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab1 LINES w_lines.
*  CLEAR w_blank_lines.
*  LOOP AT gt_outtab1 INTO gr_outtab1
*       WHERE desc IS INITIAL.
*    ADD 1 TO w_blank_lines.
*  ENDLOOP.
*  IF w_blank_lines LT 3.
*    WHILE w_blank_lines LT 3.
*      CLEAR gr_outtab1.
*      APPEND gr_outtab1 TO gt_outtab1.
*      ADD 1 TO w_blank_lines.
*    ENDWHILE.
*  ENDIF.
*
** When this event is called the screen display does not get updated.
** This code forces the PAI process to be executed and subsequently
** the screen fields get updated.
*  w_ucomm = 'NEXT'.
*  CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>set_new_ok_code
*    EXPORTING
*      new_code = w_ucomm.
*
*  w_changed = abap_true.

ENDFORM.                    " HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_1002A

FORM handle_data_changed_1002b  USING ir_data_changed
                         TYPE REF TO cl_alv_changed_data_protocol.

*  DATA: ls_mod_cell       TYPE lvc_s_modi,
*         lv_value          TYPE lvc_value,
*         lv_ucomm          TYPE sy-ucomm,
*         l_code(2)         TYPE n,
*         l_amount          TYPE zotm_meal_rate-meal_rate,
*         l_field(20)       TYPE c,
*         l_date            TYPE d.
*
*  FIELD-SYMBOLS : <fs>.
*
*  LOOP AT ir_data_changed->mt_mod_cells
*          INTO ls_mod_cell
*          WHERE fieldname = 'MAIN_NAT'.
*    CALL METHOD ir_data_changed->get_cell_value
*      EXPORTING
*        i_row_id    = ls_mod_cell-row_id
*        i_fieldname = ls_mod_cell-fieldname

*          IF gr_outtab2-main_nat IS NOT INITIAL.

*            MODIFY gt_outtab2 FROM gr_outtab2.
*          ENDIF.
*        ENDIF.
*      ENDLOOP.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDLOOP.

*
** If there is less than 2 blank lines, add more
*  DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab2 LINES w_lines.
*  CLEAR w_blank_lines.
*  LOOP AT gt_outtab2 INTO gr_outtab2
*       WHERE desc IS INITIAL.
*    ADD 1 TO w_blank_lines.

*  IF w_blank_lines LT 3.
*    WHILE w_blank_lines LT 3.
*      CLEAR gr_outtab2.
*      APPEND gr_outtab2 TO gt_outtab2.
*      ADD 1 TO w_blank_lines.
*    ENDWHILE.
*  ENDIF.
*
** When this event is called the screen display does not get updated.
** This code forces the PAI process to be executed and subsequently
** the screen fields get updated.
*  w_ucomm = 'NEXT'.
*  CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>set_new_ok_code
*    EXPORTING
*      new_code = w_ucomm.

ENDFORM.                    " HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_1002B

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_2105
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_GT_OUTTAB1  text
*      -->P_ER_DATA_CHANGED  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM handle_data_changed_2105  USING ir_data_changed
                         TYPE REF TO cl_alv_changed_data_protocol.

  DATA: ls_mod_cell       TYPE lvc_s_modi,
         lv_value          TYPE lvc_value,
         lv_ucomm          TYPE sy-ucomm,
         l_code(2)         TYPE n,
         l_amount          TYPE zotm_meal_rate-meal_rate,
         l_field(20)       TYPE c,
         l_date            TYPE d.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS : <fs>.

  LOOP AT ir_data_changed->mt_mod_cells
          INTO ls_mod_cell
          WHERE fieldname = 'MED_PRAC'.
    CALL METHOD ir_data_changed->get_cell_value
      EXPORTING
        i_row_id    = ls_mod_cell-row_id
        i_fieldname = ls_mod_cell-fieldname
      IMPORTING
        e_value     = lv_value.

    READ TABLE gt_outtab6 INTO gr_outtab6 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
    gr_outtab6-med_prac = lv_value.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_med
           WHERE med_prac = gr_outtab6-med_prac.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab6-name  = zim_med-name.
      gr_outtab6-phone = zim_med-phone.
      gr_outtab6-fax   = zim_med-fax.
      gr_outtab6-email = zim_med-email.
      gr_outtab6-health_prof = zim_med-health_prof.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_med_proft
             WHERE sprsl = 'E'
               AND health_prof = gr_outtab6-health_prof.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab6-health_desc = zim_med_proft-health_desc.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_outtab6-ntd = abap_true.
        zim_in_mast-ntd = gr_outtab6-med_prac.
        w_ntd_name      = zim_med-name.
        w_ntd_phone     = zim_med-phone.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_outtab6-nts = abap_true.
        zim_in_mast-nts = gr_outtab6-med_prac.
        w_nts_name      = zim_med-name.
        w_nts_phone     = zim_med-phone.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR : gr_outtab6-name, gr_outtab6-phone, gr_outtab6-health_prof,
              gr_outtab6-health_desc.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab6 FROM gr_outtab6 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT ir_data_changed->mt_mod_cells
          INTO ls_mod_cell
          WHERE fieldname = 'NTD'.
    CALL METHOD ir_data_changed->get_cell_value
      EXPORTING
        i_row_id    = ls_mod_cell-row_id
        i_fieldname = ls_mod_cell-fieldname
      IMPORTING
        e_value     = lv_value.

    IF lv_value = abap_true.
      LOOP AT gt_outtab6 INTO gr_outtab6.
        IF sy-tabix NE ls_mod_cell-row_id.
          IF gr_outtab6-ntd IS NOT INITIAL.
            CLEAR gr_outtab6-ntd.
            MODIFY gt_outtab6 FROM gr_outtab6.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
      READ TABLE gt_outtab6 INTO gr_outtab6 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
      IF gr_outtab6-med_prac IS NOT INITIAL.
        zim_in_mast-ntd = gr_outtab6-med_prac.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_med
           WHERE med_prac = gr_outtab6-med_prac.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          w_ntd_name      = zim_med-name.
          w_ntd_phone     = zim_med-phone.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR : zim_in_mast-ntd, w_ntd_name, w_ntd_phone.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT ir_data_changed->mt_mod_cells
          INTO ls_mod_cell
          WHERE fieldname = 'NTS'.
    CALL METHOD ir_data_changed->get_cell_value
      EXPORTING
        i_row_id    = ls_mod_cell-row_id
        i_fieldname = ls_mod_cell-fieldname
      IMPORTING
        e_value     = lv_value.

    IF lv_value = abap_true.
      LOOP AT gt_outtab6 INTO gr_outtab6.
        IF sy-tabix NE ls_mod_cell-row_id.
          IF gr_outtab6-nts IS NOT INITIAL.
            CLEAR gr_outtab6-nts.
            MODIFY gt_outtab6 FROM gr_outtab6.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
      READ TABLE gt_outtab6 INTO gr_outtab6 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
      IF gr_outtab6-med_prac IS NOT INITIAL.
        zim_in_mast-nts = gr_outtab6-med_prac.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_med
           WHERE med_prac = gr_outtab6-med_prac.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          w_nts_name      = zim_med-name.
          w_nts_phone     = zim_med-phone.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR : zim_in_mast-nts, w_nts_name, w_nts_phone.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


* If there is less than 2 blank lines, add more
  DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab6 LINES w_lines.
  CLEAR w_blank_lines.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab6 INTO gr_outtab6
       WHERE med_prac IS INITIAL.
    ADD 1 TO w_blank_lines.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF w_blank_lines LT 3.
    WHILE w_blank_lines LT 3.
      CLEAR gr_outtab6.
      gr_outtab6-ins_icon = icon_create.
      gr_outtab6-upd_icon = icon_change.
      APPEND gr_outtab6 TO gt_outtab6.
      ADD 1 TO w_blank_lines.
    ENDWHILE.
  ENDIF.

* When this event is called the screen display does not get updated.
* This code forces the PAI process to be executed and subsequently
* the screen fields get updated.
  w_ucomm = 'NEXT'.
  CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>set_new_ok_code
    EXPORTING
      new_code = w_ucomm.


ENDFORM.                    " HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_2105

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_3102
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_GT_OUTTAB1  text
*      -->P_ER_DATA_CHANGED  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM handle_data_changed_3102  USING ir_data_changed
                         TYPE REF TO cl_alv_changed_data_protocol.

  DATA: ls_mod_cell       TYPE lvc_s_modi,
         lv_value          TYPE lvc_value,
         lv_ucomm          TYPE sy-ucomm,
         l_code(2)         TYPE n,
         l_amount          TYPE zotm_meal_rate-meal_rate,
         l_field(20)       TYPE c,
         l_date            TYPE d.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS : <fs>.

  LOOP AT ir_data_changed->mt_mod_cells
          INTO ls_mod_cell
          WHERE fieldname = 'STATUS'.
    CALL METHOD ir_data_changed->get_cell_value
      EXPORTING
        i_row_id    = ls_mod_cell-row_id
        i_fieldname = ls_mod_cell-fieldname
      IMPORTING
        e_value     = lv_value.

    READ TABLE gt_outtab8 INTO gr_outtab8 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
    gr_outtab8-status = lv_value.
    w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab8-status.
    IF w_request = 'R'.
      w_domname = 'ZIM_ROSA_STATUS'.
    ELSE.
      w_domname = 'ZIM_PMO_STATUS'.
    ENDIF.
    CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
      EXPORTING
        domname  = w_domname
        value    = w_domvalue_l
        langu    = 'E'
      IMPORTING
        dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
        rc       = w_rc
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS   = 1.
    IF  w_rc = 0.
      gr_outtab8-status_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    ENDIF.
    IF w_request = 'R'.
      IF gr_outtab8-status IS NOT INITIAL AND
         gr_outtab8-status NE 'P'         AND        " Pass
         gr_outtab8-review_date IS INITIAL.
        gr_outtab8-review_date = sy-datum + 28.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      IF gr_outtab8-status IS NOT INITIAL AND
         gr_outtab8-status NE 'O'         AND        " Operational
         gr_outtab8-review_date IS INITIAL.
        gr_outtab8-review_date = sy-datum + 91.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab8 FROM gr_outtab8 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
  ENDLOOP.

* When this event is called the screen display does not get updated.
* This code forces the PAI process to be executed and subsequently
* the screen fields get updated.
  w_ucomm = 'NEXT'.
  CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>set_new_ok_code
    EXPORTING
      new_code = w_ucomm.


ENDFORM.                    " HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_3102

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_3707
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_GT_OUTTAB1  text
*      -->P_ER_DATA_CHANGED  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM handle_data_changed_3707  USING ir_data_changed
                         TYPE REF TO cl_alv_changed_data_protocol.

  DATA: ls_mod_cell       TYPE lvc_s_modi,
         lv_value          TYPE lvc_value,
         lv_ucomm          TYPE sy-ucomm,
         l_code(2)         TYPE n,
         l_amount          TYPE zotm_meal_rate-meal_rate,
         l_field(20)       TYPE c,
         l_date            TYPE d.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS : <fs>.

  LOOP AT ir_data_changed->mt_mod_cells
          INTO ls_mod_cell
          WHERE fieldname = 'STATUS'.
    CALL METHOD ir_data_changed->get_cell_value
      EXPORTING
        i_row_id    = ls_mod_cell-row_id
        i_fieldname = ls_mod_cell-fieldname
      IMPORTING
        e_value     = lv_value.

    READ TABLE gt_outtab19 INTO gr_outtab19 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
    gr_outtab19-status = lv_value.
    PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_STATUS'
                                   gr_outtab19-status
                          CHANGING gr_outtab19-status_desc.

    MODIFY gt_outtab19 FROM gr_outtab19 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT ir_data_changed->mt_mod_cells
          INTO ls_mod_cell
          WHERE fieldname = 'MC_DATE'.
    CALL METHOD ir_data_changed->get_cell_value
      EXPORTING
        i_row_id    = ls_mod_cell-row_id
        i_fieldname = ls_mod_cell-fieldname
      IMPORTING
        e_value     = lv_value.

    READ TABLE gt_outtab19 INTO gr_outtab19 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
    gr_outtab19-mc_date = lv_value.
    IF gr_outtab19-mc_date IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR : gr_outtab19-date_from, gr_outtab19-date_to,
              gr_outtab19-status, gr_outtab19-status_desc.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab19-mc_date GT sy-datum.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Medical Certificate date cannot'
                        'be in the future'.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab19 FROM gr_outtab19 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
  ENDLOOP.

* When this event is called the screen display does not get updated.
* This code forces the PAI process to be executed and subsequently
* the screen fields get updated.
  w_ucomm = 'NEXT'.
  CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>set_new_ok_code
    EXPORTING
      new_code = w_ucomm.


ENDFORM.                    " HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_3707

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_5000
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_GT_OUTTAB1  text
*      -->P_ER_DATA_CHANGED  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM handle_data_changed_5000  USING ir_data_changed
                         TYPE REF TO cl_alv_changed_data_protocol.

  DATA: ls_mod_cell       TYPE lvc_s_modi,
         lv_value          TYPE lvc_value,
         lv_ucomm          TYPE sy-ucomm,
         l_code(2)         TYPE n,
         l_amount          TYPE zotm_meal_rate-meal_rate,
         l_field(20)       TYPE c,
         l_date            TYPE d.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS : <fs>.

  LOOP AT ir_data_changed->mt_mod_cells
          INTO ls_mod_cell
          WHERE fieldname = 'INSPECT_EMP'.
    CALL METHOD ir_data_changed->get_cell_value
      EXPORTING
        i_row_id    = ls_mod_cell-row_id
        i_fieldname = ls_mod_cell-fieldname
      IMPORTING
        e_value     = lv_value.

    READ TABLE gt_outtab10 INTO gr_outtab10 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
    IF lv_value = '00000000'.
      CLEAR gr_outtab10-inspect_ref.
    ELSEIF lv_value NE gr_outtab10-inspect_emp.
      gr_outtab10-inspect_ref = sy-datum.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab10-inspect_emp = lv_value.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab10-inspect_emp
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab10-ename = pa0001-ename.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR gr_outtab10-ename.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab10 FROM gr_outtab10 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
  ENDLOOP.


* When this event is called the screen display does not get updated.
* This code forces the PAI process to be executed and subsequently
* the screen fields get updated.
  w_ucomm = 'NEXT'.
  CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>set_new_ok_code
    EXPORTING
      new_code = w_ucomm.


ENDFORM.                    " HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_5000

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_5100
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_GT_OUTTAB1  text
*      -->P_ER_DATA_CHANGED  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM handle_data_changed_5100  USING ir_data_changed
                         TYPE REF TO cl_alv_changed_data_protocol.

  DATA: ls_mod_cell       TYPE lvc_s_modi,
         lv_value          TYPE lvc_value,
         lv_ucomm          TYPE sy-ucomm,
         l_code(2)         TYPE n,
         l_amount          TYPE zotm_meal_rate-meal_rate,
         l_field(20)       TYPE c,
         l_date            TYPE d.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS : <fs>.

  LOOP AT ir_data_changed->mt_mod_cells
          INTO ls_mod_cell
          WHERE fieldname = 'ASS_EMP'.
    CALL METHOD ir_data_changed->get_cell_value
      EXPORTING
        i_row_id    = ls_mod_cell-row_id
        i_fieldname = ls_mod_cell-fieldname
      IMPORTING
        e_value     = lv_value.

    READ TABLE gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
    IF lv_value = '00000000'.
      CLEAR gr_outtab17-ass_emp.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab17-ass_emp = lv_value.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab17-ass_emp
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab17-ass_emp_name = pa0001-ename.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR gr_outtab17-ass_emp_name.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab17 FROM gr_outtab17 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
  ENDLOOP.


* When this event is called the screen display does not get updated.
* This code forces the PAI process to be executed and subsequently
* the screen fields get updated.
  w_ucomm = 'NEXT'.
  CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>set_new_ok_code
    EXPORTING
      new_code = w_ucomm.


ENDFORM.                    " HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_5100

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_9600
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_ER_DATA_CHANGED  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM handle_data_changed_9600 USING ir_data_changed
                         TYPE REF TO cl_alv_changed_data_protocol.

  DATA: ls_mod_cell       TYPE lvc_s_modi,
         lv_value          TYPE lvc_value,
         lv_ucomm          TYPE sy-ucomm,
         l_code(2)         TYPE n,
         l_amount          TYPE zotm_meal_rate-meal_rate,
         l_date            TYPE d,
         l_subrc           TYPE sy-subrc.


  LOOP AT ir_data_changed->mt_mod_cells
          INTO ls_mod_cell
          WHERE fieldname = 'CORR_CODE'.
    CALL METHOD ir_data_changed->get_cell_value
      EXPORTING
        i_row_id    = ls_mod_cell-row_id
        i_fieldname = ls_mod_cell-fieldname
      IMPORTING
        e_value     = lv_value.

    READ TABLE gt_outtab18 INTO gr_outtab18 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
    gr_outtab18-corr_code = lv_value.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_ltr_deft
           WHERE sprsl = 'E'
             AND corr_code = gr_outtab18-corr_code.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab18-corr_desc = zim_ltr_deft-corr_desc.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR gr_outtab18-corr_desc.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab18 FROM gr_outtab18 INDEX ls_mod_cell-row_id.
  ENDLOOP.



* If there is less than 2 blank lines, add more
  DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab18 LINES w_lines.
  CLEAR w_blank_lines.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab18 INTO gr_outtab18
       WHERE corr_code IS INITIAL.
    ADD 1 TO w_blank_lines.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF w_blank_lines LT 2.
    WHILE w_blank_lines LT 2.
      CLEAR gr_outtab18.
      gr_outtab18-file_icon = icon_linked_document.
      gr_outtab18-html_icon = icon_linked_document.
      gr_outtab18-del_icon  = icon_delete.
      APPEND gr_outtab18 TO gt_outtab18.
      ADD 1 TO w_blank_lines.
    ENDWHILE.
  ENDIF.


  PERFORM refresh_doc_sel_alv_total.

* When this event is called the screen display does not get updated.
* This code forces the PAI process to be executed and subsequently
* the screen fields get updated.
  w_ucomm = 'NEXT'.
  CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>set_new_ok_code
    EXPORTING
      new_code = w_ucomm.

ENDFORM.                    " HANDLE_DATA_CHANGED_9600

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  DISPLAY_DOCUMENT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_GR_OUTTAB12_DOC_LINK  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM display_document  USING    p_doc.

  DATA: l_result             TYPE abap_bool,
         l_doc                TYPE string.

  l_doc = p_doc.

  CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>file_exist
    EXPORTING
      file                 = l_doc
    RECEIVING
      result               = l_result
    EXCEPTIONS
      cntl_error           = 1
      error_no_gui         = 2
      wrong_parameter      = 3
      not_supported_by_gui = 4
      OTHERS               = 5.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
  ENDIF.

  IF l_result IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Invalid filename'.
  ELSE.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>execute
      EXPORTING
        document               = l_doc
        synchronous            = 'X'
        operation              = 'OPEN'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error             = 1
        error_no_gui           = 2
        bad_parameter          = 3
        file_not_found         = 4
        path_not_found         = 5
        file_extension_unknown = 6
        error_execute_failed   = 7
        synchronous_failed     = 8
        not_supported_by_gui   = 9
        OTHERS                 = 10.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0 AND
       sy-subrc <> 8.
      MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Error displaying attachment'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " DISPLAY_DOCUMENT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FILENAME_VALUE_REQUEST
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_GT_OUTTAB18  text
*      -->P_ES_COL_ID  text
*      -->P_ES_ROW_NO  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM filename_value_request TABLES t_outtab STRUCTURE gr_outtab18
                            USING  p_col_id
                                   p_row_no STRUCTURE lvc_s_roid.

  DATA: w_file_table      TYPE filetable,
         w_rc              TYPE i,
         w_user_action     TYPE i,
         l_file            TYPE string.

  REFRESH w_file_table.
  CLEAR : w_file_table, l_file.

  IF p_col_id = 'ADD_ICON'.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>file_open_dialog
*  EXPORTING
*    WINDOW_TITLE            = WINDOW_TITLE
*    DEFAULT_EXTENSION       = DEFAULT_EXTENSION
*    DEFAULT_FILENAME        = DEFAULT_FILENAME
*    FILE_FILTER             = FILE_FILTER
*    WITH_ENCODING           = WITH_ENCODING
*    INITIAL_DIRECTORY       = INITIAL_DIRECTORY
*    MULTISELECTION          = MULTISELECTION
      CHANGING
        file_table              = w_file_table
        rc                      = w_rc
        user_action             = w_user_action
*    FILE_ENCODING           = FILE_ENCODING
    EXCEPTIONS
      file_open_dialog_failed = 1
      cntl_error              = 2
      error_no_gui            = 3
      not_supported_by_gui    = 4
      OTHERS                  = 5
            .
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
*            WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
    ENDIF.

    LOOP AT w_file_table INTO l_file.
      EXIT.
    ENDLOOP.

    IF l_file IS NOT INITIAL.
      READ TABLE t_outtab INTO gr_outtab18 INDEX p_row_no-row_id.
      gr_outtab18-doc_link = l_file.
      MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab18 INDEX p_row_no-row_id.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR gr_outtab18.
    gr_outtab18-file_icon = icon_linked_document.
    gr_outtab18-html_icon = icon_linked_document.
    gr_outtab18-del_icon  = icon_delete.
    MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab18 INDEX p_row_no-row_id.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " FILENAME_VALUE_REQUEST

    IMPORTING
      es_col_info = gr_col_info2
      es_row_no   = gr_row_no2.
  CALL METHOD inc_inj_alv->get_current_cell
    IMPORTING
      es_col_id = gr_col_info
      es_row_no = gr_row_no.

  CALL METHOD inc_inj_alv->get_frontend_layout
    IMPORTING
      es_layout = inc_inj_layout.
  inc_inj_layout-cwidth_opt = ' '.
  CALL METHOD inc_inj_alv->set_frontend_layout
    EXPORTING
      is_layout = inc_inj_layout.

  CALL METHOD inc_inj_alv->refresh_table_display.

  CALL METHOD inc_inj_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
    EXPORTING
      is_col_info = gr_col_info2
      is_row_no   = gr_row_no2.
  CALL METHOD inc_inj_alv->set_current_cell_via_id
    EXPORTING
      is_column_id = gr_col_info
      is_row_no    = gr_row_no.



ENDFORM.                    " REFRESH_INC_INJ_ALV_TOTAL

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  REFRESH_DOC_SEL_ALV_TOTAL
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM refresh_doc_sel_alv_total .

* When the amounts are refreshed, the total is not automatically
* refreshed.  The total only gets refreshed when the Refresh Table
* Display method is called.  This method then sets the cursor on the
* first line.  Therefore the Get and Set Current Cell methods are
* called to keep the cursor at the same position.
* Scrolling information also needs to be retrieved to ensure the
* first displayed column doesn't change.
  CALL METHOD doc_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
    IMPORTING
      es_col_info = gr_col_info2
      es_row_no   = gr_row_no2.
  CALL METHOD doc_sel_alv->get_current_cell
    IMPORTING
      es_col_id = gr_col_info
      es_row_no = gr_row_no.

  CALL METHOD doc_sel_alv->refresh_table_display.

  CALL METHOD doc_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
    EXPORTING
      is_col_info = gr_col_info2
      is_row_no   = gr_row_no2.
  CALL METHOD doc_sel_alv->set_current_cell_via_id
    EXPORTING
      is_column_id = gr_col_info
      is_row_no    = gr_row_no.



ENDFORM.                    " REFRESH_DOC_SEL_ALV_TOTAL

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  REFRESH_RED_PAY_ALV_TOTAL
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM refresh_red_pay_alv_total .

  CALL METHOD red_pay_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
    IMPORTING
      es_col_info = gr_col_info2
      es_row_no   = gr_row_no2.
  CALL METHOD red_pay_alv->get_current_cell
    IMPORTING
      es_col_id = gr_col_info
      es_row_no = gr_row_no.

  CALL METHOD red_pay_alv->get_frontend_layout
    IMPORTING
      es_layout = red_pay_layout.
  red_pay_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
  CALL METHOD red_pay_alv->set_frontend_layout
    EXPORTING
      is_layout = red_pay_layout.

  CALL METHOD red_pay_alv->refresh_table_display.

  CALL METHOD red_pay_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
    EXPORTING
      is_col_info = gr_col_info2
      is_row_no   = gr_row_no2.
  CALL METHOD red_pay_alv->set_current_cell_via_id
    EXPORTING
      is_column_id = gr_col_info
      is_row_no    = gr_row_no.



ENDFORM.                    " REFRESH_RED_PAY_ALV_TOTAL

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  REFRESH_MC_SEL_ALV_TOTAL
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM refresh_mc_sel_alv_total .

  CALL METHOD mc_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
    IMPORTING
      es_col_info = gr_col_info2
      es_row_no   = gr_row_no2.
  CALL METHOD mc_sel_alv->get_current_cell
    IMPORTING
      es_col_id = gr_col_info
      es_row_no = gr_row_no.

  CALL METHOD mc_sel_alv->get_frontend_layout
    IMPORTING
      es_layout = mc_sel_layout.
  mc_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
  CALL METHOD mc_sel_alv->set_frontend_layout
    EXPORTING
      is_layout = mc_sel_layout.

  CALL METHOD mc_sel_alv->refresh_table_display.

  CALL METHOD mc_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
    EXPORTING
      is_col_info = gr_col_info2
      is_row_no   = gr_row_no2.
  CALL METHOD mc_sel_alv->set_current_cell_via_id
    EXPORTING
      is_column_id = gr_col_info
      is_row_no    = gr_row_no.



ENDFORM.                    " REFRESH_MC_SEL_ALV_TOTAL

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_INC_INJ
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--P_W_ERROR  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_inc_inj  CHANGING p_error.

  DATA: l_protocol        TYPE lvc_t_msg1,
         l_count           TYPE i,
         l_frac            TYPE p DECIMALS 2,
         l_error(1)        TYPE c,
         l_date            TYPE d,
         l_message1(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message2(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message3(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message4(50)    TYPE c,
         l_pos_off_emp     TYPE zpos_mast-pos_off_emp.

  CLEAR : p_error, l_count.
  REFRESH tbl_bapiret.

*  DESCRIBE TABLE gt_table_changed->mt_protocol LINES w_lines.
*
*  IF w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
*    EXIT.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF w_lines NE 0.
*    p_error = abap_true.
*    MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Errors still exist in the table'.
*    EXIT.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  LOOP AT gt_outtab1 INTO gr_outtab1.
** clear all existing colours
*    IF NOT gr_outtab1-row_colour IS INITIAL.
*      CLEAR gr_outtab1-row_colour.
*      MODIFY gt_outtab1 FROM gr_outtab1.
*    ENDIF.
*    CLEAR l_error.
*
*    IF gr_outtab1-desc IS INITIAL AND
*       gr_outtab1-main_inj IS NOT INITIAL.
*      PERFORM add_message USING 'Please enter the Location of'
*                                'the Injury'
*                                sy-tabix
*                                'E'.
*      gr_outtab1-row_colour = 'C600'.
*      MODIFY gt_outtab1 FROM gr_outtab1.
*    ENDIF.
*
*  ENDLOOP.
*
  DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab1 LINES w_lines.
  IF w_lines = 0.
    PERFORM add_message USING 'At least one Injury'
                              'must be entered'
                              0
                              'E'.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab1 INTO gr_outtab1
       WHERE main_injury IS NOT INITIAL.
    EXIT.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    PERFORM add_message USING 'Please nominate the Injury'
                              'that is the Main Injury'
                              0
                              'E'.
  ENDIF.

  DESCRIBE TABLE tbl_bapiret LINES w_lines.
  IF w_lines GT 0.
    p_error = abap_true.
    CALL FUNCTION 'Z_MESSAGE_SHOW'
      EXPORTING
        i_title = 'OLIMS - Create an Incident'
      TABLES
        msgtbl  = tbl_bapiret.

  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_INC_INJ

*       text

*      <--P_W_ERROR  text

FORM validate_inc_nat  CHANGING p_error.

*  DATA: l_protocol        TYPE lvc_t_msg1,
*         l_count           TYPE i,
*         l_frac            TYPE p DECIMALS 2,
*         l_error(1)        TYPE c,
*         l_date            TYPE d,

*  REFRESH tbl_bapiret.

*  IF w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
*    EXIT.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF w_lines NE 0.
*    p_error = abap_true.
*    MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Errors still exist in the table'.

*       WHERE desc IS NOT INITIAL.

*  ENDLOOP.
*  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
*    PERFORM add_message USING 'At least one Nature of Injury'
*                              'must be entered'
*                              0
*                              'E'.
**   gr_outtab1-row_colour = 'C600'.
**   modify gt_outtab1 from gr_outtab1.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  LOOP AT gt_outtab2 INTO gr_outtab2
*       WHERE main_nat IS NOT INITIAL.
*    EXIT.

*  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
*    PERFORM add_message USING 'Please nominate the Nature'

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_MED_PRAC
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--P_W_ERROR  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_med_prac  CHANGING p_error.

  DATA: l_protocol        TYPE lvc_t_msg1,
         l_count           TYPE i,
         l_frac            TYPE p DECIMALS 2,
         l_error(1)        TYPE c,
         l_date            TYPE d,
         l_med_prac        TYPE zim_med-med_prac,
         l_message1(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message2(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message3(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message4(50)    TYPE c,
         l_ntd(1)          TYPE c.

  CLEAR : p_error, l_count.
  REFRESH tbl_bapiret.

  DESCRIBE TABLE gt_table_changed->mt_protocol LINES w_lines.

  IF w_lines NE 0.
    p_error = abap_true.
    MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Errors still exist in the table'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab6 INTO gr_outtab6.
* clear all existing colours
    IF NOT gr_outtab6-row_colour IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR gr_outtab6-row_colour.
      MODIFY gt_outtab6 FROM gr_outtab6.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR l_error.

    IF gr_outtab6-med_prac IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_med
             WHERE med_prac = gr_outtab6-med_prac.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        PERFORM add_message USING 'Invalid Health Professional'
                                  ' '
                                  sy-tabix
                                  'E'.
        gr_outtab6-row_colour = 'C600'.
        MODIFY gt_outtab6 FROM gr_outtab6.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF gr_outtab6-med_prac IS INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab6-ntd      IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM add_message USING 'A Health Professional must be'
                                'entered for the NTD'
                                sy-tabix
                                'E'.
      gr_outtab6-row_colour = 'C600'.
      MODIFY gt_outtab6 FROM gr_outtab6.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR l_ntd.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab6 INTO gr_outtab6.
    IF gr_outtab6-ntd = abap_true.
      l_ntd = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF l_ntd IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR : zim_in_mast-ntd, w_ntd_name.
  ENDIF.

  gt_outtab6_copy[] = gt_outtab6[].
  SORT gt_outtab6_copy BY med_prac.
  CLEAR l_med_prac.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab6_copy INTO gr_outtab6
       WHERE med_prac IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF gr_outtab6-med_prac = l_med_prac.
      l_message1 = l_med_prac.
      SHIFT l_message1 LEFT DELETING LEADING '0'.
      CONCATENATE 'The Health Professional' l_message1 INTO
                  l_message1 SEPARATED BY ' '.
      PERFORM add_message USING l_message1
                                'occurs more than once'
                                ' '
                                'E'.
    ENDIF.
    l_med_prac = gr_outtab6-med_prac.
  ENDLOOP.

  DESCRIBE TABLE tbl_bapiret LINES w_lines.
  IF w_lines GT 0.
    p_error = abap_true.
    CALL FUNCTION 'Z_MESSAGE_SHOW'
      EXPORTING
        i_title = 'OLIMS - Health Professionals'
      TABLES
        msgtbl  = tbl_bapiret.

  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_MED_PRAC

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_ERG_ASS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--P_W_ERROR  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_erg_ass  CHANGING p_error.

  DATA: l_protocol        TYPE lvc_t_msg1,
         l_count           TYPE i,
         l_frac            TYPE p DECIMALS 2,
         l_error(1)        TYPE c,
         l_date            TYPE d,
         l_message1(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message2(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message3(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message4(50)    TYPE c,
         l_pos_off_emp     TYPE zpos_mast-pos_off_emp.

  CLEAR : p_error, l_count.
  REFRESH tbl_bapiret.

  DESCRIBE TABLE gt_table_changed->mt_protocol LINES w_lines.

  IF w_lines NE 0.
    p_error = abap_true.
    MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Errors still exist in the table'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab10 INTO gr_outtab10.
* clear all existing colours
    IF NOT gr_outtab10-row_colour IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR gr_outtab10-row_colour.
      MODIFY gt_outtab10 FROM gr_outtab10.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR l_error.

    IF gr_outtab10-inspect_emp IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
             WHERE ima = gr_outtab10-inspect_emp.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        PERFORM add_message USING 'Invalid Inspector'
                                  ' '
                                  sy-tabix
                                  'E'.
        gr_outtab10-row_colour = 'C600'.
        MODIFY gt_outtab10 FROM gr_outtab10.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF gr_outtab10-inspect_comp IS NOT INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab10-inspect_emp IS INITIAL.
      PERFORM add_message USING 'The officer that is to perform'
                                'the inspection must be entered'
                                sy-tabix
                                'E'.
      gr_outtab10-row_colour = 'C600'.
      MODIFY gt_outtab10 FROM gr_outtab10.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.


  DESCRIBE TABLE tbl_bapiret LINES w_lines.
  IF w_lines GT 0.
    p_error = abap_true.
    CALL FUNCTION 'Z_MESSAGE_SHOW'
      EXPORTING
        i_title = 'OLIMS - Ergonomic Inspections'
      TABLES
        msgtbl  = tbl_bapiret.

  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_ERG_ASS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_DOC_LINK
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_GT_OUTTAB9  text
*      <--P_W_ERROR  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_doc_link TABLES   t_outtab STRUCTURE gr_outtab18
                       CHANGING p_error.

  DATA: l_protocol        TYPE lvc_t_msg1,
         l_count           TYPE i,
         l_frac            TYPE p DECIMALS 2,
         l_error(1)        TYPE c,
         l_date            TYPE d,
         l_datec(10)       TYPE c,
         l_timec(5)        TYPE c,
         l_message1(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message2(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message3(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message4(50)    TYPE c.

  CLEAR : p_error, l_count.
  REFRESH tbl_bapiret.

  DESCRIBE TABLE gt_table_changed->mt_protocol LINES w_lines.

  IF w_lines NE 0.
    p_error = abap_true.
    MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Errors still exist in the table'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT t_outtab INTO gr_outtab18.
* clear all existing colours
    IF NOT gr_outtab18-row_colour IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR gr_outtab18-row_colour.
      MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab18.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR l_error.

    IF gr_outtab18-corr_code  IS INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab18-doc_title IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab18-row_colour = 'C600'.
      PERFORM add_message USING 'Please enter a Correspondence'
                                'Code for the document.'
                                sy-tabix
                                'E'.
      MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab18.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

    IF gr_outtab18-corr_code  IS NOT INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab18-doc_title IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab18-row_colour = 'C600'.
      PERFORM add_message USING 'Please enter a description for'
                                'the document.'
                                sy-tabix
                                'E'.
      MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab18.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

    IF gr_outtab18-corr_code  IS NOT INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab18-doc_link IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab18-row_colour = 'C600'.
      PERFORM add_message USING 'Please enter a document link.'
                                ' '
                                sy-tabix
                                'E'.
      MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab18.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.


  DESCRIBE TABLE tbl_bapiret LINES w_lines.
  IF w_lines GT 0.
    p_error = abap_true.
    CALL FUNCTION 'Z_MESSAGE_SHOW'
      EXPORTING
        i_title = 'OLIMS - Letter Processing'
      TABLES
        msgtbl  = tbl_bapiret.

  ENDIF.



ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_DOC_LINK

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_GAO_MED_CERT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--P_W_ERROR  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_gao_med_cert TABLES t_outtab STRUCTURE gr_outtab19
                       CHANGING p_error.

  DATA: l_protocol        TYPE lvc_t_msg1,
         l_count           TYPE i,
         l_frac            TYPE p DECIMALS 2,
         l_error(1)        TYPE c,
         l_date            TYPE d,
         l_days            TYPE i,
         l_message1(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message2(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message3(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message4(50)    TYPE c,
         l_message(132)    TYPE c.

  CLEAR : p_error, l_count.
  REFRESH tbl_bapiret.

  DESCRIBE TABLE gt_table_changed->mt_protocol LINES w_lines.

  IF w_lines NE 0.
    p_error = abap_true.
    MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Errors still exist in the table'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT t_outtab INTO gr_outtab19.
* clear all existing colours
    IF NOT gr_outtab19-row_colour IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR gr_outtab19-row_colour.
      MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab19.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR l_error.

    IF gr_outtab19-mc_date IS NOT INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab19-category = 'G'.
      IF gr_outtab19-mc_date LT zim_incident-dateincident.
        PERFORM add_message USING 'The Medical Certificate Date cannot'
                                  'be before the incident date'
                                  sy-tabix
                                  'E'.
        gr_outtab19-row_colour = 'C600'.
        MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab19.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      IF gr_outtab19-mc_date GT sy-datum.
        PERFORM add_message USING 'The Medical Certificate date'
                                  'cannot be in the future'
                                  sy-tabix
                                  'E'.
        gr_outtab19-row_colour = 'C600'.
        MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab19.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      IF gr_outtab19-status IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM add_message USING 'The Medical Certificate Status'
                                  'must be entered'
                                  sy-tabix
                                  'E'.
        gr_outtab19-row_colour = 'C600'.
        MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab19.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      IF gr_outtab19-date_from IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM add_message USING 'Enter the Date From'
                                  ' '
                                  sy-tabix
                                  'E'.
        gr_outtab19-row_colour = 'C600'.
        MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab19.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      IF gr_outtab19-date_from LT zim_incident-dateincident.
        PERFORM add_message USING 'The From Date cannot'
                                  'be before the incident date'
                                  sy-tabix
                                  'E'.
        gr_outtab19-row_colour = 'C600'.
        MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab19.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      IF gr_outtab19-status = 'U' OR
         gr_outtab19-status = 'SD'.
        IF gr_outtab19-date_to IS INITIAL.
          PERFORM add_message USING 'Enter the Date To'
                                    ' '
                                    sy-tabix
                                    'E'.
          gr_outtab19-row_colour = 'C600'.
          MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab19.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.

        IF gr_outtab19-date_from GT
           gr_outtab19-date_to.
          PERFORM add_message USING 'The Date From must be less'
                                    'than the Date To'
                                    sy-tabix
                                    'E'.
          gr_outtab19-row_colour = 'C600'.
          MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab19.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.

        l_days = gr_outtab19-date_to - gr_outtab19-date_from.
        IF l_days GT 92.
          PERFORM add_message USING 'The Certificate cannot be'
                                    'for more than 3 months'
                                    sy-tabix
                                    'E'.
          gr_outtab19-row_colour = 'C600'.
          MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab19.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR gr_outtab19-date_to.
        MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab19.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

    IF gr_outtab19-mc_date IS INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab19-mc_sent IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM add_message USING
                           'The Medical Certificate cannot be deleted'
                           'after a document has been imported and sent'
                                 sy-tabix
                                 'E'.
      gr_outtab19-row_colour = 'C600'.
      MODIFY t_outtab FROM gr_outtab19.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.


  DESCRIBE TABLE tbl_bapiret LINES w_lines.
  IF w_lines GT 0.
    p_error = abap_true.
    IF mc_sel_alv IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM refresh_mc_sel_alv_total.
    ENDIF.
    CALL FUNCTION 'Z_MESSAGE_SHOW'
      EXPORTING
        i_title = 'WC - Medical Certificate'
      TABLES
        msgtbl  = tbl_bapiret.

  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_GAO_MED_CERT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ADD_MESSAGE
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_7263   text
*      -->P_7264   text
*      -->P_7265   text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM add_message  USING    p_mess1
                           p_mess2
                           p_index
                           p_code.

  DATA:  l_char(5)         TYPE c.

  CLEAR tbl_bapiret.
  tbl_bapiret-type = p_code.
  tbl_bapiret-id   = 'ZZ'.
  tbl_bapiret-number = '999'.
  tbl_bapiret-message_v1 = p_mess1.
  tbl_bapiret-message_v2 = p_mess2.
  IF p_index IS NOT INITIAL.
    l_char = p_index.
    SHIFT l_char LEFT DELETING LEADING '0'.
    tbl_bapiret-message_v3 = '- Line'.
    tbl_bapiret-message_v4 = l_char.
  ENDIF.
  APPEND tbl_bapiret.


ENDFORM.                    " ADD_MESSAGE

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_ADD_FILE
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_add_file .

  REFRESH gt_outtab18.
  SELECT * FROM zim_add_file
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab18.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab18 INTO gr_outtab18.
    gr_outtab18-file_icon = icon_linked_document.
    gr_outtab18-html_icon = icon_linked_document.
    gr_outtab18-del_icon  = icon_delete.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_ltr_deft
           WHERE sprsl = 'E'
             AND corr_code = gr_outtab18-corr_code.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab18-corr_desc = zim_ltr_deft-corr_desc.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab18 FROM gr_outtab18.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR gr_outtab18.
  gr_outtab18-file_icon = icon_linked_document.
  gr_outtab18-html_icon = icon_linked_document.
  gr_outtab18-del_icon  = icon_delete.
  APPEND gr_outtab18 TO gt_outtab18.
  APPEND gr_outtab18 TO gt_outtab18.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_ADD_FILE
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PROCESS_DOC_LINK
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_GT_OUTTAB12  text
*      <--P_W_ERROR  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM process_doc_link  TABLES   t_outtab STRUCTURE gr_outtab18
                       CHANGING p_error.

  DATA: l_seq(3)       TYPE n.

  DELETE FROM zim_add_file.

  l_seq = 0.
  LOOP AT t_outtab INTO gr_outtab18
       WHERE corr_code IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR rec_zim_add_file.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING gr_outtab18 TO rec_zim_add_file.
    ADD 1 TO l_seq.
    rec_zim_add_file-seq_no = l_seq.
    rec_zim_add_file-mandt = sy-mandt.
    INSERT zim_add_file FROM rec_zim_add_file.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " PROCESS_DOC_LINK
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CREATE_INCIDENT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM create_incident .

  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <a> TYPE any.
  DATA: l_fieldname(30)         TYPE c,
        l_index(3)              TYPE n.

  DATA: BEGIN OF ty_ptext OCCURS 0,
         line(72),
         END OF ty_ptext.

  CLEAR w_error.

  IF w_1000_action NE 'UPD' AND
     w_1000_action NE 'UNM' AND
     w_1000_action NE 'HIS'.
    PERFORM generate_incident_no  CHANGING p9001-identnr.
    p9001-itxex         = abap_true.
    p9001-reportinguser = sy-uname.
    p9001-reportingdate = sy-datum.
    p9001-reportingtime = sy-uzeit.
    IF p9001-bp_number = 'BP1'.
      CONCATENATE 'BP' p9001-identnr INTO p9001-bp_number.
    ENDIF.
    PERFORM get_user_name  USING    sy-uname
                           CHANGING p9001-reportingperson.
  ENDIF.

  p9001-new_format = 'X'.

  PERFORM clear_p9001_numbers.

  PERFORM update_p9001_numbers.

  IF w_1000_action = 'UPD'.
    PERFORM update_incident.
  ELSEIF w_1000_action = 'UNM' OR
         w_1000_action = 'HIS'.
    PERFORM convert_nearmiss.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM create_incident_9001.
    IF w_1000_action = 'INS' AND
      p9001-nearmiss = '4'.          "Recurring Injury
      CLEAR w_popup_ucomm.
      w_pernr = p9001-pernr.
      CLEAR w_old_identnr.
      CALL SCREEN 1200 STARTING AT 5 6 ENDING AT 125 23.
      IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
        w_1000_action = 'COP'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF w_1000_action = 'COP'.
      PERFORM copy_im USING p9001-pernr
                            w_old_identnr
                            p9001-identnr.
      MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Incident Activities have been copied from'
                        w_old_identnr
                        'to'
                        p9001-identnr.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " CREATE_INCIDENT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM free_gen_txt .

  IF NOT gen_txt_container IS INITIAL.
    " destroy tree containers (detroys contained tree control, too)
    CALL METHOD gen_txt_container->free
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_system_error = 1
        cntl_error        = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_system_error = 1
        cntl_error        = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR gen_txt_container.
    CLEAR gen_txt_text.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " FREE_GEN_TXT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM free_sec_clm .

  IF NOT sec_clm_container IS INITIAL.
    " destroy tree containers (detroys contained tree control, too)
    CALL METHOD sec_clm_container->free
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_system_error = 1
        cntl_error        = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_system_error = 1
        cntl_error        = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR sec_clm_container.
    CLEAR sec_clm_alv.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " FREE_SEC_CLM
