*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RETRIEVE_INCIDENT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_P_PERNR  text
*      -->P_P_INCIDENT  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM retrieve_incident  USING    p_pernr
                                 p_incident.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <a> TYPE any.
  DATA: l_fieldname(30)    TYPE c,
        l_count(3)         TYPE n,
        l_main(1)          TYPE c,
        tb_inc_inj         TYPE zp902_inc_inj_odata_t,
        l_inc_inj          TYPE zp902_inc_inj_odata,
        tb_inc_p902_inj    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zim_inc_p902_inj,
        l_inc_p902_inj     TYPE zim_inc_p902_inj,
        tb_inc_email       TYPE zp902_email_odata_t,
        l_inc_email        TYPE zp902_email_odata.

  DATA: BEGIN OF ty_ptext OCCURS 0,
         line(72),
         END OF ty_ptext.


  REFRESH : gt_outtab1, gt_outtab2.
  CLEAR p9001.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
         WHERE pernr = p_pernr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0001.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF p9001
         WHERE pernr   = p_pernr
           AND identnr = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR p9001.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-bp_number IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF zim_inc_p902
           WHERE pernr   = p_pernr
             AND identnr = p_incident.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR zim_inc_p902.
    ENDIF.



  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-new_format IS INITIAL.
    l_count = 1.
    WHILE l_count LE c_act_count.
      CONCATENATE 'P9001-ACT_' l_count INTO l_fieldname.
      ASSIGN (l_fieldname) TO <a>.
      IF <a> = abap_true.
        p9001-activity_new = l_count.
      ENDIF.
      ADD 1 TO l_count.
    ENDWHILE.

    l_count = 1.
    WHILE l_count LE c_cas_count.
      CONCATENATE 'P9001-CAS_' l_count INTO l_fieldname.
      ASSIGN (l_fieldname) TO <a>.
      IF <a> = abap_true.
        p9001-cause_new = l_count.
      ENDIF.
      ADD 1 TO l_count.
    ENDWHILE.

    l_count = 1.
    WHILE l_count LE c_whe_count.
      CONCATENATE 'P9001-WHE_' l_count INTO l_fieldname.
      ASSIGN (l_fieldname) TO <a>.
      IF <a> = abap_true.
        p9001-where_new = l_count.
      ENDIF.
      ADD 1 TO l_count.
    ENDWHILE.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT * FROM zim_inc_inj
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE tb_inc_inj
         WHERE pernr   = p_pernr
           AND identnr = p_incident.
  DESCRIBE TABLE tb_inc_inj LINES w_lines.
  IF w_lines = 0.
    CALL METHOD zcl_p902_class=>retrieve_injuries
      EXPORTING
        i_p9001         = p9001
      IMPORTING
        tb_inc_inj_list = tb_inc_inj.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT tb_inc_inj INTO l_inc_inj.
    CLEAR gr_outtab1.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING l_inc_inj TO gr_outtab1.

    SELECT SINGLE location_desc FROM zp902_locationt
           INTO gr_outtab1-location_desc
           WHERE sprsl = 'E'
             AND location_code = gr_outtab1-location_code.

    SELECT SINGLE nature_desc FROM zp902_naturet
           INTO gr_outtab1-nature_desc
           WHERE sprsl = 'E'
             AND nature_code = gr_outtab1-nature_code.

    APPEND gr_outtab1 TO gt_outtab1.
  ENDLOOP.

  gt_outtab1_copy[] = gt_outtab1[].

  PERFORM get_notes_incident TABLES gt_outtxt1
                                    gt_outtxt1_old
                             USING  ' '.

* Retrieve the BP Injuries
  IF p9001-bp_number IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT * FROM zim_inc_p902_inj
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE tb_inc_p902_inj
           WHERE pernr     = p_pernr
             AND bp_number = p9001-bp_number
             AND incident  = p_incident.

    LOOP AT tb_inc_p902_inj INTO l_inc_p902_inj.
      CLEAR gr_outtab58.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING l_inc_p902_inj TO gr_outtab58.

      SELECT SINGLE body FROM zpbp_body_locatt
             INTO gr_outtab58-location_desc
             WHERE sprsl = 'E'
               AND sys_id = gr_outtab58-bodily_location_subtype.

      SELECT SINGLE injury FROM zpbp_injury_sutt
             INTO gr_outtab58-nature_desc
             WHERE sprsl = 'E'
               AND sys_id = gr_outtab58-injury_subtype.

      APPEND gr_outtab58 TO gt_outtab58.
    ENDLOOP.

    gt_outtab58_copy[] = gt_outtab58[].
  ENDIF.


* Determine whether the Incident is unsworn.
  CLEAR w_unsworn_claim.
  SELECT SINGLE unsworn_claim
    FROM zim_in_cm
    INTO w_unsworn_claim
    WHERE pernr    EQ p_pernr
      AND incident EQ p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    zcl_hr_utilities=>is_person_sworn_unsworn(
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr   = p_pernr
      IMPORTING
        e_unsworn = w_unsworn_claim ).
  ENDIF.

* Retrieve the email notifications
  CALL METHOD zcl_p902_class=>retrieve_email_sent_list
    EXPORTING
      i_pernr       = p_pernr
      i_incident_no = p_incident
    IMPORTING
      tb_email_list = tb_inc_email.

  REFRESH gt_outtab49.
  LOOP AT tb_inc_email INTO l_inc_email.
    CLEAR gr_outtab49.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING l_inc_email TO gr_outtab49.
    APPEND gr_outtab49 TO gt_outtab49.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF zim_incident-bp_number IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM get_bp_incident USING p_pernr
                                  p_incident.
  ENDIF.



ENDFORM.                    " RETRIEVE_INCIDENT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RETRIEVE_NEARMISS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_P_PERNR  text
*      -->P_P_INCIDENT  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM retrieve_nearmiss  USING    p_pernr
                                 p_incident.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <a> TYPE any.
  DATA: l_fieldname(30)    TYPE c,
        l_count(3)         TYPE n,
        l_main(1)          TYPE c,
        tb_inc_inj         TYPE zp902_inc_inj_odata_t,
        l_inc_inj          TYPE zp902_inc_inj_odata,
        tb_inc_p902_inj    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zim_inc_p902_inj,
        l_inc_p902_inj     TYPE zim_inc_p902_inj.

  DATA: BEGIN OF ty_ptext OCCURS 0,
         line(72),
         END OF ty_ptext.


  REFRESH : gt_outtab1, gt_outtab2, gt_outtab58.
  CLEAR p9001.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
         WHERE pernr = p_pernr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0001.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9001
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF p9001
         WHERE pernr   = p_pernr
           AND identnr = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR p9001.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-bp_number IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_inc_p902
           WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
             AND incident = p_incident.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR zim_inc_p902.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-new_format IS INITIAL.
    l_count = 1.
    WHILE l_count LE c_act_count.
      CONCATENATE 'P9001-ACT_' l_count INTO l_fieldname.
      ASSIGN (l_fieldname) TO <a>.
      IF <a> = abap_true.
        p9001-activity_new = l_count.
      ENDIF.
      ADD 1 TO l_count.
    ENDWHILE.

    l_count = 1.
    WHILE l_count LE c_cas_count.
      CONCATENATE 'P9001-CAS_' l_count INTO l_fieldname.
      ASSIGN (l_fieldname) TO <a>.
      IF <a> = abap_true.
        p9001-cause_new = l_count.
      ENDIF.
      ADD 1 TO l_count.
    ENDWHILE.

    l_count = 1.
    WHILE l_count LE c_whe_count.
      CONCATENATE 'P9001-WHE_' l_count INTO l_fieldname.
      ASSIGN (l_fieldname) TO <a>.
      IF <a> = abap_true.
        p9001-where_new = l_count.
      ENDIF.
      ADD 1 TO l_count.
    ENDWHILE.

    CALL METHOD zcl_p902_class=>retrieve_injuries
      EXPORTING
        i_p9001         = p9001
      IMPORTING
        tb_inc_inj_list = tb_inc_inj.

  ELSE.
    SELECT * FROM zp902_inc_inj
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE tb_inc_inj
           WHERE pernr   = p_pernr
             AND identnr = p_incident.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT tb_inc_inj INTO l_inc_inj.
    CLEAR gr_outtab1.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING l_inc_inj TO gr_outtab1.

    SELECT SINGLE location_desc FROM zp902_locationt
           INTO gr_outtab1-location_desc
           WHERE sprsl = 'E'
             AND location_code = gr_outtab1-location_code.

    SELECT SINGLE nature_desc FROM zp902_naturet
           INTO gr_outtab1-nature_desc
           WHERE sprsl = 'E'
             AND nature_code = gr_outtab1-nature_code.

    APPEND gr_outtab1 TO gt_outtab1.
  ENDLOOP.

  PERFORM get_notes_pa9001 TABLES gt_outtxt1
                           USING  ' '.

  REFRESH gt_outtxt1_old.

* Retrieve the BP Injuries
  IF p9001-bp_number IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT * FROM zim_inc_p902_inj
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE tb_inc_p902_inj
           WHERE pernr     = p_pernr
             AND bp_number = p9001-bp_number
             AND incident  = p_incident.

    LOOP AT tb_inc_p902_inj INTO l_inc_p902_inj.
      CLEAR gr_outtab58.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING l_inc_p902_inj TO gr_outtab58.

      SELECT SINGLE body FROM zpbp_body_locatt
             INTO gr_outtab58-location_desc
             WHERE sprsl = 'E'
               AND sys_id = gr_outtab58-bodily_location_subtype.

      SELECT SINGLE injury FROM zpbp_injury_sutt
             INTO gr_outtab58-nature_desc
             WHERE sprsl = 'E'
               AND sys_id = gr_outtab58-injury_subtype.

      APPEND gr_outtab58 TO gt_outtab58.
    ENDLOOP.

    gt_outtab58_copy[] = gt_outtab58[].
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " RETRIEVE_NEARMISS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  DISPLAY_P902
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM display_p902 USING p_form.

  DATA l_formname          TYPE tdsfname.

  PERFORM retrieve_p902_info USING p_form.

  IF p9001-bp_number IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM get_bp_injury_data.
    l_formname = 'ZP902_INCIDENT_BP'.
  ELSE.
    l_formname = 'ZP902_INCIDENT'.
  ENDIF.


  CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
    EXPORTING
      formname           = l_formname
      variant            = ' '
      direct_call        = ' '
    IMPORTING
      fm_name            = w_formname
    EXCEPTIONS
      no_form            = 1
      no_function_module = 2
      OTHERS             = 3.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
*         WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR : w_control_parameters, w_output_options.
  w_control_parameters-preview    = abap_true.
  w_control_parameters-no_dialog  = abap_true.
* w_output_options-tddest         = 'LOCL'.
  w_output_options-tdimmed        = abap_true.
  w_output_options-tddelete       = abap_true.

  w_control_parameters-getotf     = ' '.

  CALL FUNCTION w_formname
    EXPORTING
      user_settings      = ''
      control_parameters = w_control_parameters
      output_options     = w_output_options
      incident           = w_incident_form
      longtext           = lt_longt
      tb_injury          = tb_injury_form
      tb_bp_injury       = tbl_bp_injury
    IMPORTING
      job_output_info    = w_output_info.


ENDFORM.                    " DISPLAY_P902

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RETRIEVE_P902_INFO
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM retrieve_p902_info USING p_form.

  DATA: test              TYPE zhr_inc_tbody,
        fieldname(30)     TYPE c,
        fieldname1(30)    TYPE c,
        lt_longs          TYPE table_of_strings,
        l_longt           TYPE char255,
        l_longs           TYPE string,
        index(3)          TYPE n,
        act_index         TYPE sytabix,
        length            TYPE sytabix,
        maxlen            TYPE sytabix,
        finish(1)         TYPE c,
        num(2)            TYPE n,
        l_cr_lf(2)        TYPE c,
        l_struc(10)       TYPE c,
        pkey              TYPE pskey,
        l_original(1)     TYPE c.


  IF p_form = 'O'.
    l_original = 'X'.
  ENDIF.

  CALL METHOD zcl_p902_class=>retrieve_inc_for_smartform
    EXPORTING
      i_pernr                 = p9001-pernr
      i_incident_no           = p9001-identnr
      i_original              = l_original
    CHANGING
      c_incident_form         = w_incident_form
      tb_injury_location_list = tb_injury_form.


  CALL METHOD zcl_p902_class=>convert_string_to_table(
    EXPORTING
      i_string    = w_incident_form-inc_notes
      i_line_size = 65
      i_append    = ' '
      i_smartform = 'X'
    IMPORTING
      e_table     = lt_longt ).

*
*
*  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <a> TYPE any.
*  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <b> TYPE any.
*
*  CREATE OBJECT the_incident.
*  CLEAR wa_incident.
*
*  IF p_form = 'O'.
*    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9001
*           WHERE pernr   = p9001-pernr
*             AND identnr = p9001-identnr.
*    MOVE-CORRESPONDING pa9001 TO wa_incident.
*    l_struc = 'PA9001'.
*  ELSE.
*    PERFORM clear_p9001_numbers.
*    PERFORM update_p9001_numbers.
*    MOVE-CORRESPONDING p9001 TO wa_incident.
*    l_struc = 'P9001'.
*  ENDIF.
*  wa_incident = the_incident->enrich_data( incident_in = wa_incident
*                                      injuries = test
*                                      natures = test ).
*
*  index = 1.
*  DO c_loc_count TIMES.
*    CONCATENATE l_struc '-LOC_' index INTO fieldname.
*    CONCATENATE 'wa_incident-LOC_' index INTO fieldname1.
*    ASSIGN (fieldname) TO <a>.
*    ASSIGN (fieldname1) TO <b>.
*    <b> = <a>.
*    index = index + 1.
*  ENDDO.
*  index = 1.
*  DO c_nat_count TIMES.
*    CONCATENATE l_struc '-NAT_' index INTO fieldname.
*    CONCATENATE 'wa_incident-NAT_' index INTO fieldname1.
*    ASSIGN (fieldname) TO <a>.
*    ASSIGN (fieldname1) TO <b>.
*    <b> = <a>.
*    index = index + 1.
*  ENDDO.
*  index = 1.
*  DO c_whe_count TIMES.
*    CONCATENATE l_struc '-WHE_' index INTO fieldname.
*    CONCATENATE 'wa_incident-WHE_' index INTO fieldname1.
*    ASSIGN (fieldname) TO <a>.
*    ASSIGN (fieldname1) TO <b>.
*    <b> = <a>.
*    index = index + 1.
*  ENDDO.
*
*  IF p_form = 'O'.
*    WRITE pa9001-identnr TO wa_incident-identnr NO-ZERO.
*  ELSE.
*    WRITE p9001-identnr TO wa_incident-identnr NO-ZERO.
*  ENDIF.
*  CONDENSE wa_incident-identnr NO-GAPS.
*  the_incident->set_incident( wa_incident ).
*
*  l_cr_lf = cl_abap_char_utilities=>cr_lf.
*
*  CLEAR   : lt_longs, lt_longt.
*  REFRESH : lt_longs, lt_longt.
*  IF p_form = 'O'.
*    CLEAR tx-key.
*    MOVE-CORRESPONDING pa9001 TO tx-key.
*    PERFORM retrieve_pa9001_text.
*  ELSE.
*    PERFORM get_p902_text.
*    LOOP AT gt_outtxt1 INTO gr_outtxt1.
*      APPEND gr_outtxt1 TO lt_longt.
*    ENDLOOP.
*  ENDIF.
*

ENDFORM.                    " RETRIEVE_P902_INFO

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  DISPLAY_DETAILED_EARNINGS_RPT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Display the Detailed Earnings Report, showing a breakdown
*       of the PIAWE earnings by Wage Type.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM display_detailed_earnings_rpt.

  DATA:
    lt_wt_pre_52_wk           TYPE zhswc_piawe_calc_wt_tbl,
    lt_wt_post_52_wk          TYPE zhswc_piawe_calc_wt_tbl.

  DATA:
    ls_piawe_calc             TYPE zhswc_piawe_calc.

  CALL METHOD zcl_p902_class=>retrieve_smartform_earnings
    EXPORTING
      i_pernr       = p9001-pernr
      i_incident_no = p9001-identnr
      i_display     = 'X'.

*  PERFORM retrieve_p902_info USING 'O'.
*
*  PERFORM retrieve_detailed_earnings_inf
*    USING
*      wa_incident
*    CHANGING
*      ls_piawe_calc
*      lt_wt_pre_52_wk
*      lt_wt_post_52_wk.
*
*  CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
*    EXPORTING
*      formname           = 'ZHR_FORMS_PIAWE_EARNINGS'
*      variant            = ' '
*      direct_call        = ' '
*    IMPORTING
*      fm_name            = w_formname
*    EXCEPTIONS
*      no_form            = 1
*      no_function_module = 2
*      OTHERS             = 3.
*  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
** MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
**         WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  CLEAR:
*    w_control_parameters,
*    w_output_options.
*  w_control_parameters-preview    = abap_true.
*  w_control_parameters-no_dialog  = abap_true.
*  w_output_options-tdimmed        = abap_true.
*  w_output_options-tddelete       = abap_true.
*
*  w_control_parameters-getotf     = ' '.
*
*  CALL FUNCTION w_formname
*    EXPORTING
*      user_settings      = ''
*      control_parameters = w_control_parameters
*      output_options     = w_output_options
*      incident           = wa_incident
*      piawe_calc         = ls_piawe_calc
*      wt_pre_52_wk_tbl   = lt_wt_pre_52_wk
*      wt_post_52_wk_tbl  = lt_wt_post_52_wk
*    IMPORTING
*      job_output_info    = w_output_info.

ENDFORM.                    " DISPLAY_DETAILED_EARNINGS_RPT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RETRIEVE_DETAILED_EARNINGS_INF
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Retrieve data for the Detailed Earnings Report.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM retrieve_detailed_earnings_inf
  USING
    fs_incident               TYPE zhr_inc_incident
  CHANGING
    fs_piawe_calc             TYPE zhswc_piawe_calc
    ft_wt_pre_52_wk           TYPE zhswc_piawe_calc_wt_tbl
    ft_wt_post_52_wk          TYPE zhswc_piawe_calc_wt_tbl.

  CALL FUNCTION 'ZHWC_CALCULATE_PIAWE'
    EXPORTING
      i_pernr                 = fs_incident-pernr
      i_date_as_at            = fs_incident-dateincident
      i_time_as_at            = fs_incident-timeincident
    IMPORTING
      e_piawe_calc            = fs_piawe_calc
      et_wt_pre_52_wk         = ft_wt_pre_52_wk
      et_wt_post_52_wk        = ft_wt_post_52_wk
    EXCEPTIONS
      paid_lt_min_pay_periods = 1
      OTHERS                  = 2.

  CASE sy-subrc.
    WHEN 0.
    WHEN 1.
      MESSAGE 'No PIAWE calc possible - employed < min pay periods'
              TYPE 'E'.
    WHEN OTHERS.
      MESSAGE 'Error during PIAWE calculate' TYPE 'E'.
  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.                    " RETRIEVE_DETAILED_EARNINGS_INF

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_EMP_ADDRESS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_emp_address USING p_pernr.

  DATA: num(2)                 TYPE n,
        l_zim_address          TYPE zim_address.

* June 2020
* Change logic to :
* - Display the contact details entered on the P902 if the P902
*   was entered by somebody else.
* - Only allow the mobile and email address to be changed
* - Retrieve all other fields from IT0006/IT0105

* Retrieve the contact details from the incident
  zim_screen_address-oth_work_external =
                                   zim_incident-oth_work_external.
  zim_screen_address-oth_work_eagle  = zim_incident-oth_work_eagle.
  zim_screen_address-oth_home_tel    = zim_incident-oth_home_tel.
  zim_screen_address-oth_home_mobile = zim_incident-oth_home_mobile.
  zim_screen_address-oth_home_email  = zim_incident-oth_home_email.

  zim_screen_address-land1 = 'AU'.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0006
         WHERE pernr = p_pernr
           AND subty = '1   '
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    zim_screen_address-stras    = pa0006-stras.
    zim_screen_address-locat    = pa0006-locat.
    zim_screen_address-ort01    = pa0006-ort01.
    zim_screen_address-regio    = pa0006-state.
    zim_screen_address-postcode = pa0006-pstlz.
    CONCATENATE pa0006-stras '/'
                pa0006-ort01
                pa0006-state
                pa0006-pstlz
               INTO wa_incident-address
               SEPARATED BY space.
    num = 1.
    DO 6 TIMES.
      CONCATENATE 'PA0006-COM' num INTO l_field.
      ASSIGN (l_field) TO <fs>.
      IF <fs> = 'TEL'.
        CONCATENATE 'PA0006-NUM' num INTO l_field.
        ASSIGN (l_field) TO <fs>.
        zim_screen_address-home_phone = <fs>.
      ENDIF.
      num = num + 1.
    ENDDO.
    num = 1.
    DO 6 TIMES.
      CONCATENATE 'PA0006-COM' num INTO l_field.
      ASSIGN (l_field) TO <fs>.
      IF <fs> = 'MOBL'.
        CONCATENATE 'PA0006-NUM' num INTO l_field.
        ASSIGN (l_field) TO <fs>.
        zim_screen_address-mobile = <fs>.
      ENDIF.
      num = num + 1.
    ENDDO.
  ENDIF.

*    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0006
*           WHERE pernr = p_pernr
*             AND subty = '98  '
*             AND begda LE sy-datum
*             AND endda GE sy-datum.
*    IF sy-subrc = 0.
*      zim_screen_address-stras    = pa0006-stras.
*      zim_screen_address-locat    = pa0006-locat.
*      zim_screen_address-ort01    = pa0006-ort01.
*      zim_screen_address-regio    = pa0006-state.
*      zim_screen_address-postcode = pa0006-pstlz.
*      CONCATENATE pa0006-stras '/'
*                  pa0006-ort01
*                  pa0006-state
*                  pa0006-pstlz
*                 INTO wa_incident-address
*                 SEPARATED BY space.
*    ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0006
         WHERE pernr = p_pernr
           AND subty = '99  '
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    num = 1.
    DO 6 TIMES.
      CONCATENATE 'PA0006-COM' num INTO l_field.
      ASSIGN (l_field) TO <fs>.
      IF <fs> = 'WORK'.
        CONCATENATE 'PA0006-NUM' num INTO l_field.
        ASSIGN (l_field) TO <fs>.
        zim_screen_address-work_phone = <fs>.
      ENDIF.
      num = num + 1.
    ENDDO.
  ENDIF.

*  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0105
*         WHERE pernr = p_pernr
*           AND subty = 'MAIL'
*           AND begda LE sy-datum
*           AND endda GE sy-datum.
*  IF sy-subrc = 0.
*    zim_screen_address-email    = pa0105-usrid_long.
*  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_address
         INTO l_zim_address
         WHERE pernr = p_pernr.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    zim_screen_address-alternate_contact =
          l_zim_address-alternate_contact.
    zim_screen_address-alternate_phone =
          l_zim_address-alternate_phone.
    zim_screen_address-no_corro         = l_zim_address-no_corro.
    zim_screen_address-contact_details1 =
          l_zim_address-contact_details1.
    zim_screen_address-contact_details2 =
          l_zim_address-contact_details2.
    IF l_zim_address-mobile IS NOT INITIAL.
      zim_screen_address-mobile = l_zim_address-mobile.
    ENDIF.
    IF l_zim_address-email IS NOT INITIAL.
      zim_screen_address-email  = l_zim_address-email.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  w_old_screen = zim_screen_address.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_EMP_ADDRESS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text

      IMPORTING
        table  = gt_outtxt1
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.

      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.

  ENDIF.


  PERFORM save_text_incident TABLES gt_outtxt1
                                   gt_outtxt1_old
                            USING  '   '
                                   ' '.


ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_INCIDENT_NOTES

*&      Form  SAVE_INCIDENT_NOTES
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_contact_notes .


  IF NOT emp_txt_container IS INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->get_text_as_r3table
      IMPORTING
        table  = gt_outtxt2
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.

  ENDIF.

  IF NOT mgr_txt_container IS INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD mgr_txt_text->get_text_as_r3table
      IMPORTING
        table  = gt_outtxt3
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.

  ENDIF.

  IF NOT ins_txt_container IS INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD ins_txt_text->get_text_as_r3table
      IMPORTING
        table  = gt_outtxt4
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.

  ENDIF.

  IF NOT ntd_txt_container IS INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD ntd_txt_text->get_text_as_r3table
      IMPORTING
        table  = gt_outtxt5
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.

  ENDIF.


  PERFORM save_text_contact TABLES gt_outtxt2
                                   gt_outtxt2_old
                            USING  'EMP'
                                   ' '
                                   ' '.

  PERFORM save_text_contact TABLES gt_outtxt3
                                   gt_outtxt3_old
                            USING  'MGR'
                                   ' '
                                   ' '.

  PERFORM save_text_contact TABLES gt_outtxt4
                                   gt_outtxt4_old
                            USING  'INS'
                                   ' '
                                   ' '.

  PERFORM save_text_contact TABLES gt_outtxt5
                                   gt_outtxt5_old
                            USING  'NTD'
                                   ' '
                                   ' '.


ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_CONTACT_NOTES

FORM save_contact_notes_pre88 .


  IF NOT emp_txt1_container IS INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD emp_txt1_text->get_text_as_r3table
      IMPORTING
        table  = gt_outtxt2a
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.

  ENDIF.

    CALL METHOD mgr_txt1_text->get_text_as_r3table
      IMPORTING
        table  = gt_outtxt2c
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.

  ENDIF.

  IF NOT ins_txt_container IS INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD ins_txt_text->get_text_as_r3table
      IMPORTING
        table  = gt_outtxt4
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.

  ENDIF.

  IF NOT ntd_txt_container IS INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD ntd_txt_text->get_text_as_r3table
      IMPORTING
        table  = gt_outtxt5
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS = 1.

  ENDIF.


  PERFORM save_text_contact TABLES gt_outtxt2a
                                   gt_outtxt2a_old
                            USING  'PRE88EMP'
                                   ' '
                                   ' '.

  PERFORM save_text_contact TABLES gt_outtxt2c
                                   gt_outtxt2c_old
                            USING  'PRE88MGR'
                                   ' '
                                   ' '.

  PERFORM save_text_contact TABLES gt_outtxt4
                                   gt_outtxt4_old
                            USING  'PRE88INS'
                                   ' '
                                   ' '.

  PERFORM save_text_contact TABLES gt_outtxt5
                                   gt_outtxt5_old
                            USING  'PRE88NTD'
                                   ' '
                                   ' '.


ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_CONTACT_NOTES_PRE88

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_SERV_REQ_NOTES
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_serv_req_notes .

  DATA: l_start             TYPE i,
         l_end               TYPE i.

  IF w_save_req IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF NOT serv_txt_container IS INITIAL.
      CALL METHOD serv_txt_text->get_text_as_r3table
        IMPORTING
          table  = gt_outtxt6
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS = 1.
      CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS = 1.
      CALL METHOD serv_txt_text->delete_text
        EXCEPTIONS
          error_cntl_call_method = 1
          OTHERS                 = 2.
    ENDIF.

    CONCATENATE 'EXT' w_request INTO w_req_key.
    PERFORM save_text_contact_72 TABLES gt_outtxt6
                                     gt_outtxt6_old
                              USING  w_req_key
                                     w_seq_no
                                     ' '.
    REFRESH gt_email_body.
    LOOP AT gt_outtxt6 INTO gr_outtxt6.
      APPEND gr_outtxt6 TO gt_email_body.
    ENDLOOP.

    CONCATENATE 'EXT' w_request w_seq_no INTO w_doc_unique.
    IF w_request = 'E'.
      REFRESH r_cat.
      PERFORM add_r_cat USING '10'.
      w_prov_code = zim_screen_serv_req-prov_code.
      PERFORM correspondence USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                   zim_in_mast-incident
                                   w_doc_unique
                                   ' '.
      CLEAR w_prov_code.
    ELSEIF w_request = 'R'.
      REFRESH r_cat.
      PERFORM add_r_cat USING '11'.
      PERFORM correspondence USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                 zim_in_mast-incident
                                 w_doc_unique
                                 ' '.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_SERV_REQ_NOTES

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_NOTES
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_2243   text
*      -->P_GT_OUTTXT2  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_notes  TABLES   tb_outtxt STRUCTURE gr_outtxt2
                         tb_outtxt_old STRUCTURE gr_outtxt2
                USING    p_code
                         p_action.

  CONCATENATE zim_in_mast-pernr zim_in_mast-incident p_code
              INTO w_text_key.
  REFRESH : tbl_tline, tb_outtxt.
  CLEAR   : tbl_tline.


  CALL FUNCTION 'READ_TEXT'
    EXPORTING
      id                      = 'ZIM1'
      language                = 'E'
      name                    = w_text_key
      object                  = 'ZPOLICE'
    TABLES
      lines                   = tbl_tline
    EXCEPTIONS
      id                      = 1
      language                = 2
      name                    = 3
      not_found               = 4
      object                  = 5
      reference_check         = 6
      wrong_access_to_archive = 7
      OTHERS                  = 8.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
  ENDIF.

  IF p_action NE 'DISP'.
    IF p_code = 'DGOAL001' OR
       p_code = 'DADJ001'.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM add_user_line TABLES tbl_tline
                            USING 100.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT tbl_tline.
    gr_outtxt2 = tbl_tline-tdline.
    APPEND gr_outtxt2 TO tb_outtxt.
  ENDLOOP.

* Create a blank line at the end to allow all the previous
* lines to be set as protected.
  DESCRIBE TABLE tbl_tline LINES w_lines.
  IF w_lines GT 0.
    CLEAR gr_outtxt2.
    APPEND gr_outtxt2 TO tb_outtxt.
  ENDIF.

  tb_outtxt_old[] = tb_outtxt[].

ENDFORM.                    " GET_NOTES

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_NOTES_FOR_LETTER
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_2243   text
*      -->P_GT_OUTTXT2  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_notes_for_letter  USING    p_code.


  CONCATENATE zim_in_mast-pernr zim_in_mast-incident p_code
              INTO w_text_key.
  REFRESH : tbl_tline.
  CLEAR   : tbl_tline.


  CALL FUNCTION 'READ_TEXT'
    EXPORTING
      id                      = 'ZIM1'
      language                = 'E'
      name                    = w_text_key
      object                  = 'ZPOLICE'
    TABLES
      lines                   = tbl_tline
    EXCEPTIONS
      id                      = 1
      language                = 2
      name                    = 3
      not_found               = 4
      object                  = 5
      reference_check         = 6
      wrong_access_to_archive = 7
      OTHERS                  = 8.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
  ENDIF.

  REFRESH gt_email_body.
  LOOP AT tbl_tline.
    APPEND tbl_tline-tdline TO gt_email_body.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_NOTES_FOR_LETTER

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_NOTES_INCIDENT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_2243   text
*      -->P_GT_OUTTXT2  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_notes_incident  TABLES   tb_outtxt STRUCTURE gr_outtxt1
                         tb_outtxt_old STRUCTURE gr_outtxt1
                USING    p_code.

  CONCATENATE p9001-pernr p9001-identnr p_code
              INTO w_text_key.
  REFRESH : tbl_tline, tb_outtxt.
  CLEAR   : tbl_tline.


  CALL FUNCTION 'READ_TEXT'
    EXPORTING
      id                      = 'ZIM1'
      language                = 'E'
      name                    = w_text_key
      object                  = 'ZPOLICE'
    TABLES
      lines                   = tbl_tline
    EXCEPTIONS
      id                      = 1
      language                = 2
      name                    = 3
      not_found               = 4
      object                  = 5
      reference_check         = 6
      wrong_access_to_archive = 7
      OTHERS                  = 8.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_1000_action NE 'DISP'.
    PERFORM add_user_line TABLES tbl_tline
                          USING 65.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT tbl_tline.
    gr_outtxt1 = tbl_tline-tdline.
    APPEND gr_outtxt1 TO tb_outtxt.
  ENDLOOP.

* Create a blank line at the end to allow all the previous
* lines to be set as protected.
  DESCRIBE TABLE tbl_tline LINES w_lines.
  IF w_lines GT 0.
    CLEAR gr_outtxt1.
    APPEND gr_outtxt1 TO tb_outtxt.
  ENDIF.

  tb_outtxt_old[] = tb_outtxt[].

ENDFORM.                    " GET_NOTES_INCIDENT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_NOTES_PA9001
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_2243   text
*      -->P_GT_OUTTXT2  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_notes_pa9001    TABLES   tb_outtxt STRUCTURE gr_outtxt1
                USING    p_code.

  DATA: l_longt           TYPE char255.

  REFRESH lt_longt.
  CLEAR tx-key.
  MOVE-CORRESPONDING p9001 TO tx-key.
  PERFORM retrieve_pa9001_text.

  REFRESH tbl_tline.
  CLEAR tbl_tline.
  LOOP AT lt_longt INTO l_longt.
    tbl_tline-tdline = l_longt.
    APPEND tbl_tline.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF w_1000_action NE 'DISP'.
    PERFORM add_user_line TABLES tbl_tline
                          USING 65.
    CLEAR tbl_tline.
    IF  w_1000_action  = 'UNM'.
      tbl_tline-tdline = 'Near Miss converted to Incident'.
      APPEND tbl_tline.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT tbl_tline.
    gr_outtxt1 = tbl_tline-tdline.
    APPEND gr_outtxt1 TO tb_outtxt.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF w_lines GT 0.
    CLEAR gr_outtxt1.
    APPEND gr_outtxt1 TO tb_outtxt.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_NOTES_PA9001

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*

                           p_type
                           p_seq
                           p_display.


ENDFORM.                    " PROCESS_DESCRIPITON

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_CONTACT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_contact .

  DATA: tb_zim_in_mast            TYPE zim_in_mast.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         INTO tb_zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab5 INTO gr_outtab5.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_act
        WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          AND contact_type = gr_outtab5-contact_type
          AND cont_act_code = gr_outtab5-cont_act_code.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      IF gr_outtab5-cont_act_comp IS NOT INITIAL.
        CLEAR zim_in_act.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING gr_outtab5 TO zim_in_act.
        zim_in_act-pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr.
        zim_in_act-incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
        zim_in_act-cont_act_date = sy-datum.
        w_action            = 'I'.
        INSERT zim_in_act.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_ACT'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM update_in_act_log USING w_action.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      IF gr_outtab5-cont_act_comp NE zim_in_act-cont_act_comp.
        zim_in_act-cont_act_comp = gr_outtab5-cont_act_comp.
        zim_in_act-cont_act_date = sy-datum.
        w_action           = 'U'.
        UPDATE zim_in_act.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_ACT'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM update_in_act_log USING w_action.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  tb_zim_in_mast-last_act_date = sy-datum.
  w_action           = 'U'.
  UPDATE zim_in_mast FROM tb_zim_in_mast.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    w_subrc = sy-subrc.
    ROLLBACK WORK.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_MAST'
                      'Return code ' w_subrc.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM update_in_mast_log USING w_action
                                     tb_zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

  zim_in_mast-last_act_date = sy-datum.



ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_CONTACT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  UPDATE_INCIDENT_LOG
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_W_ACTION  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM update_incident_log  USING   p_action
                                  p_incident STRUCTURE zim_incident.

  CLEAR zim_incident_log.
  MOVE-CORRESPONDING p_incident TO zim_incident_log.
  zim_incident_log-date_processed = sy-datum.
  zim_incident_log-time_processed = sy-uzeit.
  zim_incident_log-code_processed = p_action.
  zim_incident_log-user_processed = sy-uname.
  INSERT zim_incident_log.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    w_subrc = sy-subrc.
    ROLLBACK WORK.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_INCIDENT_LOG'
                                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " UPDATE_INCIDENT_LOG

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_IM_MAST
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_im_mast .

  DATA: tb_zim_in_mast            TYPE zim_in_mast.

  PERFORM save_im_address.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         INTO tb_zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

  zim_in_mast-last_act_date = sy-datum.
*******************MD***********
*  IF tb_zim_in_mast-background IS NOT INITIAL.
*    PERFORM update_notes USING
*                'Injury Management File Upd/Background Updates' ' '.
*  ENDIF.
*
*
*  IF tb_zim_in_mast-strategies IS NOT INITIAL.
*    PERFORM update_notes USING
*                'Injury Management Strategies/Actions Updates' ' '.
*  ENDIF.
*
**************MD*****************
  IF tb_zim_in_mast NE zim_in_mast.
    PERFORM check_init_ass_actions.
    IF tb_zim_in_mast-info_consent IS INITIAL AND
       zim_in_mast-info_consent    IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM update_notes USING
              'Information Consent Form has been received'
              ' '.
    ENDIF.
    IF tb_zim_in_mast-imp_provided IS INITIAL AND
       zim_in_mast-imp_provided    IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM update_notes USING
              'Injury Management Plan has been received'
              ' '.
    ENDIF.
    IF tb_zim_in_mast-mmi_received IS INITIAL AND
       zim_in_mast-mmi_received    IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM update_notes USING
           'Maximum Medical Improvement information has been received'
              ' '.
    ENDIF.

*->>CR2825
    IF tb_zim_in_mast-inc_status NE zim_in_mast-inc_status AND
       zim_in_mast-inc_status    = 'O'.
      zim_in_mast-opened_date = sy-datum.
      CLEAR:  zim_in_mast-date_closed, zim_in_mast-closure_status.
    ENDIF.
*<<-CR2825

    IF tb_zim_in_mast-inc_status NE zim_in_mast-inc_status AND
       zim_in_mast-inc_status    IS NOT INITIAL AND
       zim_in_mast-inc_status    = 'T'.
      zim_in_mast-reopened = 'X'.
      zim_in_mast-reopened_date = sy-datum.
      CLEAR: zim_in_mast-date_closed, zim_in_mast-closure_status.
      PERFORM update_notes USING
           'Case has been REOPENED'
           ' '.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_mast-inc_status    NE 'T'.
      CLEAR: zim_in_mast-reopened, zim_in_mast-reopened_date.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_mast-ftc IS NOT INITIAL AND
       tb_zim_in_mast-ftc IS INITIAL.
      zim_in_mast-ftco_date = sy-datum.
      zim_in_mast-ima = zim_in_mast-ftc.
      PERFORM create_reminder USING 'F'
                                    zim_in_mast-ftc
                                    '60'
                                    '16'
                                    ' '
                                    '00000000'.

    ENDIF.
    IF zim_in_deploy-deploy_status = 'M'.
    ELSE.
      IF zim_in_mast-closure_status IS NOT INITIAL AND
        tb_zim_in_mast-closure_status IS INITIAL.
        zim_in_mast-date_closed = sy-datum.
        zim_in_mast-inc_status  = 'C'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_in_mast-closure_status IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR zim_in_mast-date_closed.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_in_mast-inc_status = 'F' AND
       tb_zim_in_mast-inc_status NE 'F'.
      PERFORM create_reminder USING 'T'
                                    zim_in_mast-ftc
                                    '60'
                                    '17'
                                    ' '
                                    '00000000'.
    ENDIF.
    w_action           = 'U'.
    UPDATE zim_in_mast.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_MAST'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_mast_log USING w_action
                                       zim_in_mast.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.



ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_IM_MAST

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_IM_ADDRESS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_im_address .


  IF w_old_screen NE zim_screen_address.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_address
        WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR zim_address.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_screen_address TO zim_address.
      zim_address-mandt   = sy-mandt.
      zim_address-pernr   = zim_in_mast-pernr.
      w_action            = 'I'.
      INSERT zim_address.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_ADDRESS'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_screen_address TO zim_address.
      zim_address-mandt   = sy-mandt.
      zim_address-pernr   = zim_in_mast-pernr.
      w_action           = 'U'.
      UPDATE zim_address.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_ADDRESS'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    PERFORM update_address_log USING w_action.
  ENDIF.

  w_old_screen = zim_screen_address.


ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_IM_ADDRESS


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_IM_PRE88
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_im_pre88 .

  DATA: l_zim_in_mast            TYPE zim_in_mast,
        l_zim_in_pre88           TYPE zim_in_pre88.

  IF w_old_screen NE zim_screen_address.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_address
        WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR zim_address.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_screen_address TO zim_address.
      zim_address-mandt   = sy-mandt.
      zim_address-pernr   = zim_in_mast-pernr.
      w_action            = 'I'.
      INSERT zim_address.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_ADDRESS'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_screen_address TO zim_address.
      zim_address-mandt   = sy-mandt.
      zim_address-pernr   = zim_in_mast-pernr.
      w_action           = 'U'.
      UPDATE zim_address.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_ADDRESS'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    PERFORM update_address_log USING w_action.
  ENDIF.

  w_old_screen = zim_screen_address.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         INTO l_zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

  IF l_zim_in_mast NE zim_in_mast.
    w_action           = 'U'.
    UPDATE zim_in_mast.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_MAST'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_mast_log USING w_action
                                       zim_in_mast.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR w_action.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_pre88
         INTO l_zim_in_pre88
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    w_action     = 'I'.
    zim_in_pre88-created_date   = sy-datum.
  ELSEIF l_zim_in_pre88 NE zim_in_pre88.
    w_action           = 'U'.
  ENDIF.

* Cater for changing from incident only
  IF zim_in_pre88-ass_status   NE 'I' AND
     l_zim_in_pre88-ass_status  = 'I'.
    CLEAR : zim_in_pre88-current_status, zim_in_pre88-ass_comp_date,
            zim_in_pre88-closed_date.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-ass_status    = 'I' AND
     l_zim_in_pre88-ass_status NE 'I'.
    zim_in_pre88-current_status = 'F'.
    zim_in_pre88-ass_comp_date  = sy-datum.
    zim_in_pre88-closed_date    = sy-datum.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-ass_status    = 'T' AND
     l_zim_in_pre88-ass_status NE 'T'.
    zim_in_pre88-ass_comp_date  = sy-datum.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-ass_status NE l_zim_in_pre88-ass_status.
    zim_in_pre88-status_date = sy-datum.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-liab_ltr_comp NE l_zim_in_pre88-liab_ltr_comp AND
     zim_in_pre88-liab_ltr_comp IS NOT INITIAL.
    zim_in_pre88-current_status = 'M'.
    zim_in_pre88-current_action = '10'.
    zim_in_pre88-status_date = sy-datum.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-liab_ltr_app NE l_zim_in_pre88-liab_ltr_app AND
     zim_in_pre88-liab_ltr_app IS NOT INITIAL.
    zim_in_pre88-current_status = 'C'.
    zim_in_pre88-current_action = '11'.
    zim_in_pre88-status_date = sy-datum.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-rec_comp NE l_zim_in_pre88-rec_comp AND
     zim_in_pre88-rec_comp IS NOT INITIAL.
    zim_in_pre88-current_status = 'T'.
    zim_in_pre88-current_action = '30'.
    zim_in_pre88-status_date    = sy-datum.
    zim_in_pre88-rec_comp_date  = sy-datum.
    zim_in_pre88-rec_comp_user  = sy-uname.
    PERFORM get_employee_name USING    sy-uname
                              CHANGING w_pre_rec_name.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-rec_app_tl NE l_zim_in_pre88-rec_app_tl AND
     zim_in_pre88-rec_app_tl IS NOT INITIAL.
    zim_in_pre88-current_status   = 'M'.
    zim_in_pre88-current_action   = '31'.
    zim_in_pre88-status_date      = sy-datum.
    zim_in_pre88-tl_approved      = sy-uname.
    zim_in_pre88-tl_approved_date = sy-datum.
    PERFORM get_employee_name USING    sy-uname
                              CHANGING w_pre_tl_name.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-rec_app_mgr NE l_zim_in_pre88-rec_app_mgr AND
     zim_in_pre88-rec_app_mgr IS NOT INITIAL.
    zim_in_pre88-current_status    = 'D'.
    zim_in_pre88-current_action    = '32'.
    zim_in_pre88-status_date       = sy-datum.
    zim_in_pre88-mgr_approved      = sy-uname.
    zim_in_pre88-mgr_approved_date = sy-datum.
    PERFORM get_employee_name USING    sy-uname
                              CHANGING w_pre_mgr_name.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-rec_app_dir NE l_zim_in_pre88-rec_app_dir AND
     zim_in_pre88-rec_app_dir IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_in_pre88-recommendation = 'D'.
      zim_in_pre88-current_status    = 'M'.
      zim_in_pre88-current_action    = '33'.
    ELSE.
      zim_in_pre88-current_status    = 'C'.
      zim_in_pre88-current_action    = '34'.
    ENDIF.
    zim_in_pre88-status_date       = sy-datum.
    zim_in_pre88-dir_approved      = sy-uname.
    zim_in_pre88-dir_approved_date = sy-datum.
    PERFORM get_employee_name USING    sy-uname
                              CHANGING w_pre_dir_name.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_action IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF w_action = 'I'.
      INSERT zim_in_pre88.
    ELSEIF w_action = 'U'.
      UPDATE zim_in_pre88.
    ENDIF.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_PRE88'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM update_in_pre88_log USING w_action
                                      zim_in_pre88.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_IM_PRE88

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_HEALTH_PROF
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_health_prof .

  DATA: l_seq_no                  TYPE zim_in_med-seq_no.

  IF gt_outtab6_old[] NE gt_outtab6[].
* First delete all records and then re-add them
    LOOP AT gt_outtab6_old INTO gr_outtab6
         WHERE med_prac IS NOT INITIAL.
      w_action           = 'D'.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING gr_outtab6 TO zim_in_med.
      DELETE zim_in_med.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_MED'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_in_med_log USING w_action.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
    l_seq_no = 1.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab6 INTO gr_outtab6
         WHERE med_prac IS NOT INITIAL.
      w_action           = 'I'.
      zim_in_med-mandt = sy-mandt.
      zim_in_med-pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr.
      zim_in_med-incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
      zim_in_med-seq_no = l_seq_no.
      zim_in_med-med_prac = gr_outtab6-med_prac.
      INSERT zim_in_med.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_MED'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_in_med_log USING w_action.
      ENDIF.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_in_med TO gr_outtab6.
      MODIFY gt_outtab6 FROM gr_outtab6.
      ADD 1 TO l_seq_no.
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDIF.

  gt_outtab6_old[] = gt_outtab6[].


ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_HEALTH_PROF

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_CLAIM_DETAILS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_claim_details .

  DATA: l_operation            TYPE  pspar-actio.

  IF w_old_ins = w_ins.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  l_operation = 'INS'.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9082
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND casno = zim_in_mast-incident.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9076
           WHERE pernr = pa9082-pernr
             AND objps = pa9082-objps.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      l_operation = 'MOD'.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR pa9076.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    ROLLBACK WORK.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Infotype 9082 Not Found'.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR p9076.
  MOVE-CORRESPONDING pa9076 TO p9076.
  p9076-pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr.
  p9076-infty = '9076'.
  p9076-objps = pa9082-objps.
  IF p9076-subty IS INITIAL.
    IF zim_in_mast-hod = abap_true.
      p9076-subty = '9000'.
    ELSE.
      p9076-subty = pa9082-subty.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  p9076-wclmn = w_ins-claim_no.
  p9076-stats = w_ins-claim_stats.
  p9076-begda = w_ins-claim_sdate.
  p9076-endda = w_ins-claim_sdate.
  p9076-sdate = w_ins-claim_sdate.

  PERFORM lock_employee USING p9076-pernr
                              '9076'.

  CALL FUNCTION 'HR_INFOTYPE_OPERATION'
    EXPORTING
      infty         = '9076'
      number        = p9076-pernr
      subtype       = p9076-subty
      lockindicator = p9076-sprps
      validityend   = pa9076-endda
      validitybegin = pa9076-begda
      recordnumber  = p9076-seqnr
      objectid      = p9076-objps
      record        = p9076
      operation     = l_operation
    IMPORTING
      return        = w_bapiret
      key           = w_bapi_key.

  PERFORM unlock_employee USING p9076-pernr
                                '9076'.


  IF w_bapiret-type = 'E' OR
     w_bapiret-type = 'A'.
    w_error = abap_true.
    CLEAR w_code2.
    ROLLBACK WORK.
    MESSAGE ID     w_bapiret-id
            TYPE   w_bapiret-type
            NUMBER w_bapiret-number
            WITH   w_bapiret-message_v1
                   w_bapiret-message_v2
                   w_bapiret-message_v3
                   w_bapiret-message_v4.
  ENDIF.

  w_old_ins = w_ins.


ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_CLAIM_DETAILS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_SERVICE_REQUEST
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_service_request .

  CLEAR : w_save_req, w_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab8 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab8 INTO gr_outtab8.
    w_seq_no = gr_outtab8-seq_no.
    READ TABLE gt_outtab8_old INTO gr_outtab8_old INDEX sy-tabix.
    IF w_request = 'E'.
      IF gr_outtab8-ref_sent NE gr_outtab8_old-ref_sent.
        w_action                 = 'U'.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_prov
               WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
                 AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
                 AND seq_no   = gr_outtab8-seq_no.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_PROV'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ELSE.
          zim_in_prov-ref_sent  = gr_outtab8-ref_sent.
          UPDATE zim_in_prov.
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
            w_subrc = sy-subrc.
            ROLLBACK WORK.
            MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_PROV'
                              'Return code ' w_subrc.
          ELSE.
            PERFORM update_in_prov_log USING w_action.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF w_request = 'R'.
      IF gr_outtab8-ref_date    NE gr_outtab8_old-ref_date OR
         gr_outtab8-rec_date    NE gr_outtab8_old-rec_date OR
         gr_outtab8-status      NE gr_outtab8_old-status   OR
         gr_outtab8-review_date NE gr_outtab8_old-review_date.
        w_action                 = 'U'.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_rosa
               WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
                 AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
                 AND seq_no   = gr_outtab8-seq_no.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_ROSA'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ELSE.
          zim_in_rosa-ref_date    = gr_outtab8-ref_date.
          zim_in_rosa-rec_date    = gr_outtab8-rec_date.
          zim_in_rosa-status      = gr_outtab8-status.
          zim_in_rosa-review_date = gr_outtab8-review_date.
          IF gr_outtab8-review_date NE gr_outtab8_old-review_date.
            zim_in_mast-rosa_review = gr_outtab8-review_date.
          ENDIF.
          UPDATE zim_in_rosa.
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
            w_subrc = sy-subrc.
            ROLLBACK WORK.
            MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_ROSA'
                              'Return code ' w_subrc.
          ELSE.
            PERFORM update_in_rosa_log USING w_action.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF w_request = 'P'.
      IF gr_outtab8-rec_date    NE gr_outtab8_old-rec_date OR
         gr_outtab8-status      NE gr_outtab8_old-status   OR
         gr_outtab8-review_date NE gr_outtab8_old-review_date.
        w_action                 = 'U'.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_pmo
               WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
                 AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
                 AND seq_no   = gr_outtab8-seq_no.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_PMO'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ELSE.
          zim_in_pmo-rec_date    = gr_outtab8-rec_date.
          zim_in_pmo-status      = gr_outtab8-status.
          zim_in_pmo-review_date = gr_outtab8-review_date.
          IF gr_outtab8-review_date NE gr_outtab8_old-review_date.
            zim_in_mast-pmo_review = gr_outtab8-review_date.
          ENDIF.
          UPDATE zim_in_pmo.
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
            w_subrc = sy-subrc.
            ROLLBACK WORK.
            MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_PMO'
                              'Return code ' w_subrc.
          ELSE.
            PERFORM update_in_pmo_log USING w_action.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_seq_no.
  WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.

  IF w_request = 'E' AND
     zim_screen_serv_req-prov_code IS NOT INITIAL.
    w_action              = 'I'.
    w_save_req            = abap_true.
    CLEAR zim_in_prov.
    zim_in_prov-pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    zim_in_prov-incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.
    zim_in_prov-seq_no    = w_seq_no.
    zim_in_prov-date_req  = sy-datum.
    zim_in_prov-prov_code = zim_screen_serv_req-prov_code.
    zim_in_prov-prov_activity = zim_screen_serv_req-prov_activity.
    INSERT zim_in_prov.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_PROV'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_prov_log USING w_action.
    ENDIF.
  ELSEIF w_request = 'R' AND
     zim_screen_serv_req-rosa_request IS NOT INITIAL.
    w_action             = 'I'.
    w_save_req           = abap_true.
    CLEAR zim_in_rosa.
    zim_in_rosa-pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    zim_in_rosa-incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
    zim_in_rosa-seq_no   = w_seq_no.
    zim_in_rosa-req_date = sy-datum.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast-rosa_review.
    INSERT zim_in_rosa.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_ROSA'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_rosa_log USING w_action.
    ENDIF.
  ELSEIF w_request = 'P' AND
     zim_screen_serv_req-pmo_request IS NOT INITIAL.
    w_action             = 'I'.
    w_save_req           = abap_true.
    CLEAR zim_in_pmo.
    zim_in_pmo-pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    zim_in_pmo-incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
    zim_in_pmo-seq_no   = w_seq_no.
    zim_in_pmo-ref_date = sy-datum.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast-pmo_review.
    INSERT zim_in_pmo.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_PMO'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_pmo_log USING w_action.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.



ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_SERVICE_REQUEST

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_REHAB
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_rehab .

  CLEAR : w_save_rehab, w_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab9 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab9 INTO gr_outtab9.
    w_seq_no = gr_outtab9-seq_no.
    READ TABLE gt_outtab9_old INTO gr_outtab9_old INDEX sy-tabix.
    IF gr_outtab9-begda       NE gr_outtab9_old-begda   OR
       gr_outtab9-endda       NE gr_outtab9_old-endda   OR
       gr_outtab9-review_date NE gr_outtab9_old-review_date OR
       gr_outtab9-complete    NE gr_outtab9_old-complete.
      w_action                 = 'U'.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_rehab
             WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
               AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
               AND seq_no   = gr_outtab9-seq_no.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REHAB'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        zim_in_rehab-begda       = gr_outtab9-begda.
        zim_in_rehab-endda       = gr_outtab9-endda.
        zim_in_rehab-review_date = gr_outtab9-review_date.
        zim_in_rehab-complete    = gr_outtab9-complete.
        UPDATE zim_in_rehab.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REHAB'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM update_in_rehab_log USING w_action.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_seq_no.
  WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.

  IF zim_screen_rehab-orgeh IS NOT INITIAL.
    w_action              = 'I'.
    w_save_rehab          = abap_true.
    CLEAR zim_in_rehab.
    zim_in_rehab-pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    zim_in_rehab-incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.
    zim_in_rehab-seq_no    = w_seq_no.
    zim_in_rehab-orgeh     = zim_screen_rehab-orgeh.
    zim_in_rehab-begda     = zim_screen_rehab-begda.
    zim_in_rehab-endda     = zim_screen_rehab-endda.
    zim_in_rehab-review_date  = zim_screen_rehab-review_date.
    INSERT zim_in_rehab.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REHAB'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_rehab_log USING w_action.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_REHAB

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_ERG
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_erg .

  CLEAR : w_save_erg, w_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab10 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab10 INTO gr_outtab10.
    w_seq_no = gr_outtab10-seq_no.
    READ TABLE gt_outtab10_old INTO gr_outtab10_old INDEX sy-tabix.
    IF gr_outtab10-erg_rec_date   NE gr_outtab10_old-erg_rec_date  OR
       gr_outtab10-inspect_ref    NE gr_outtab10_old-inspect_ref.
      w_action                 = 'U'.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_erg
             WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
               AND incident = gr_outtab10-incident
               AND seq_no   = gr_outtab10-seq_no.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_ERG'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        zim_in_erg-erg_rec_date   = gr_outtab10-erg_rec_date.
        zim_in_erg-inspect_ref    = gr_outtab10-inspect_ref.
        UPDATE zim_in_erg.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_ERG'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM update_in_erg_log USING w_action.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_seq_no.
  WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.

  gt_outtab10_old[] = gt_outtab10[].

  IF zim_screen_erg-erg_sent IS NOT INITIAL.
    w_action              = 'I'.
    w_save_erg            = abap_true.
    CLEAR zim_in_erg.
    zim_in_erg-pernr         = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    zim_in_erg-incident      = zim_in_mast-incident.
    zim_in_erg-seq_no        = w_seq_no.
    zim_in_erg-erg_sent      = zim_screen_erg-erg_sent.
    zim_in_erg-erg_sent_date = sy-datum.
    INSERT zim_in_erg.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_ERG'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_erg_log USING w_action.
    ENDIF.

    COMMIT WORK AND WAIT.
    CLEAR zim_screen_erg-erg_sent.
    PERFORM get_erg.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_ERG

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_ERG_2
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_erg_2 .

  IF gt_outtab10[] NE gt_outtab10_old[].
    LOOP AT gt_outtab10 INTO gr_outtab10.
      READ TABLE gt_outtab10_old INTO gr_outtab10_old INDEX sy-tabix.
      IF gr_outtab10-pernr          NE gr_outtab10_old-pernr         OR
         gr_outtab10-incident       NE gr_outtab10_old-incident      OR
         gr_outtab10-seq_no         NE gr_outtab10_old-seq_no        OR
         gr_outtab10-inspect_emp    NE gr_outtab10_old-inspect_emp   OR
         gr_outtab10-inspect_comp   NE gr_outtab10_old-inspect_comp.
        w_action                 = 'U'.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_erg
               WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab10-pernr
                 AND incident = gr_outtab10-incident
                 AND seq_no   = gr_outtab10-seq_no.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_ERG'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ELSE.
          zim_in_erg-inspect_emp    = gr_outtab10-inspect_emp.
          zim_in_erg-inspect_comp   = gr_outtab10-inspect_comp.
          zim_in_erg-inspect_ref    = gr_outtab10-inspect_ref.
          UPDATE zim_in_erg.
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
            w_subrc = sy-subrc.
            ROLLBACK WORK.
            MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_ERG'
                              'Return code ' w_subrc.
          ELSE.
            PERFORM update_in_erg_log USING w_action.
          ENDIF.
          CONCATENATE 'ERG' zim_in_erg-seq_no INTO w_doc_unique.
          IF gr_outtab10-inspect_comp IS NOT INITIAL.
* Close any existing reminders
            SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_rem
                   WHERE pernr         = gr_outtab10-pernr
                     AND incident      = gr_outtab10-incident
                     AND doc_unique    = w_doc_unique
                     AND note_cat      = '14'
                     AND business_rule = '01'.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              w_action = 'U'.
              zim_in_rem-note_comp = abap_true.
              UPDATE zim_in_rem.
              IF sy-subrc NE 0.
                w_subrc = sy-subrc.
                ROLLBACK WORK.
                MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REM'
                                  'Return code ' w_subrc.
              ELSE.
                PERFORM update_in_rem_log USING w_action.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          ELSE.
* Update Notes/Reminders
            IF gr_outtab10-inspect_emp NE gr_outtab10_old-inspect_emp.
              PERFORM get_note_desc USING '14'
                                          '01'.
* First delete out the reminder for the previous inspector
              IF gr_outtab10_old-inspect_emp IS NOT INITIAL.
                SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_rem
                       WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab10-pernr
                         AND incident = gr_outtab10-incident
                         AND ass_emp  = gr_outtab10_old-inspect_emp
                         AND doc_unique = w_doc_unique
                         AND note_cat = '14'
                         AND business_rule = '01'.
                IF sy-subrc = 0.
                  w_action = 'D'.
                  DELETE zim_in_rem.
                  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
                    w_subrc = sy-subrc.
                    ROLLBACK WORK.
                    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REM'
                                      'Return code ' w_subrc.
                  ELSE.
                    PERFORM update_in_rem_log USING w_action.
                  ENDIF.
                ENDIF.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
* Then add the reminder for the new inspector
            IF gr_outtab10-inspect_emp IS NOT INITIAL.
              WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
              SELECT MAX( seq_no ) FROM zim_in_rem
                     INTO w_seq_no
                     WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab10-pernr
                       AND incident = gr_outtab10-incident
                       AND ass_to   = ' '.
              IF sy-subrc NE 0.
                w_seq_no = 1.
              ELSE.
                ADD 1 TO w_seq_no.
              ENDIF.
              w_action = 'I'.
              CLEAR zim_in_rem.
              zim_in_rem-pernr         = gr_outtab10-pernr.
              zim_in_rem-incident      = gr_outtab10-incident.
              zim_in_rem-seq_no        = w_seq_no.
              zim_in_rem-ass_emp       = gr_outtab10-inspect_emp.
              zim_in_rem-doc_unique    = w_doc_unique.
              zim_in_rem-created_date  = sy-datum.
              zim_in_rem-created_by    = sy-uname.
              zim_in_rem-note_cat      = '14'.
              zim_in_rem-business_rule = '01'.
              zim_in_rem-message_type  = 'A'.
              zim_in_rem-action        = zim_rem-action.
              INSERT zim_in_rem.
              IF sy-subrc NE 0.
                w_subrc = sy-subrc.
                ROLLBACK WORK.
                MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REM'
                                  'Return code ' w_subrc.
              ELSE.
                PERFORM update_in_rem_log USING w_action.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.

          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    gt_outtab10_old[] = gt_outtab10[].
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_ERG_2

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_REM_LIST
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_rem_list .

  gt_outtab17_copy[]  = gt_outtab17[].
  gt_outtab17_copy2[] = gt_outtab17_old[].
  SORT gt_outtab17_copy  BY pernr incident ass_to seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab17_copy2 BY pernr incident ass_to seq_no.
  IF gt_outtab17_copy[] NE gt_outtab17_copy2[].
    LOOP AT gt_outtab17_copy INTO gr_outtab17.
      READ TABLE gt_outtab17_copy2 INTO gr_outtab17_old INDEX sy-tabix.
      IF gr_outtab17-pernr          NE gr_outtab17_old-pernr         OR
         gr_outtab17-incident       NE gr_outtab17_old-incident      OR
         gr_outtab17-ass_to         NE gr_outtab17_old-ass_to        OR
         gr_outtab17-ass_emp        NE gr_outtab17_old-ass_emp       OR
         gr_outtab17-seq_no         NE gr_outtab17_old-seq_no        OR
         gr_outtab17-review_date    NE gr_outtab17_old-review_date   OR
         gr_outtab17-action2        NE gr_outtab17_old-action2       OR
         gr_outtab17-note_comp      NE gr_outtab17_old-note_comp.
        w_action                 = 'U'.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_rem
               WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab17-pernr
                 AND incident = gr_outtab17-incident
                 AND ass_to   = gr_outtab17-ass_to
                 AND seq_no   = gr_outtab17-seq_no.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REM'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ELSE.
          zim_in_rem-note_comp   = gr_outtab17-note_comp.
          zim_in_rem-ass_emp     = gr_outtab17-ass_emp.
          zim_in_rem-review_date = gr_outtab17-review_date.
          zim_in_rem-action2     = gr_outtab17-action2.
          UPDATE zim_in_rem.
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
            w_subrc = sy-subrc.
            ROLLBACK WORK.
            MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REM'
                              'Return code ' w_subrc.
          ELSE.
            PERFORM update_in_rem_log USING w_action.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    gt_outtab17_old[] = gt_outtab17[].
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_REM_LIST

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_CM
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_cm .

  DATA : tb_zim_in_claim         TYPE zim_in_claim,
         l_year(4)               TYPE n,
         l_date                  TYPE d,
         l_start                 TYPE d,
         l_start_last            TYPE d,
         l_yrs                   TYPE i.

  CHECK zim_in_mast-hod IS INITIAL.

* Determine the current medical certificate status
  CLEAR : w_mc_status, tbl_zim_in_cert, l_start.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_cert
         INTO TABLE tbl_zim_in_cert
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND inactive = ' '.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_cap
         APPENDING CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE tbl_zim_in_cert
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND inactive = ' '.
  SORT tbl_zim_in_cert BY mc_date
                          date_from.
  LOOP AT tbl_zim_in_cert.
    IF l_start IS INITIAL.
      l_start    = tbl_zim_in_cert-date_from.
    ENDIF.
    IF w_mc_status = 'P' AND
       tbl_zim_in_cert-status = 'P'.
    ELSE.
      l_start_last = tbl_zim_in_cert-date_from.
    ENDIF.
    w_mc_status  = tbl_zim_in_cert-status.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF w_mc_status = 'P'.
    zim_in_cm-pid_date  = l_start_last.
    IF zim_in_cm-wc_status = 'O' OR
       zim_in_cm-wc_status = ' '.
      zim_in_cm-wc_status = 'P'.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_in_cm-ip_status = 'O' OR
       zim_in_cm-ip_status = ' '.
      zim_in_cm-ip_status = 'P'.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR zim_in_cm-pid_date.
    IF zim_in_cm-wc_status = 'P'.
      zim_in_cm-wc_status = 'O'.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_in_cm-ip_status = 'P'.
      zim_in_cm-ip_status = 'O'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* Get Disablement date, IP dates and day271
  PERFORM get_dis_ip_dates.
*
  IF zim_screen-claim_review IS NOT INITIAL.
    zim_in_cm-claim_review    = zim_screen-claim_review.
    zim_in_cm-claim_review_dt = zim_screen-claim_review_dt.
    CLEAR : zim_screen-claim_review, zim_screen-claim_review_dt.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM update_in_cm_table.

  IF w_in_cm-day_271 NE zim_in_cm-day_271 AND
     zim_in_mast-inc_type NE 'N'.
    PERFORM check_med_cert USING 'I'
                                 zim_in_cm-day_271
                                 '99991231'.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_in_cm-plan_hours_date NE zim_in_cm-plan_hours_date AND
     zim_in_cm-plan_hours_date IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM check_med_cert USING 'P'
                                 zim_in_cm-plan_hours_date
                                 '99991231'.
  ENDIF.


  w_3408_refresh = abap_true.
  w_3409_refresh = abap_true.
  w_3410_refresh = abap_true.
  w_3415_refresh = abap_true.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_CM

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  get_dis_ip_dates

FORM get_dis_ip_dates.
  DATA: lv_dis_date     TYPE datum,

        lv_ip_endda     TYPE datum,
        lv_top_begda    TYPE datum,
        lv_top_endda    TYPE datum,
