        lv_epss_endda_p1   TYPE datum,
        lv_epss_begda_p2   TYPE datum,
        lv_epss_endda_p2   TYPE datum.
  DATA: lt_ip           TYPE TABLE OF zim_in_ip,
        ls_ip           TYPE zim_in_ip.

  PERFORM get_ip_records.
* disablement date
  lv_dis_date = gr_ip_rec->get_disablement_date( ).
  IF lv_dis_date IS NOT INITIAL
    AND lv_dis_date NE zim_in_cm-disablement_date.
    zim_in_cm-disablement_date =  lv_dis_date.
  ENDIF.



  IF w_epss IS INITIAL. " AZORBA EPSS BRD 03

* Top-up start and end dates
    gr_ip_rec->get_topup_dates( IMPORTING ev_begda = lv_top_begda
                                          ev_endda = lv_top_endda ).

* IP start and end dates
    gr_ip_rec->get_ip_dates( IMPORTING ev_begda = lv_ip_begda
                                       ev_endda = lv_ip_endda ).

* if  zim_in_cm-disablement_date is initial.
    IF lv_ip_begda NE zim_in_cm-ip_start_date
      AND lv_ip_begda IS  NOT INITIAL.
      zim_in_cm-ip_start_date = lv_ip_begda.
*  endif.
    ELSE.
      zim_in_cm-day_271 = zim_in_cm-ip_start_date.
    ENDIF.

    IF lv_ip_endda NE zim_in_cm-ip_end_date AND
       lv_ip_endda IS NOT INITIAL.
      zim_in_cm-ip_end_date = lv_ip_endda.
    ENDIF.

*<<< AZORBA EPSS BRD 03
* Implementing logic for EPSS
  ELSE.
* EPSS start and end dates
* Period 1
    gr_ip_rec->get_epss_dates(
      EXPORTING
*        iv_date   =     " Date
        iv_period = '1'    " EPSS Period (1 or 2)
      IMPORTING
        ev_begda  = lv_epss_begda_p1    " Date
        ev_endda  = lv_epss_endda_p1 ).    " Date

    IF lv_epss_begda_p1 NE zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p1
      AND lv_epss_begda_p1 IS  NOT INITIAL.

      zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p1 = lv_epss_begda_p1.

    ELSE.
      zim_in_cm-day_271 = zim_in_cm-ip_start_date.
    ENDIF.

    IF lv_epss_endda_p1 NE zim_in_cm-epss_end_date_p1 AND
       lv_epss_endda_p1 IS NOT INITIAL.

        zim_in_cm-epss_end_date_p1 = lv_epss_endda_p1.
      ENDIF.

    gr_ip_rec->get_epss_dates(
      EXPORTING

        iv_period = '2'    " EPSS Period (1 or 2)
      IMPORTING
        ev_begda  = lv_epss_begda_p2    " Date
        ev_endda  = lv_epss_endda_p2 ).    " Date

    IF lv_epss_begda_p2 NE zim_in_cm-epss_init_start_date_p2
      AND lv_epss_begda_p2 IS  NOT INITIAL.
      IF zim_in_cm-epss_init_start_date_p2 IS INITIAL.
        zim_in_cm-epss_init_start_date_p2 = lv_epss_begda_p2.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      zim_in_cm-day_271 = zim_in_cm-ip_start_date.
    ENDIF.

    IF lv_epss_endda_p2 NE zim_in_cm-epss_init_end_date_p2 AND
       lv_epss_endda_p2 IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF zim_in_cm-epss_init_end_date_p2 IS INITIAL.
        zim_in_cm-epss_init_end_date_p2 = lv_epss_endda_p2.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.  "IF w_epss IS INITIAL.

  IF zim_in_cm-day_271 IS INITIAL.
    zim_in_cm-day_271 = zim_in_cm-ip_start_date.
  ENDIF.


* set the top-up end date.
  IF lv_top_endda NE zim_in_cm-topup_end_date
    AND lv_top_endda IS  NOT INITIAL.
    zim_in_cm-topup_end_date = lv_top_endda.
*  endif.
  ENDIF.

* day 271 after disablement date
  IF lv_dis_date IS NOT INITIAL.


*MD
*tvermeulen
*Change 3
*In Case Details tab in WC, 'Day 274' to be replaced with 'IP Start Date'
*using same logic as on the Case Details tab in IP. This date is based on the
*date record referred to in Change 2 - 'IP Claim Details'.
*    lt_ip = gr_ip_rec->get_ip_ip( ).
*    loop at lt_ip into ls_ip.
*      zim_in_cm-day_271 = ls_ip-begda.
*    endloop.

*    zim_in_cm-day_271 = zcl_olims_config=>get_topup_period_date( lv_dis_date ) + 1.
  ENDIF.

* Functionality to update the dates in the ZIM_IN_CM table when the record is
* first displayed.

  IF w_ip_dates_create IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR w_ip_dates_create.

    PERFORM update_in_cm_table.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    "get_dis_ip_dates
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_DISABLEMENT_DATE
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_disablement_date .

  DATA: tb_zim_in_cm            TYPE zim_in_cm,
         tb_zim_in_claim         TYPE zim_in_claim,
         l_year(4)               TYPE n,
         l_date                  TYPE d,
         l_start                 TYPE d,
         l_start_last            TYPE d,
         l_yrs                   TYPE i.


  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm
         INTO tb_zim_in_cm
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Save the Case Management Details before'
                      'changing the Date of Disablement'.
  ELSE.
    w_old_disablement_date        = tb_zim_in_cm-disablement_date.
    w_old_day_271                 = zim_in_cm-day_271.
    zim_in_cm-disablement_date    = w_new_disablement_date.
*<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03 - Handling new record type EPSS
    IF w_epss = abap_true.
      PERFORM calculate_epss_ip_dates.
      tb_zim_in_cm-epss_init_start_date_p2    = zim_in_cm-epss_init_start_date_p2. "EPSS Phase 4
      tb_zim_in_cm-epss_init_end_date_p2     = zim_in_cm-epss_init_end_date_p2. "EPSS Phase 4
    ELSE.
*>>> AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03 - Handling new record type EPSS
      PERFORM calculate_ip_dates.
    ENDIF.
    tb_zim_in_cm-disablement_date = zim_in_cm-disablement_date.
    tb_zim_in_cm-ip_start_date    = zim_in_cm-ip_start_date.
    tb_zim_in_cm-ip_end_date      = zim_in_cm-ip_end_date.
    tb_zim_in_cm-day_271          = zim_in_cm-day_271.
    UPDATE zim_in_cm FROM tb_zim_in_cm.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_CM'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_cm_log USING w_action.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
         WHERE ima = w_user_pernr.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_advisor.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM create_reminder USING 'C'
                                zim_advisor-sima
                                '80'
                                '13'
                                ' '
                                '00000000'.

* Determine if any medical certificates exist for the period
* that could result in changed WC Recoup Benefit records
* A split in IT14 records must be created on Day 271 to ensure
* the top-up is calculated correctly.
  IF zim_in_mast-inc_type NE 'N'.
    IF w_old_day_271 LT zim_in_cm-day_271.
      l_date = w_old_day_271.
    ELSE.
      l_date = zim_in_cm-day_271.
    ENDIF.
    PERFORM check_med_cert USING 'I'
                                 l_date
                                 '99991231'.
    w_3409_refresh = abap_true.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_DISABLEMENT_DATE

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_CLAIM_STATUS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_claim_status .

  DATA: rec_zim_in_claim   TYPE zim_in_claim,
         l_begda            TYPE d,
         l_endda            TYPE d,
         l_change_stat(1)   TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique       TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  IF zim_in_claim-stat IS INITIAL.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR zim_in_claim-sent_date.
  CLEAR l_change_stat.
  IF w_claim_type = 'W'.
    IF zim_in_claim-stat = '5' AND
       w_ins-claim_stats NE '5'.
      l_change_stat = 'E'.
    ELSEIF zim_in_claim-stat = '7' AND
       w_ins-claim_stats NE '7'.
      l_change_stat = 'D'.
    ELSEIF zim_in_claim-stat = '4' AND
       w_ins-claim_stats NE '4'.
      l_change_stat = 'A'.
    ELSEIF zim_in_claim-stat = '8' AND
       w_ins-claim_stats NE '8'.
      l_change_stat = 'F'.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_in_claim-begda LE sy-datum AND
       zim_in_claim-endda GE sy-datum.
      w_ins-claim_stats = zim_in_claim-stat.
      IF w_ins-claim_sdate IS INITIAL.
        w_ins-claim_sdate = zim_in_claim-begda.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM save_claim_details.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_in_claim-stat NE '2' AND
       zim_in_claim-stat NE '4' AND
       zim_in_claim-stat NE '6'.
      PERFORM delete_it0014 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                  zim_in_mast-incident
                                  zim_in_claim-begda
                                  zim_in_claim-endda.
    ENDIF.
  ELSEIF w_claim_type = 'I'.
    IF zim_in_claim-stat = 'C' OR      " Closed
       zim_in_claim-stat = 'D' OR      " Declined
       zim_in_claim-stat = 'F'.        " Finalised/End of Matter
      zim_in_cm-ip_status     = 'C'.
      zim_in_cm-ip_close_date = sy-datum.
    ELSE.
      zim_in_cm-ip_status = zim_in_claim-stat.
      CLEAR : zim_in_cm-ip_close_date.
    ENDIF.
  ELSEIF w_claim_type = 'H'.
    IF zim_in_claim-begda LE sy-datum AND
       zim_in_claim-endda GE sy-datum.
      w_ins-claim_stats = zim_in_claim-stat.
      IF w_ins-claim_sdate IS INITIAL.
        w_ins-claim_sdate = zim_in_claim-begda.
      ENDIF.
      IF zim_in_claim-stat = '8'.
        zim_in_pre88-current_status = 'F'.
        zim_in_pre88-closed_date    = sy-datum.
        zim_in_pre88-status_date    = sy-datum.
      ELSEIF zim_in_claim-stat = '2' OR  " Provisional Liability
             zim_in_claim-stat = '4' OR  " Accepted
             zim_in_claim-stat = '7'.    " Declined
        zim_in_pre88-liab_dec_date  = sy-datum.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM save_claim_details.
    ENDIF.

*<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03
  ELSEIF w_claim_type = 'E' OR w_claim_type = 'F'.
    IF zim_in_claim-stat = 'C' OR      " Closed
       zim_in_claim-stat = 'D' OR      " Declined
       zim_in_claim-stat = 'F'.        " Finalised/End of Matter
      zim_in_cm-ip_status     = 'C'.
      zim_in_cm-ip_close_date = sy-datum.
    ELSE.
      zim_in_cm-ip_status = zim_in_claim-stat.
      CLEAR : zim_in_cm-ip_close_date.
    ENDIF.
  ELSEIF w_claim_type = 'H'.
    IF zim_in_claim-begda LE sy-datum AND
       zim_in_claim-endda GE sy-datum.
      w_ins-claim_stats = zim_in_claim-stat.
      IF w_ins-claim_sdate IS INITIAL.
        w_ins-claim_sdate = zim_in_claim-begda.
      ENDIF.
      IF zim_in_claim-stat = '8'.
        zim_in_pre88-current_status = 'F'.
        zim_in_pre88-closed_date    = sy-datum.
        zim_in_pre88-status_date    = sy-datum.
      ELSEIF zim_in_claim-stat = '2' OR  " Provisional Liability
             zim_in_claim-stat = '4' OR  " Accepted
             zim_in_claim-stat = '7'.    " Declined
        zim_in_pre88-liab_dec_date  = sy-datum.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM save_claim_details.
    ENDIF.
*>>> AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03
  ENDIF.

* Determine if an existing record is to be overwritten
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_claim
         INTO rec_zim_in_claim
         WHERE pernr          = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident       = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND claim_type     = w_claim_type
           AND begda          = zim_in_claim-begda
           AND endda          = zim_in_claim-endda.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    w_action                = 'U'.
    zim_in_claim-pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    zim_in_claim-incident   = zim_in_mast-incident.
    zim_in_claim-seq_no     = rec_zim_in_claim-seq_no.
    zim_in_claim-claim_type = w_claim_type.
    zim_in_claim-date_updated = sy-datum.
    UPDATE zim_in_claim.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_CLAIM'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_claim_log USING w_action.
    ENDIF.
* Determine if any medical certificates exist for the period
* that could result in changed WC Recoup Benefit records
    IF w_claim_type = 'W'.
      PERFORM check_med_cert USING 'C'
                                   zim_in_claim-begda
                                   zim_in_claim-endda.
    ENDIF.
    w_3404_refresh = abap_true.
    w_2209_refresh = abap_true.
    w_3602_refresh = abap_true.
    w_4102_refresh = abap_true.
    CLEAR zim_in_claim.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

* Add the screen record
  w_action                = 'I'.
  zim_in_claim-pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr.
  zim_in_claim-incident   = zim_in_mast-incident.
  zim_in_claim-claim_type = w_claim_type.
  zim_in_claim-seq_no     = w_next_cs_seq_no.
  zim_in_claim-date_updated = sy-datum.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_cs_seq_no.
  INSERT zim_in_claim.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    w_subrc = sy-subrc.
    ROLLBACK WORK.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_CLAIM'
                      'Return code ' w_subrc.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM update_in_claim_log USING w_action.
  ENDIF.

* Delimit any existing records
  l_begda = zim_in_claim-begda.
  l_endda = zim_in_claim-endda.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab30 INTO gr_outtab30
       WHERE begda LE l_endda
         AND endda GE l_begda.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING gr_outtab30 TO zim_in_claim.
    IF gr_outtab30-begda GE l_begda AND
       gr_outtab30-endda LE l_endda.
      w_action           = 'D'.
      DELETE zim_in_claim.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_CLAIM'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_in_claim_log USING w_action.
      ENDIF.
      CONCATENATE 'CS' w_claim_type gr_outtab30-seq_no
                  INTO l_doc_unique.
      PERFORM call_delete_unique USING gr_outtab30-doc_unique.
    ELSE.
      w_action           = 'D'.
      DELETE zim_in_claim.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_CLAIM'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_in_claim_log USING w_action.
      ENDIF.
      w_action           = 'I'.
      IF gr_outtab30-begda LT l_begda.
        zim_in_claim-endda = l_begda - 1.
      ELSE.
        zim_in_claim-begda = l_endda + 1.
      ENDIF.
      INSERT zim_in_claim.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_CLAIM'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_in_claim_log USING w_action.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_outtab30-begda LT l_begda AND
         gr_outtab30-endda GT l_endda.
        w_action           = 'I'.
        zim_in_claim-begda = l_endda + 1.
        zim_in_claim-endda = gr_outtab30-endda.
        CLEAR zim_in_claim-sent_date.
        INSERT zim_in_claim.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_CLAIM'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM update_in_claim_log USING w_action.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

* Determine if any medical certificates exist for the period
* that could result in changed WC Recoup Benefit records
  IF w_claim_type = 'W'.
    PERFORM check_med_cert USING 'C'
                                 l_begda
                                 l_endda.
  ENDIF.

  w_3404_refresh = abap_true.
  w_2209_refresh = abap_true.
  w_3602_refresh = abap_true.
  w_4102_refresh = abap_true.
  CLEAR zim_in_claim.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_CLAIM_STATUS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_REQ_REVIEW
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_req_review .

  DATA: rec_zim_in_req_rev  TYPE zim_in_req_rev,
         l_begda            TYPE d,
         l_endda            TYPE d,
         l_change_stat(1)   TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique       TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  IF w_review_type IS INITIAL OR
     zim_in_req_rev-begda IS INITIAL OR
     zim_in_req_rev-endda IS INITIAL.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

* Determine if an existing record is to be overwritten
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_req_rev
         INTO rec_zim_in_req_rev
         WHERE pernr          = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident       = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND review_type     = w_review_type
           AND begda          = zim_in_req_rev-begda
           AND endda          = zim_in_req_rev-endda.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    w_action                = 'U'.
    zim_in_req_rev-pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    zim_in_req_rev-incident   = zim_in_mast-incident.
    zim_in_req_rev-seq_no     = rec_zim_in_req_rev-seq_no.
    zim_in_req_rev-review_type = w_review_type.
    zim_in_req_rev-date_updated = sy-datum.
    zim_in_req_rev-user_processed = sy-uname.
    UPDATE zim_in_req_rev.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REQ_REV'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_req_rev_log USING w_action.
    ENDIF.

    w_4103_refresh = abap_true.
    CLEAR zim_in_req_rev.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

* Add the screen record
  w_action                = 'I'.
  zim_in_req_rev-pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr.
  zim_in_req_rev-incident   = zim_in_mast-incident.
  zim_in_req_rev-review_type = w_review_type.
  zim_in_req_rev-seq_no     = w_next_req_seq_no.
  zim_in_req_rev-date_updated = sy-datum.
  zim_in_req_rev-user_processed = sy-uname.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_req_seq_no.
  INSERT zim_in_req_rev.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    w_subrc = sy-subrc.
    ROLLBACK WORK.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REQ_REV'
                      'Return code ' w_subrc.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM update_in_req_rev_log USING w_action.
  ENDIF.

  w_4103_refresh = abap_true.
  CLEAR zim_in_req_rev.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_REQ_REVIEW

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_EPSS_CLAIM_ASSIGNMENT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_epss_claim_assignment .

  DATA: lt_epss_claim_h  TYPE TABLE OF zim_epss_claim_h.
  DATA: ls_epss_claim_h  TYPE zim_epss_claim_h.

  zim_epss_claim_h-examiner_id = zim_in_cm-epss_examiner_id.
  zim_epss_claim_h-supervisor = zim_in_cm-epss_supervisor.

  REFRESH lt_epss_claim_h.
  SELECT * FROM zim_epss_claim_h INTO TABLE lt_epss_claim_h
    WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
      AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

  IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
    " Insert only if record has changed from last
    SORT lt_epss_claim_h BY upd_date DESCENDING upd_time DESCENDING.
    READ TABLE lt_epss_claim_h INTO ls_epss_claim_h INDEX 1.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF ls_epss_claim_h-examiner_id NE zim_in_cm-epss_examiner_id
        OR ls_epss_claim_h-supervisor NE zim_in_cm-epss_supervisor.
        zim_epss_claim_h-pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr.
        zim_epss_claim_h-incident   = zim_in_mast-incident.
        zim_epss_claim_h-seq_no     = w_next_adv_seq_no.
        zim_epss_claim_h-upd_date = sy-datum.
        zim_epss_claim_h-upd_time = sy-uzeit.
        zim_epss_claim_h-upd_user = sy-uname.
        ADD 1 TO w_next_adv_seq_no.

        INSERT zim_epss_claim_h.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_EPSS_CLAIM_H'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ENDIF.

        w_4101_refresh = abap_true.
        CLEAR zim_epss_claim_h.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    zim_epss_claim_h-pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    zim_epss_claim_h-incident   = zim_in_mast-incident.
    zim_epss_claim_h-seq_no     = w_next_adv_seq_no.
    zim_epss_claim_h-upd_date = sy-datum.
    zim_epss_claim_h-upd_time = sy-uzeit.
    zim_epss_claim_h-upd_user = sy-uname.
    ADD 1 TO w_next_adv_seq_no.

    INSERT zim_epss_claim_h.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_EPSS_CLAIM_H'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ENDIF.

    w_4101_refresh = abap_true.
    CLEAR zim_epss_claim_h.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_EPSS_CLAIM_ASSIGNMENT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_FIXED_AMOUNT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_fixed_amount .

  DATA: rec_zim_in_rec_amt TYPE zim_in_rec_amt,
         l_begda            TYPE d,
         l_endda            TYPE d,
         l_doc_unique       TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique,
         l_amount           TYPE zim_in_rec_amt-amount,
         l_ext_earnings     TYPE zim_in_rec_amt-ext_earnings,
         lv_betrg           TYPE pa0014-betrg,
         lv_lgart           TYPE lgart.

  IF zim_in_rec_amt-begda       IS INITIAL.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

* May also need to maintain corresponding P0014 InfoType records,
* in order to make the fixed values available to payroll.
* Determine the corresponding Wage Type.
  CASE w_amount_type.
    WHEN 'E'.           "External Earnings
      IF w_unsworn_claim EQ abap_true.
        lv_lgart =
               zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_ext_earnings.
        lv_betrg = zim_in_rec_amt-ext_earnings.
      ENDIF.
    WHEN 'W'.           "Work Capacity Decision
      IF w_unsworn_claim EQ abap_true.
        lv_lgart =
               zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_work_cap_decn.
        lv_betrg = zim_in_rec_amt-amount.
      ENDIF.
    WHEN OTHERS.
  ENDCASE.

* Also maintain corresponding P0014 InfoType.
  IF lv_lgart IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL FUNCTION 'ZHWC_MAINT_P0014_PER_INCIDENT'
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr        = zim_in_mast-pernr
        i_incident     = zim_in_mast-incident
        i_wclmn        = w_ins-claim_no
        i_begda        = zim_in_rec_amt-begda
        i_endda        = zim_in_rec_amt-endda
        i_lgart        = lv_lgart
        i_betrg        = lv_betrg
      EXCEPTIONS
        enqueue_failed = 1
        update_failed  = 2
        OTHERS         = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating P0014'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* Determine if an existing record is to be overwritten
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_rec_amt
         INTO rec_zim_in_rec_amt
         WHERE pernr          = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident       = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND amount_type    = w_amount_type
           AND begda          = zim_in_rec_amt-begda
           AND endda          = zim_in_rec_amt-endda.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    w_action                   = 'U'.
    zim_in_rec_amt-pernr       = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    zim_in_rec_amt-incident    = zim_in_mast-incident.
    zim_in_rec_amt-seq_no      = rec_zim_in_rec_amt-seq_no.
    zim_in_rec_amt-amount_type = w_amount_type.
    UPDATE zim_in_rec_amt.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REC_AMT'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_rec_amt_log USING w_action.
    ENDIF.
    PERFORM check_med_cert USING 'F'
                                 zim_in_rec_amt-begda
                                 zim_in_rec_amt-endda.
    w_3405_refresh = abap_true.
    CLEAR zim_in_rec_amt.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  l_begda        = zim_in_rec_amt-begda.
  l_endda        = zim_in_rec_amt-endda.
  l_amount       = zim_in_rec_amt-amount.
  l_ext_earnings = zim_in_rec_amt-ext_earnings.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_rec_amt
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab32a
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND begda   LE l_endda
           AND endda   GE l_begda.

* Delimit any existing records
  LOOP AT gt_outtab32a INTO gr_outtab32
       WHERE begda LE l_endda
         AND endda GE l_begda.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING gr_outtab32 TO zim_in_rec_amt.
    IF gr_outtab32-begda GE l_begda AND
       gr_outtab32-endda LE l_endda.
      w_action           = 'D'.
      DELETE zim_in_rec_amt.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REC_AMT'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_in_rec_amt_log USING w_action.
      ENDIF.
      CONCATENATE 'FIX' w_amount_type gr_outtab32-seq_no
                  INTO l_doc_unique.
      PERFORM call_delete_unique USING l_doc_unique.
    ELSE.
      w_action           = 'D'.
      DELETE zim_in_rec_amt.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REC_AMT'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_in_rec_amt_log USING w_action.
      ENDIF.
      w_action           = 'I'.
      IF gr_outtab32-begda LT l_begda.
        zim_in_rec_amt-endda = l_begda - 1.
      ELSE.
        zim_in_rec_amt-begda = l_endda + 1.
      ENDIF.
      INSERT zim_in_rec_amt.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REC_AMT'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_in_rec_amt_log USING w_action.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_outtab32-begda LT l_begda AND
         gr_outtab32-endda GT l_endda.
        w_action           = 'I'.
        zim_in_rec_amt-begda = l_endda + 1.
        zim_in_rec_amt-endda = gr_outtab32-endda.
        INSERT zim_in_rec_amt.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REC_AMT'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM update_in_rec_amt_log USING w_action.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.

* Add the screen record
  w_action                    = 'I'.
  zim_in_rec_amt-pernr        = zim_in_mast-pernr.
  zim_in_rec_amt-incident     = zim_in_mast-incident.
  zim_in_rec_amt-amount_type  = w_amount_type.
  zim_in_rec_amt-begda        = l_begda.
  zim_in_rec_amt-endda        = l_endda.
  zim_in_rec_amt-amount       = l_amount.
  zim_in_rec_amt-ext_earnings = l_ext_earnings.
* IF w_amount_type NE 'E'.
  zim_in_rec_amt-seq_no      = w_next_fix_seq_no.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_fix_seq_no.
  INSERT zim_in_rec_amt.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    w_subrc = sy-subrc.
    ROLLBACK WORK.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_REC_AMT'
                      'Return code ' w_subrc.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM update_in_rec_amt_log USING w_action.
  ENDIF.
* ELSE.
*   PERFORM split_external_amount USING l_begda
*                                       l_endda.
* ENDIF.

* Determine if any medical certificates exist for the period
* that could result in changed WC Recoup Benefit records
  PERFORM check_med_cert USING 'F'
                               l_begda
                               l_endda.

  w_3405_refresh = abap_true.
  CLEAR zim_in_rec_amt.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_FIXED_AMOUNT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_COMPARABLE_EARNINGS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_comparable_earnings .

  DATA: rec_zim_in_comp    TYPE zim_in_comp,
         l_begda            TYPE d,
         l_endda            TYPE d,
         l_doc_unique       TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique,
         l_amount           TYPE zim_in_comp-amount.

  IF zim_in_comp-amount       IS INITIAL.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.


* Determine if an existing record is to be overwritten
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_comp
         INTO rec_zim_in_comp
         WHERE pernr          = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident       = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND begda          = zim_in_comp-begda
           AND endda          = zim_in_comp-endda.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    w_action                = 'U'.
    zim_in_comp-pernr       = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    zim_in_comp-incident    = zim_in_mast-incident.
    zim_in_comp-seq_no      = rec_zim_in_comp-seq_no.
    UPDATE zim_in_comp.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_COMP'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_comp_log USING w_action.
    ENDIF.
    PERFORM check_med_cert USING 'E'
                                 zim_in_comp-begda
                                 zim_in_comp-endda.
    w_3411_refresh = abap_true.
    CLEAR zim_in_comp.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  l_begda        = zim_in_comp-begda.
  l_endda        = zim_in_comp-endda.
  l_amount       = zim_in_comp-amount.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_comp
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab37a
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND begda   LE l_endda
           AND endda   GE l_begda.

* Delimit any existing records
  LOOP AT gt_outtab37a INTO gr_outtab37
       WHERE begda LE l_endda
         AND endda GE l_begda.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING gr_outtab37 TO zim_in_comp.
    IF gr_outtab37-begda GE l_begda AND
       gr_outtab37-endda LE l_endda.
      w_action           = 'D'.
      DELETE zim_in_comp.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_COMP'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_in_comp_log USING w_action.
      ENDIF.
      CONCATENATE 'COMPEARN' gr_outtab37-seq_no
                  INTO l_doc_unique.
      PERFORM call_delete_unique USING l_doc_unique.
    ELSE.
      w_action           = 'D'.
      DELETE zim_in_comp.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_COMP'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_in_comp_log USING w_action.
      ENDIF.
      w_action           = 'I'.
      IF gr_outtab37-begda LT l_begda.
        zim_in_comp-endda = l_begda - 1.
      ELSE.
        zim_in_comp-begda = l_endda + 1.
      ENDIF.
      INSERT zim_in_comp.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_COMP'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_in_comp_log USING w_action.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_outtab37-begda LT l_begda AND
         gr_outtab37-endda GT l_endda.
        w_action          = 'I'.
        zim_in_comp-begda = l_endda + 1.
        zim_in_comp-endda = gr_outtab37-endda.
        INSERT zim_in_comp.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_COMP'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM update_in_comp_log USING w_action.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.

* Add the screen record
  w_action                 = 'I'.
  zim_in_comp-pernr        = zim_in_mast-pernr.
  zim_in_comp-incident     = zim_in_mast-incident.
  zim_in_comp-begda        = l_begda.
  zim_in_comp-endda        = l_endda.
  zim_in_comp-amount       = l_amount.
  zim_in_comp-seq_no       = w_next_comp_seq_no.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_comp_seq_no.
  INSERT zim_in_comp.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    w_subrc = sy-subrc.
    ROLLBACK WORK.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_COMP'
                      'Return code ' w_subrc.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM update_in_comp_log USING w_action.
  ENDIF.

* Determine if any medical certificates exist for the period
* that could result in changed WC Recoup Benefit records
  PERFORM check_med_cert USING 'E'
                               l_begda
                               l_endda.

  w_3411_refresh = abap_true.
  CLEAR zim_in_comp.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_COMPARABLE_EARNINGS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_PIAWE_DETAILS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_piawe_details.

  DATA:
    lv_amount                  TYPE c LENGTH 10,
    lv_begda                   TYPE c LENGTH 10,
    lv_endda                   TYPE c LENGTH 10,
    lv_incident                TYPE c LENGTH 10.

* Pre 52 Week PIAWE.
  IF w_piawe_det-piawe_pre_52_wk NE w_piawe_det_old-piawe_pre_52_wk OR
     w_piawe_det-begda_pre_52_wk NE w_piawe_det_old-begda_pre_52_wk OR
     w_piawe_det-endda_pre_52_wk NE w_piawe_det_old-endda_pre_52_wk.
    CALL FUNCTION 'ZHWC_MAINT_P0014_PER_INCIDENT'
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr        = zim_in_mast-pernr
        i_incident     = zim_in_mast-incident
        i_wclmn        = w_ins-claim_no
        i_begda        = w_piawe_det-begda_pre_52_wk
        i_endda        = w_piawe_det-endda_pre_52_wk
        i_lgart        = zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_piawe_pre_52
        i_preas        = zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_preas-manual_piawe
        i_betrg        = w_piawe_det-piawe_pre_52_wk
      EXCEPTIONS
        enqueue_failed = 1
        update_failed  = 2
        OTHERS         = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
              WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
    ENDIF.
    w_3412_refresh = abap_true.
  ENDIF.

* Post 52 Week PIAWE.
  IF w_piawe_det-piawe_post_52_wk NE
                          w_piawe_det_old-piawe_post_52_wk OR
     w_piawe_det-begda_post_52_wk NE
                          w_piawe_det_old-begda_post_52_wk OR
     w_piawe_det-endda_post_52_wk NE
                          w_piawe_det_old-endda_post_52_wk.
    CALL FUNCTION 'ZHWC_MAINT_P0014_PER_INCIDENT'
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr        = zim_in_mast-pernr
        i_incident     = zim_in_mast-incident
        i_wclmn        = w_ins-claim_no
        i_begda        = w_piawe_det-begda_post_52_wk
        i_endda        = w_piawe_det-endda_post_52_wk
        i_lgart        = zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_piawe_post_52
        i_preas        = zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_preas-manual_piawe
        i_betrg        = w_piawe_det-piawe_post_52_wk
      EXCEPTIONS
        enqueue_failed = 1
        update_failed  = 2
        OTHERS         = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
              WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
    ENDIF.
    w_3412_refresh = abap_true.
  ENDIF.

* New 52 Week PIAWE.
  IF w_piawe_det-piawe_new_52_wk NE
                          w_piawe_det_old-piawe_new_52_wk OR
     w_piawe_det-begda_new_52_wk NE
                          w_piawe_det_old-begda_new_52_wk OR
     w_piawe_det-endda_new_52_wk NE
                          w_piawe_det_old-endda_new_52_wk.
    CALL FUNCTION 'ZHWC_MAINT_P0014_PER_INCIDENT'
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr        = zim_in_mast-pernr
        i_incident     = zim_in_mast-incident
        i_wclmn        = w_ins-claim_no
        i_begda        = w_piawe_det-begda_new_52_wk
        i_endda        = w_piawe_det-endda_new_52_wk
        i_lgart        = zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_piawe_new
        i_preas        = zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_preas-manual_piawe
        i_betrg        = w_piawe_det-piawe_new_52_wk
      EXCEPTIONS
        enqueue_failed = 1
        update_failed  = 2
        OTHERS         = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
              WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
    ENDIF.
    w_3412_refresh = abap_true.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_PIAWE_DETAILS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_PRIMARY_CLAIM_DETAILS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_primary_claim_details.

*  DATA:
*    lo_provide_exception       TYPE REF TO cx_sy_provide_exception.
*
*  DATA:
*    lt_outtab47_old            LIKE gt_outtab47,
*    lt_prim_cec                TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zim_in_prim,
*    lt_prim_new                TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zim_in_prim,
*    lt_prim_pc                 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zim_in_prim,
*    lt_prim_temp               TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zim_in_prim.
*
*  DATA:
*    ls_outtab47                TYPE gs_outtab_type47,
*    ls_prim_cec                TYPE zim_in_prim,
*    ls_prim_new                TYPE zim_in_prim,
*    ls_prim_pc                 TYPE zim_in_prim,
*    ls_prim_temp               TYPE zim_in_prim.
*
*  DATA:
*    lv_error_text              TYPE string,
*    lv_valid_outtab47          TYPE boole_d,
*    lv_valid_prim_cec          TYPE boole_d,
*    lv_valid_prim_pc           TYPE boole_d,
*    lv_valid_prim_temp         TYPE boole_d.
*
*  lt_outtab47_old[] = gt_outtab47[].
*
*  SORT lt_outtab47_old BY begda endda.
*
** Build a table for entered Concurrent External Claim.
*  IF w_prim_det-conc_ext_claim NE w_prim_det_old-conc_ext_claim OR
*     w_prim_det-begda_cec      NE w_prim_det_old-begda_cec OR
*     w_prim_det-endda_cec      NE w_prim_det_old-endda_cec.
*    IF w_prim_det-begda_cec IS NOT INITIAL AND
*       w_prim_det-endda_cec IS NOT INITIAL.
*      CLEAR ls_prim_cec.
*      ls_prim_cec-pernr          = zim_in_mast-pernr.
*      ls_prim_cec-incident       = zim_in_mast-incident.
*      ls_prim_cec-begda          = w_prim_det-begda_cec.
*      ls_prim_cec-endda          = w_prim_det-endda_cec.
*      ls_prim_cec-conc_ext_claim = w_prim_det-conc_ext_claim.
*      APPEND ls_prim_cec TO lt_prim_cec.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDIF.
*
** Build a table for entered Primary Claim.
*  IF w_prim_det-prim_claim NE w_prim_det_old-prim_claim OR
*     w_prim_det-begda_pc   NE w_prim_det_old-begda_pc OR
*     w_prim_det-endda_pc   NE w_prim_det_old-endda_pc.
*    IF w_prim_det-begda_pc IS NOT INITIAL AND
*       w_prim_det-endda_pc IS NOT INITIAL.
*      CLEAR ls_prim_pc.
*      ls_prim_pc-pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr.
*      ls_prim_pc-incident   = zim_in_mast-incident.
*      ls_prim_pc-begda      = w_prim_det-begda_pc.
*      ls_prim_pc-endda      = w_prim_det-endda_pc.
*      ls_prim_pc-prim_claim = w_prim_det-prim_claim.
*      APPEND ls_prim_pc TO lt_prim_pc.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDIF.
*
** Build new date intervals for Primary and Concurrent External Claim
** into local table LT_PRIM_TEMP.
*  TRY.
*      PROVIDE FIELDS pernr incident prim_claim conc_ext_claim
*                FROM lt_outtab47_old
*                INTO ls_outtab47
*                VALID lv_valid_outtab47
*                BOUNDS begda AND endda
*              FIELDS pernr incident conc_ext_claim
*                FROM lt_prim_cec
*                INTO ls_prim_cec
*                VALID lv_valid_prim_cec
*                BOUNDS begda AND endda
*              FIELDS pernr incident prim_claim
*                FROM lt_prim_pc
*                INTO ls_prim_pc
*                VALID lv_valid_prim_pc
*                BOUNDS begda AND endda
*              BETWEEN '00000000' AND '99991231'.
*        CLEAR ls_prim_temp.
*        IF lv_valid_outtab47 EQ abap_true.
*          MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_outtab47 TO ls_prim_temp.
*        ELSEIF lv_valid_prim_cec EQ abap_true.
*          ls_prim_temp = ls_prim_cec.
*        ELSEIF lv_valid_prim_pc EQ abap_true.
*          ls_prim_temp = ls_prim_pc.
*        ELSE.
*          CONTINUE.
*        ENDIF.
*        IF lv_valid_prim_cec EQ abap_true.
*          ls_prim_temp-conc_ext_claim = ls_prim_cec-conc_ext_claim.
*        ENDIF.
*        IF lv_valid_prim_pc EQ abap_true.
*          ls_prim_temp-prim_claim = ls_prim_pc-prim_claim.
*        ENDIF.
*        APPEND ls_prim_temp TO lt_prim_temp.
*      ENDPROVIDE.
*    CATCH cx_sy_provide_exception INTO lo_provide_exception.
*      ROLLBACK WORK.
*      lv_error_text = lo_provide_exception->get_text( ).
*      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred in PROVIDE for ZIM_IN_PRIM'
*                        lv_error_text.
*  ENDTRY.
*
** Combine the date intervals where possible.
*  TRY.
*      PROVIDE FIELDS pernr incident prim_claim conc_ext_claim
*                FROM lt_prim_temp
*                INTO ls_prim_temp
*                VALID lv_valid_prim_temp
*                BOUNDS begda AND endda
*              BETWEEN '00000000' AND '99991231'.
*        APPEND ls_prim_temp TO lt_prim_new.
*      ENDPROVIDE.
*    CATCH cx_sy_provide_exception INTO lo_provide_exception.
*      ROLLBACK WORK.
*      lv_error_text = lo_provide_exception->get_text( ).
*      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred in PROVIDE for ZIM_IN_PRIM'
*                        lv_error_text.
*  ENDTRY.
*
** Compare the old and new data tables to determine which records need
** to be deleted, inserted or updated.
*  LOOP AT lt_prim_new INTO ls_prim_new
*          WHERE prim_claim     EQ abap_true OR
*                conc_ext_claim EQ abap_true.
*    READ TABLE lt_outtab47_old INTO ls_outtab47
*               WITH KEY begda = ls_prim_new-begda
*                        endda = ls_prim_new-endda.
*    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*      DELETE lt_outtab47_old INDEX sy-tabix.
*      IF ls_prim_new-prim_claim     NE ls_outtab47-prim_claim OR
*         ls_prim_new-conc_ext_claim NE ls_outtab47-conc_ext_claim.
*        UPDATE zim_in_prim
*               FROM ls_prim_new.
*        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
*          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
*          ROLLBACK WORK.
*          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_PRIM'
*                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
*        ELSE.
*          PERFORM update_in_prim_log USING 'U'
*                                           ls_prim_new.
*        ENDIF.
*      ENDIF.
*    ELSE.
*      INSERT zim_in_prim
*             FROM ls_prim_new.
*      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
*        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
*        ROLLBACK WORK.
*        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when inserting ZIM_IN_PRIM'
*                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
*      ELSE.
*        PERFORM update_in_prim_log USING 'I'
*                                         ls_prim_new.
*      ENDIF.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDLOOP.
** Anything remaining in LT_OUTTAB47_OLD needs to be deleted.
*  LOOP AT lt_outtab47_old INTO ls_outtab47.
*    MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_outtab47 TO ls_prim_temp.
*    DELETE zim_in_prim
*           FROM ls_prim_temp.
*    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
*      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
*      ROLLBACK WORK.
*      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when deleting ZIM_IN_PRIM'
*                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
*    ELSE.
*      PERFORM update_in_prim_log USING 'D'
*                                       ls_prim_temp.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDLOOP.
*
*  w_3413_refresh = abap_true.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_PRIMARY_CLAIM_DETAILS

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  save_concurrent_claim
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_concurrent_claim.
  DATA: ls_cec_claim      TYPE zim_in_prim,
        ls_prim_log       TYPE zim_in_prim_log,
        lv_seq_no         TYPE zim_in_prim-seq_no,
        lv_updkz          TYPE updkz.

  IF w_cec_claim IS INITIAL AND               "Insert new record
     w_prim_det-conc_ext_claim = abap_true.

    SELECT MAX( seq_no ) FROM zim_in_prim
      INTO lv_seq_no
      WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
        AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR lv_seq_no.
    ENDIF.
    lv_seq_no = lv_seq_no + 1.
    ls_cec_claim-pernr  = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    ls_cec_claim-incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
    ls_cec_claim-seq_no = lv_seq_no.
    ls_cec_claim-begda  = w_prim_det-begda_cec.
    ls_cec_claim-endda  = w_prim_det-endda_cec.
    ls_cec_claim-conc_ext_claim = abap_true.
    lv_updkz = 'I'.
  ELSEIF w_cec_claim IS NOT INITIAL AND         "modify existing
         w_prim_det-conc_ext_claim = abap_true.

    IF   w_cec_claim-begda NE w_prim_det-begda_cec
      OR w_cec_claim-endda NE w_prim_det-endda_cec.
      ls_cec_claim-begda  = w_prim_det-begda_cec.
      ls_cec_claim-endda  = w_prim_det-endda_cec.
      lv_updkz = 'U'.
    ENDIF.

  ELSEIF w_cec_claim IS NOT INITIAL AND         "Delete
         w_prim_det-conc_ext_claim = space.
    CLEAR: ls_cec_claim-begda, ls_cec_claim-endda.
    ls_cec_claim-inactive = abap_true.
    lv_updkz = 'D'.
  ENDIF.

  IF NOT ls_cec_claim IS INITIAL.
    MODIFY zim_in_prim FROM ls_cec_claim.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_cec_claim TO ls_prim_log.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        ls_prim_log-date_processed = sy-datum.
        ls_prim_log-time_processed = sy-uzeit.
        ls_prim_log-user_processed = sy-uname.
        ls_prim_log-code_processed = lv_updkz.
        INSERT zim_in_prim_log  FROM ls_prim_log.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error while saving Concurrent Claim'.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  w_3413_refresh = abap_true.
  CLEAR: w_cec_claim, w_prim_det.
ENDFORM.                    "save_concurrent_claim
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_EXTERNAL_CLAIM_PC_DETAILS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Save External Claim Percentage details.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_external_claim_pc_details.

  DATA:
    lv_pc_ext_claim            TYPE c LENGTH 10,
    lv_begda                   TYPE c LENGTH 10,
    lv_endda                   TYPE c LENGTH 10.

  w_trans   = 'PA30'.
  w_mode    = 'N'.
  w_updt    = 'S'.

* Percentage of Entitlement due to an External Claim.
  IF w_ecp_det-pc_ext_claim IS NOT INITIAL AND
     w_ecp_det-begda        IS NOT INITIAL AND
     w_ecp_det-endda        IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF w_ecp_det-pc_ext_claim NE w_ecp_det_old-pc_ext_claim OR
       w_ecp_det-begda        NE w_ecp_det_old-begda OR
       w_ecp_det-endda        NE w_ecp_det_old-endda.
      PERFORM refresh_tbl_bdcdata.
      PERFORM gen_bdc_header USING 'SAPMP50A'      '1000'.
      PERFORM gen_bdc_field  USING 'RP50G-PERNR'   zim_in_mast-pernr.
      PERFORM gen_bdc_field  USING 'RP50G-CHOIC'   '0014'.
      PERFORM gen_bdc_field  USING 'RP50G-TIMR6'   abap_true.
      WRITE w_ecp_det-begda TO lv_begda.
      PERFORM gen_bdc_field  USING 'RP50G-BEGDA'   lv_begda.
      WRITE w_ecp_det-endda TO lv_endda.
      PERFORM gen_bdc_field  USING 'RP50G-ENDDA'   lv_endda.
      PERFORM gen_bdc_field  USING 'RP50G-SUBTY'
         zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_wc_pcent_for_apport.
      PERFORM gen_bdc_field  USING 'BDC_OKCODE'    '=INS'.

      PERFORM gen_bdc_header USING 'MP001400'      '2000'.
      WRITE w_ecp_det-pc_ext_claim TO lv_pc_ext_claim.
      PERFORM gen_bdc_field  USING 'P0014-ANZHL'   lv_pc_ext_claim.
      PERFORM gen_bdc_field  USING 'BDC_OKCODE'    'UPD'.

      PERFORM process_call_transaction.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  w_3414_refresh = abap_true.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_EXTERNAL_CLAIM_PC_DETAILS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_med_cert_gao .


  IF zim_in_mast-gao IS INITIAL.
    zim_in_mast-gao = w_gao.
    w_action = 'U'.
    UPDATE zim_in_mast.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_MAST'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_mast_log USING w_action
                                       zim_in_mast.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_MED_CERT_GAO
