*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_INCIDENT_INJURY
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_incident_injury.

  IF w_7050_action = 'INS' OR
     w_7050_action = 'UPD'.

    IF w_7050_action = 'INS'.
      CLEAR gr_outtab1.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab1-location_code   = w_location_code.
    gr_outtab1-other_location  = w_other_location.
    gr_outtab1-nature_code     = w_nature_code.
    gr_outtab1-other_nature    = w_other_nature.
    gr_outtab1-main_injury     = w_main_inj.

    SELECT SINGLE location_desc FROM zp902_locationt
       INTO gr_outtab1-location_desc
       WHERE sprsl = 'E'
         AND location_code = gr_outtab1-location_code.

    SELECT SINGLE nature_desc FROM zp902_naturet
           INTO gr_outtab1-nature_desc
           WHERE sprsl = 'E'
             AND nature_code = gr_outtab1-nature_code.

    IF w_7050_action = 'INS'.
      APPEND gr_outtab1 TO gt_outtab1.
    ELSE.
      MODIFY gt_outtab1 FROM gr_outtab1
             INDEX gr_outtab1_row_table-index.
    ENDIF.

  ELSEIF w_7050_action = 'DEL'.
    DELETE gt_outtab1 INDEX gr_outtab1_row_table-index.

  ENDIF.

  PERFORM refresh_inc_inj_alv_total.

  w_1000_changed = 'X'.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_INCIDENT_INJURY

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_INCIDENT_INJURY_BP
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_incident_injury_bp .

  IF w_7080_action = 'INS' OR
     w_7080_action = 'UPD'.

    IF w_7080_action = 'INS'.
      CLEAR gr_outtab58.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab58-bodily_location_subtype = w_location_bp.
    gr_outtab58-injury_subtype          = w_nature_bp.
    gr_outtab58-main_injury             = w_main_inj.

    SELECT SINGLE body FROM zpbp_body_locatt
       INTO gr_outtab58-location_desc
       WHERE sprsl = 'E'
         AND sys_id = gr_outtab58-bodily_location_subtype.

    SELECT SINGLE injury      FROM zpbp_injury_sutt
           INTO gr_outtab58-nature_desc
           WHERE sprsl = 'E'
             AND sys_id = gr_outtab58-injury_subtype.

    IF w_7080_action = 'INS'.
      APPEND gr_outtab58 TO gt_outtab58.
    ELSE.
      MODIFY gt_outtab58 FROM gr_outtab58
             INDEX gr_outtab58_row_table-index.
    ENDIF.

  ELSEIF w_7080_action = 'DEL'.
    DELETE gt_outtab58 INDEX gr_outtab58_row_table-index.

  ENDIF.

  PERFORM refresh_inc_bp_inj_alv_total.

  w_1000_changed = 'X'.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_INCIDENT_INJURY_BP

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_EMP_DETAILS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_emp_details USING p_pernr.

  DATA:
    lt_in_prim                TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zim_in_prim.

  DATA:
    ls_in_prim                TYPE zim_in_prim.

  DATA:
    lv_incident_like          TYPE c LENGTH 20,
    lv_valid_in_prim          TYPE boole_d.

  DATA : l_pa9203             TYPE pa9203,
         l_pa0329             TYPE pa0329.

  CLEAR: w_emp_det, w_persk.

  IF zim_in_mast-hod = abap_true.
    w_hod_msg  = 'Pre 88 - HOD Officer'.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9203
         INTO l_pa9203
         WHERE pernr          = p_pernr
           AND z_duty_status NE 'PID'
           AND begda         LE sy-datum
           AND endda         GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    IF w_hod_msg IS NOT INITIAL.
      CONCATENATE w_hod_msg '- PRD'
                  INTO w_hod_msg SEPARATED BY ' '.
    ELSE.
      w_hod_msg = 'The Officer is PRD'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0000
         WHERE pernr = p_pernr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0000.
  ENDIF.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM t529u
         WHERE sprsl = 'E'
           AND statn = '2'
           AND statv = pa0000-stat2.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    w_emp_det-status = t529u-text1.
  ENDIF.
  IF pa0000-stat2 NE '3'.
    w_emp_det-term_date = pa0000-begda.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
         WHERE pernr = p_pernr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0001.
  ENDIF.
  w_emp_name = pa0001-ename.
  w_persk    = pa0001-persk.

* CR3185 DEVK9HRFNJ - MD
  IF ( pa0001-persk GE 'XG' AND
       pa0001-persk LE 'XT' ) OR
       pa0001-persk = 'XX' OR
       pa0001-persk = 'XY'.
    w_emp_det-admin = abap_true.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR w_emp_det-admin.
  ENDIF.


  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
         WHERE pernr = p_pernr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0002.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0007
         WHERE pernr = p_pernr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0007.
  ENDIF.
  w_emp_det-zterf = pa0007-zterf.
  IF pa0007-schkz+4(1) = ' '.
    w_emp_det-emp_type = 'Full Time'.
  ELSE.
    w_emp_det-emp_type = 'Part Time'.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0573
         WHERE pernr = p_pernr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum
           AND ( subty = '5000'
            OR   subty = '5010'
            OR   subty = '5100'
            OR   subty = '5110'
            OR   subty = '5502'
            OR   subty = '5503'
            OR   subty = '5504'
            OR   subty = '5505'
            OR   subty = '5506' ).
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    w_susp_msg = 'The employee is currently Suspended'.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0509
         WHERE pernr = p_pernr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    w_emp_det-hda_plans = pa0509-plans.
* determine the hda org unit
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM hrp1001
            WHERE otype =  'S'
            AND   objid =  pa0509-plans
            AND   plvar =  c_plvar_01
            AND   rsign =  'A'
            AND   relat =  '003'
            AND   istat =  '1'
            AND   priox LIKE  '%'
            AND begda <= sy-datum
            AND endda >= sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      w_emp_det-hda_orgeh = hrp1001-sobid(8).
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  w_emp_det-orgeh = pa0001-orgeh.
  w_emp_det-plans = pa0001-plans.
  w_emp_det-persk = pa0001-persk.

  PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    w_emp_det-orgeh
                               'O'
                      CHANGING w_emp_det-orgeh_stext
                               w_short.

  PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    w_emp_det-plans
                               'S'
                      CHANGING w_emp_det-plans_stext
                               w_short.

  w_emp_det-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
  w_emp_det-vorna = pa0002-vorna.
  w_emp_det-gbdat = pa0002-gbdat.
  w_emp_det-gesch = pa0002-gesch.
  w_emp_det-anred = pa0002-anred.
  IF pa0002-titel IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                           pa0002-namzu
                     CHANGING w_emp_det-rank.
    w_emp_det-rank_text = 'Rank'.
    w_emp_det-rank2 = w_emp_det-rank.
    CONCATENATE w_emp_det-rank w_emp_name
                INTO w_emp_name2
                SEPARATED BY ' '.
  ELSE.
    w_emp_det-rank      = w_short.
    w_emp_det-rank_text = 'Grade'.
    w_emp_name2         = w_emp_name.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zhorgstruc
         WHERE unit_code = pa0001-orgeh
           AND vbegda   <= sy-datum
           AND vendda   >= sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zhorgstruc.
  ENDIF.

  IF  zhorgstruc-lvl3_code = 0.
    w_emp_det-lvl3_orgeh = pa0001-orgeh.
  ELSE.
    w_emp_det-lvl3_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl3_code.
  ENDIF.
  IF  zhorgstruc-lvl4_code = 0.
    w_emp_det-lvl4_orgeh = pa0001-orgeh.
  ELSE.
    w_emp_det-lvl4_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl4_code.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    w_emp_det-lvl3_orgeh
                               'O'
                      CHANGING w_emp_det-lvl3_orgeh_stext
                               w_short.

  PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    w_emp_det-lvl4_orgeh
                               'O'
                      CHANGING w_emp_det-lvl4_orgeh_stext
                               w_short.


  IF w_emp_det-hda_orgeh IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    w_emp_det-hda_orgeh
                                 'O'
                        CHANGING w_emp_det-hda_orgeh_stext
                                 w_short.

    PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    w_emp_det-hda_plans
                                 'S'
                        CHANGING w_emp_det-hda_plans_stext
                                 w_short.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zhorgstruc
           WHERE unit_code = w_emp_det-hda_orgeh
             AND vbegda   <= sy-datum
             AND vendda   >= sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR zhorgstruc.
    ENDIF.

    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl3_code = 0.
      w_emp_det-hda_lvl3_orgeh = w_emp_det-hda_orgeh.
    ELSE.
      w_emp_det-hda_lvl3_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl3_code.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl4_code = 0.
      w_emp_det-hda_lvl4_orgeh = w_emp_det-hda_orgeh.
    ELSE.
      w_emp_det-hda_lvl4_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl4_code.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    w_emp_det-hda_lvl3_orgeh
                                 'O'
                        CHANGING w_emp_det-hda_lvl3_orgeh_stext
                                 w_short.

    PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    w_emp_det-hda_lvl4_orgeh
                                 'O'
                        CHANGING w_emp_det-hda_lvl4_orgeh_stext
                                 w_short.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM hrp1002
         WHERE objid = w_emp_det-plans
           AND plvar = c_plvar_01
           AND otype = c_otype_s
           AND subty BETWEEN '9500' AND '9599'
           AND istat = c_istat_1
           AND begda <= sy-datum
           AND endda >= sy-datum
           AND langu = 'EN'.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    w_emp_det-duty = hrp1002-subty.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM t777u
           WHERE langu = 'E'
             AND infty = '1002'
             AND subty = w_emp_det-duty.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      w_emp_det-duty_desc = t777u-sutxt.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_emp_det-hda_plans IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM hrp1002
           WHERE objid = w_emp_det-hda_plans
             AND plvar = c_plvar_01
             AND otype = c_otype_s
             AND subty BETWEEN '9500' AND '9599'
             AND istat = c_istat_1
             AND begda <= sy-datum
             AND endda >= sy-datum
             AND langu = 'EN'.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      w_emp_det-hda_duty = hrp1002-subty.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM t777u
             WHERE langu = 'E'
               AND infty = '1002'
               AND subty = w_emp_det-hda_duty.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        w_emp_det-hda_duty_desc = t777u-sutxt.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

*<<< AZORBA BRD 04 & 05 - New fund codes 9DDL, 9DDM
* Use disablement date as reference date to retrive the corresponding funds.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm WHERE pernr = p_pernr AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
  PERFORM build_super_ranges.
  IF zim_in_cm-transition_to_epss = abap_true.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0220
           WHERE pernr = p_pernr
             AND subty IN r_subty
            AND begda LE sy-datum
            AND endda GE sy-datum.

  ELSE.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0220
           WHERE pernr = p_pernr
             AND subty IN r_subty
*           AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND begda LE zim_in_cm-disablement_date
*           AND endda GE sy-datum.
             AND endda GE zim_in_cm-disablement_date.
  ENDIF.
*<<< AZORBA BRD 04 & 05 - New fund codes 9DDL, 9DDM
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0220.
  ENDIF.
  w_emp_det-super_fund = pa0220-subty.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM t591s
         WHERE sprsl = 'E'
           AND infty = '0220'
           AND subty = w_emp_det-super_fund.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    w_emp_det-super_desc = t591s-stext.
  ENDIF.

  CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_SALARY'
    EXPORTING
      i_pernr        = p_pernr
      i_date         = sy-datum
    IMPORTING
      e_weekly_hours = w_emp_det-wkly_hours
      e_weekly_full  = w_emp_det-wkly_full
      e_weekly_base  = w_emp_det-wkly_base
      e_yearly_full  = w_emp_det-yrly_full
      e_yearly_base  = w_emp_det-yrly_base.


  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0329
         INTO l_pa0329
         WHERE pernr  = p_pernr
           AND sprps  = ' '
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum
           AND gmpfl  = 'AP'.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR l_pa0329.
  ENDIF.

  w_emp_det-sec_begda = l_pa0329-begda.
  w_emp_det-sec_endda = l_pa0329-endda.
  w_emp_det-sec_tkbez = l_pa0329-tkbez.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_EMP_DETAILS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CM_DETAILS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_cm_details USING p_pernr
                          p_incident.

  DATA: l_usrid         TYPE pa0105-usrid.
  DATA: l_date          TYPE datum.
  DATA: l_no_days_remaining TYPE zim_epss_no_days_used_p2.

  CLEAR : w_wc_name, w_ip_name, w_epss_name, w_epss_sup_name, w_si_name, w_planned_hours,
          zim_screen-claim_review, zim_screen-claim_review_dt,
          w_frem_pernr.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_cm.
    zim_in_cm-pernr    = p_pernr.
    zim_in_cm-incident = p_incident.
    IF zim_in_mast-inc_type = 'N'.
      zim_in_cm-duty_det = 'F'.
    ELSE.
      zim_in_cm-duty_det = 'O'.
    ENDIF.
*   Set the "unsworn claim" indicator if the Person is Unsworn.
    zcl_hr_utilities=>is_person_sworn_unsworn(
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr   = p_pernr
      IMPORTING
        e_unsworn = zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim ).
  ENDIF.

  w_unsworn_claim = zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim.

  l_usrid = p_pernr.
  SHIFT l_usrid LEFT DELETING LEADING '0'.
* CONCATENATE 'N' l_usrid INTO l_usrid.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0105
         WHERE usrty = 'FREM'
           AND usrid = l_usrid.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    w_frem_pernr = pa0105-pernr.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_end_dates.

  IF zim_in_cm-wc_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-wc_advisor
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      w_wc_name = pa0001-ename.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-ip_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-ip_advisor
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      w_ip_name = pa0001-ename.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR w_ip_det.

  IF zim_in_cm-epss_examiner_id IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-epss_examiner_id
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      w_epss_name = pa0001-ename.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-epss_supervisor IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-epss_supervisor
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      w_epss_sup_name = pa0001-ename.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM determine_planned_hours.

* get disability and IP dates

  PERFORM create_ip_defaults.

  PERFORM get_dis_ip_dates.

  IF zim_in_cm-use_plan_hours = abap_true.
    w_planned_hours = w_ip_det-plan_hours.
  ENDIF.
* Changes requested by JM
* 10/01/2019 - MD

*  if zim_incident-dateincident lt '20120119'.
*    w_ip_det-bs_date = '20120119'.
*    zim_in_cm-disablement_date = '20120119'.
*  else.
*    w_ip_det-bs_date = zim_incident-dateincident.
*  endif.

*<<< AZORBA EPSS BRD 03
  IF w_epss = abap_true.
    l_date = zim_in_cm-disablement_date.
  ELSE.
    l_date = w_ip_det-bs_date.
  ENDIF.
*>>> AZORBA EPSS BRD 03

  CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_SALARY'
    EXPORTING
      i_pernr        = p_pernr
      i_date         = l_date " AZORBA BRD 03
*     i_incident     = ' '
    IMPORTING
      e_weekly_hours = w_ip_det-wkly_hours
      e_weekly_full  = w_ip_det-wkly_full
      e_weekly_base  = w_ip_det-wkly_base
      e_yearly_full  = w_ip_det-yrly_full
      e_yearly_base  = w_ip_det-yrly_base.

* changed by Y Chen on 30.11.2015 to fix system dumps
*  w_ip_det-hrly_rate = w_emp_det-wkly_base / w_emp_det-wkly_hours.
  IF w_emp_det-wkly_hours = 0.
    w_ip_det-hrly_rate = 0.
  ELSE.
    w_ip_det-hrly_rate = w_emp_det-wkly_base / w_emp_det-wkly_hours.
  ENDIF.
* end of change
* w_ip_det-hrly_rate = w_emp_det-wkly_full / w_emp_det-wkly_hours.
  w_ip_det-mthly_base = w_ip_det-yrly_base / 12.

  IF w_emp_det-persk GE 'XC' AND
     w_emp_det-persk LE 'XF'.
    w_ip_det-yrly_17 = w_ip_det-yrly_base.
    w_ip_det-wkly_17 = w_ip_det-wkly_base.
    w_ip_det-comm_off = 'Commissioned Officer'.
  ELSE.
    w_ip_det-yrly_17 = w_ip_det-yrly_base * '1.17'.
    w_ip_det-wkly_17 = w_ip_det-wkly_base * '1.17'.
    CLEAR w_ip_det-comm_off.
  ENDIF.

  w_ip_det-mthly_17 = w_ip_det-yrly_17 / 12.
  w_ip_det-yrly_75  = w_ip_det-yrly_17 * '0.75'.
  w_ip_det-wkly_75  = w_ip_det-wkly_17 * '0.75'.
  w_ip_det-mthly_75 = w_ip_det-mthly_17 * '0.75'.

* Changed by Y Chen on 30.11.2015 to fix system dumps
  IF w_emp_det-wkly_hours = 0.
    w_ip_det-wkly_full_plan = 0.
  ELSE.
    w_ip_det-wkly_full_plan = w_emp_det-wkly_full *
                              w_ip_det-plan_hours /
                              w_emp_det-wkly_hours.
  ENDIF.

  w_ip_det-mthly_full_plan = w_ip_det-plan_hours *
                             w_ip_det-hrly_rate
                             * 52 / 12.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0014
         WHERE pernr = p_pernr
           AND subty = '9WCO'
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    w_ip_det-preas = pa0014-preas.
    IF pa0014-preas = 'VD' OR
       pa0014-preas = 'ZD'.
      w_ip_det-wkly_wc_plan = w_emp_det-wkly_full * '0.80'.
    ELSEIF pa0014-preas = 'VA' OR
           pa0014-preas = 'VB' OR
           pa0014-preas = 'VC' OR
           pa0014-preas = 'ZA' OR
           pa0014-preas = 'ZB' OR
           pa0014-preas = 'ZC'.
      w_ip_det-wkly_wc_plan = w_emp_det-wkly_full.
    ELSEIF pa0014-preas = 'VL' OR
           pa0014-preas = 'VM' OR
           pa0014-preas = 'ZL' OR
           pa0014-preas = 'ZM'.
      w_ip_det-wkly_wc_plan = pa0014-anzhl.
    ELSE.
      w_ip_det-wkly_wc_plan = pa0014-anzhl - w_ip_det-wkly_full_plan.
    ENDIF.
    IF w_ip_det-wkly_wc_plan GT pa0014-betrg.
      w_ip_det-wkly_wc_plan = pa0014-betrg.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  w_ip_det-mthly_wc_plan = w_ip_det-wkly_wc_plan * 52 / 12.
* changed by Y Chen on 29.11.2018 to avoid stort dump
  IF w_ip_det-mthly_17 <> 0.
* end of change
    w_ip_det-mthly_ip_plan = ( ( ( w_ip_det-mthly_17 -
                             w_ip_det-mthly_full_plan ) /
                             w_ip_det-mthly_17 ) * w_ip_det-mthly_75 )
                             - w_ip_det-mthly_wc_plan.

  ENDIF.
  IF w_ip_det-mthly_ip_plan LT 0.
    w_ip_det-mthly_ip_plan = 0.
  ENDIF.
  w_ip_det-wkly_ip_plan = w_ip_det-mthly_ip_plan / '30.4167' * 14.


  IF w_ip_det-preas IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM t530f
           WHERE sprsl = 'E'
             AND infty = '0014'
             AND preas = w_ip_det-preas.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      w_ip_det-preas_desc = t530f-rtext.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

*<<< AZORBA EPSS BRD 03
* Apply the calculations for EPSS
  PERFORM calculate_epss_dates USING p_pernr p_incident.
*>>> AZORBA EPSS BRD 03

ENDFORM.                    " GET_CM_DETAILS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  calculate_epss_dates
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Calculates the EPSS dates within the EPSS Calculations tab.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM calculate_epss_dates USING p_pernr
                                p_incident.

  DATA: lv_no_days_remaining TYPE zim_epss_no_days_used_p2,
        lv_tot_days_p2       TYPE zim_epss_no_days_used_p2,
        lv_no_r2d_days_past  TYPE zim_epss_no_days_used_p2,
        lv_no_r2d_days_future TYPE zim_epss_no_days_used_p2,
        lv_tot_r2d_days_past TYPE zim_epss_no_days_used_p2,
        lv_tot_r2d_days_future TYPE zim_epss_no_days_used_p2,
        lt_epss_p2rd         TYPE TABLE OF zim_in_epss_p2rd,
        ls_epss_p2rd         TYPE zim_in_epss_p2rd,
        lt_epss_earn         TYPE TABLE OF zim_in_epss_earn,
        ls_epss_earn         TYPE zim_in_epss_earn,
        lv_other_earnings    TYPE zim_in_epss_earn-earn_amount,
        lv_ext_earnings      TYPE zim_in_epss_earn-earn_amount,
*        lv_planned_hours     TYPE divgv,
        lv_weekly_hours      TYPE wostd,
        lv_cap_util_level    TYPE bsgrd,
        lv_rundate           TYPE datum,
        lt_week_no           TYPE TABLE OF zim_epss_week_no,
        ls_week_no           TYPE zim_epss_week_no,
        w_frly_orp           TYPE p DECIMALS 5,
        w_yrly_orp           TYPE p DECIMALS 5,
        w_frly_est_pay_p1    TYPE p DECIMALS 5,
        lo_t511k             TYPE REF TO cl_hrpayxx_t511k,
        ls_t511k             TYPE t511k,
        lv_new_r2d_record    TYPE boole_d.

  CONSTANTS: lc_other_earnings TYPE zim_in_epss_earn-earning_type VALUE 'O',
             lc_ext_earnings   TYPE zim_in_epss_earn-earning_type VALUE 'E',
             lc_molga          TYPE molga VALUE '13',
             lc_max_cap_epss   TYPE abrkn VALUE 'ZEPS2'.

  IF w_epss = abap_true. " Only when EPSS

    IF zim_in_cm-duty_det = 'O'.


      CLEAR: w_epss_det, lv_cap_util_level, lv_weekly_hours, w_frly_est_pay_p1, w_frly_orp, w_yrly_orp.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING w_ip_det TO w_epss_det.
      IF w_ip_det-wkly_wc_plan LE 0.
        w_epss_det-wkly_wc_plan_p1 = w_epss_det-wkly_wc_plan_p2 = w_ip_det-wkly_wc_plan = 0.
      ELSE.
        w_epss_det-wkly_wc_plan_p1 = w_epss_det-wkly_wc_plan_p2 = w_ip_det-wkly_wc_plan.
      ENDIF.

      w_epss_det-preas_desc_p1 = w_epss_det-preas_desc_p2 = w_ip_det-preas_desc.

      " Select the start / end dates of Period 1 & 2.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm
             WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
               AND incident = p_incident.

      IF zim_in_cm-disablement_date IS INITIAL.
        CLEAR w_epss_det.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      " Select the P1/P2 mock current dates
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_epss
             WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
               AND incident = p_incident.

*     The date zim_in_epss-mock_current_date_p1 was implemented which when populated,
*     allows the logic to use the given date as if the run is occurring in the future.
*     This was created to validate certain future-dated scenarios as the build phase was not in Period 1/2 yet
*     e.g., end of period 1 or future-dated pay runs for the "number of weeks used" logic, etc.
      CLEAR lv_rundate.
      IF zim_in_epss-mock_current_date_p1 IS INITIAL.
        lv_rundate = sy-datum.
      ELSE.
        lv_rundate = zim_in_epss-mock_current_date_p1.
      ENDIF.

*********************************
*** Calculations for Period 1 ***
*********************************

*   Calculate the Estimated EPSS P1 Dates
      " Firstly we want to read the week no table to get the number of weeks used of Period 1
      REFRESH lt_week_no.
      SELECT * FROM zim_epss_week_no INTO TABLE lt_week_no
        WHERE pernr = p_pernr
          AND incident = p_incident.

      SORT lt_week_no BY no_weeks_used_p1 DESCENDING.

      READ TABLE lt_week_no INTO ls_week_no INDEX 1.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        " EPSS Period 1 - Top up (39 weeks)
        w_epss_det-no_weeks_used_p1 = ls_week_no-no_weeks_used_p1.
        IF w_epss_det-no_weeks_used_p1 GT 39.
          w_epss_det-no_weeks_remaining_p1 = lv_no_days_remaining = 0.
        ELSE.
          w_epss_det-no_weeks_remaining_p1 = 39 - w_epss_det-no_weeks_used_p1.
          lv_no_days_remaining = 7 * w_epss_det-no_weeks_remaining_p1.
        ENDIF.
        IF w_epss_det-no_weeks_used_p1 = 0. " Set Top-up end date as initial top-up end date if no weeks has been used
          w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1 = zim_in_cm-epss_end_date_p1.
        ELSE.

          IF lv_no_days_remaining GT 0.
            w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1 = lv_rundate + lv_no_days_remaining.
          ELSE.
            IF zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p2 IS NOT INITIAL. " EPSS Phase 4
              " No P1 days remaining, Period 2 has  started.
              w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1 = zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p2 - 1.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF. " IF w_epss_det-no_weeks_used_p1 = 0

        zim_in_cm-topup_end_date = w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1.

      ELSE.
        " No record in the week no table meaning no top up paid yet and week 0.
        w_epss_det-no_weeks_used_p1 = 0.
        w_epss_det-no_weeks_remaining_p1 = 39.

        lv_no_days_remaining = 7 * w_epss_det-no_weeks_remaining_p1.
        IF zim_in_epss-mock_current_date_p1 IS INITIAL.
          w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1 = sy-datum + lv_no_days_remaining.
        ELSE. "zim_in_epss-mock_current_date_p1 NOT INITIAL.
          w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1 = zim_in_epss-mock_current_date_p1 + lv_no_days_remaining.
        ENDIF. " IF zim_in_epss-mock_current_date_p1 IS INITIAL.
        zim_in_cm-topup_end_date = w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1.
      ENDIF. " READ TABLE lt_week_no INTO ls_week_no INDEX 1.

*<<< EPSS Phase 4 AZORBA - Calculate ORP based on current date to cater for salary increments - DEVK9HRGPL
      IF lv_rundate LE w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1.
        w_epss_det-orp_date = lv_rundate.
      ELSE.
        w_epss_det-orp_date = w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1.
      ENDIF.

*     Read Ordinary Rate of Pay yearly amount and planned hours
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_EPSS_SALARY_ORP'
        EXPORTING
          i_pernr         = p_pernr
          i_date          = w_epss_det-orp_date
        IMPORTING
          e_weekly_hours  = lv_weekly_hours
          e_orp_weekly    = w_epss_det-wkly_orp
          e_orp_yearly    = w_epss_det-yrly_orp
          e_planned_hours = w_epss_det-plan_hours_p1.
*>>> EPSS Phase 4 AZORBA - Calculate ORP based on current date to cater for salary increments

*     NSWPF Other earnings and external earnings are entered manually by SS in OLIMS.
*     Those earnings must be deducted from the estimated EPSS payments.
      CLEAR lt_epss_earn[].
      SELECT * FROM zim_in_epss_earn INTO TABLE lt_epss_earn
             WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
               AND incident = p_incident.
      SORT lt_epss_earn BY seq_no.
      lv_other_earnings = lv_ext_earnings = 0.
      LOOP AT lt_epss_earn INTO ls_epss_earn WHERE begda LE w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1
                                               AND endda GE zim_in_cm-disablement_date
                                               AND inactive = abap_false.
        " Skip when earning gets expired.
        IF ls_epss_earn-endda LT lv_rundate.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.
        CASE ls_epss_earn-earning_type.
          WHEN lc_other_earnings. " NSWPF Other Earnings
            ADD ls_epss_earn-earn_amount TO lv_other_earnings.
          WHEN lc_ext_earnings. "  External Earnings
            ADD ls_epss_earn-earn_amount TO lv_ext_earnings.
          WHEN OTHERS.
        ENDCASE.
      ENDLOOP.

      IF w_epss_det-wkly_orp GT 0.
*       Other / External Earnings
        w_epss_det-other_earn_p1 = lv_other_earnings.
        w_epss_det-ext_earn_p1 = lv_ext_earnings.

*       Calculate the Ordinary Rate of Pay
        w_frly_orp = w_epss_det-wkly_orp * 2.
        w_epss_det-frly_orp = w_frly_orp.

        IF w_epss_det-plan_hours_p1 EQ 0.
          IF lv_weekly_hours = 0.
            w_epss_det-hrly_orp = 0.
          ELSE.
            " PT HDA salary is calculated based on IT7
            w_epss_det-hrly_orp = w_epss_det-wkly_orp / lv_weekly_hours.
          ENDIF.
        ELSE.
          " PT Basic salary is calculated based on IT8
          w_epss_det-hrly_orp = w_epss_det-frly_orp / w_epss_det-plan_hours_p1.
        ENDIF.

*       Calculate the estimated EPSS payments for Period 1
        w_epss_det-wkly_est_pay_p1 = ( w_epss_det-wkly_orp - ( w_epss_det-wkly_wc_plan_p1 + w_epss_det-other_earn_p1 + w_epss_det-ext_earn_p1 ) ).
        IF w_epss_det-wkly_est_pay_p1 LE 0.
          w_epss_det-wkly_est_pay_p1 = w_epss_det-frly_est_pay_p1 = 0.
        ELSE.
          w_frly_est_pay_p1 = w_epss_det-wkly_est_pay_p1 * 2.
          w_epss_det-frly_est_pay_p1 = w_frly_est_pay_p1.
        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.

*********************************
*** Calculations for Period 2 ***
*********************************
      CLEAR lv_rundate.
      IF zim_in_epss-mock_current_date_p2 IS INITIAL.
        lv_rundate = sy-datum.
      ELSE.
        lv_rundate = zim_in_epss-mock_current_date_p2.
      ENDIF.


      " Period 2 start/end dates
      IF zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p2 IS INITIAL.
        w_epss_det-epss_start_date_p2  = w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1 + 1.
        " Calculate the total number of days of 7 years
        PERFORM get_number_of_days_in_7years
          USING w_epss_det-epss_start_date_p2
          CHANGING lv_tot_days_p2.
        w_epss_det-epss_end_date_p2 = w_epss_det-epss_init_end_date_p2 = w_epss_det-epss_start_date_p2 + lv_tot_days_p2.

      ELSE.
        w_epss_det-epss_start_date_p2  = zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p2.
        w_epss_det-epss_init_end_date_p2 = zim_in_cm-epss_init_end_date_p2.
        w_epss_det-epss_end_date_p2 = zim_in_cm-epss_end_date_p2.
        " Update Period 1 Estimated End Date to reflect what it is in payroll as payroll updates
        " the start date of Period 2 when the 39 weeks of period 1 has reached.
        IF w_epss_det-no_weeks_used_p1 EQ 39 AND lv_no_days_remaining = 0.
          w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1 = w_epss_det-epss_start_date_p2 - 1.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      w_epss_det-commence_p2_payment = zim_in_epss-commence_p2_payment.

      " Get the total of return to duty days (past and future periods in days)
      CLEAR lv_new_r2d_record.
      PERFORM get_return_to_duty_days
        USING p_pernr p_incident w_epss_det-epss_start_date_p2 w_epss_det-epss_end_date_p2 lv_rundate
        CHANGING lv_tot_r2d_days_past lv_tot_r2d_days_future lv_new_r2d_record.

      " Calculate the total number of days of 7 years
      PERFORM get_number_of_days_in_7years
        USING w_epss_det-epss_start_date_p2
        CHANGING lv_tot_days_p2.


      IF lv_rundate LT w_epss_det-epss_start_date_p2.
        " P2 has not started yet... number of days is zero
        w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 = 0.
        w_epss_det-no_days_remaining_p2 = lv_tot_days_p2.
      ELSE.
        " P2 has started...
        " No of P2 days used is number of days since start (return to duty days/pauses will be added further below).
        w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 = lv_rundate - w_epss_det-epss_start_date_p2.

        IF w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 GT lv_tot_days_p2.
          " If total number of Period 2 days has been fully used, no of days used is same as total number of days of Period 2.
          w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 = lv_tot_days_p2.
        ENDIF.


        IF lv_tot_r2d_days_past GE w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2.
          " if there are more return to duty days than days used, days used is set back to zero.
          w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 = 0.
        ELSE. " lv_tot_r2d_days_past LT w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2.
          IF w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 GE 0 AND w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 LT lv_tot_days_p2.
            w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 =  w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 + 1. " +1 to include end date
          ENDIF.
          " If the return to duty period is in the past we need to subtract them from the number of days used
          w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 = w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 - lv_tot_r2d_days_past.
        ENDIF. " IF lv_tot_r2d_days_past GE w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2.

        IF w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 = 0.
          " Set IP end date as initial IP end date if there is no days used yet
*            w_epss_det-epss_end_date_p2 = zim_in_cm-epss_end_date_p2.
          w_epss_det-no_days_remaining_p2 = lv_tot_days_p2.
          IF lv_tot_r2d_days_future IS NOT INITIAL.
            " P2 end date is system date + the number of days remaining + no of (future) return to duty days
            w_epss_det-epss_end_date_p2 = lv_rundate + w_epss_det-no_days_remaining_p2 + lv_tot_r2d_days_future.
            IF zim_in_cm-epss_end_date_p2 IS NOT INITIAL. " EPSS Phase 4 - Only update dates in table if P2 has started.
              zim_in_cm-ip_end_date = zim_in_cm-epss_end_date_p2 = w_epss_det-epss_end_date_p2.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ELSE. " w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 NE 0.
          " P2 has used days
          IF w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 GE lv_tot_days_p2.
            " P2 used days more than 7 years... no remaining days left
            w_epss_det-no_days_remaining_p2 = 0.

          ELSE. " w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 LT lv_tot_days_p2.
            " P2 used days has not reached 7 years yet...

            " No of days remaining is 7 years + return to duty (future) days less the days used
            w_epss_det-no_days_remaining_p2 = lv_tot_days_p2 - w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 + lv_tot_r2d_days_future.

            IF lv_new_r2d_record IS NOT INITIAL. " Update p2 end date only when Return to duty tab has changed
              w_epss_det-epss_end_date_p2 = lv_rundate + w_epss_det-no_days_remaining_p2.
*              IF w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 LT lv_tot_days_p2.
*                w_epss_det-ip_end_date_p2 = w_epss_det-ip_end_date_p2 + 1. " +1 to include end date
*              ENDIF.
              IF zim_in_cm-epss_end_date_p2 IS NOT INITIAL. " EPSS Phase 4 - Only update dates in table if P2 has started.
                zim_in_cm-ip_end_date = zim_in_cm-epss_end_date_p2 = w_epss_det-epss_end_date_p2.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF. " IF w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 GE lv_tot_days_p2.

        ENDIF. " IF w_epss_det-no_days_used_p2 = 0.

      ENDIF. " IF lv_rundate LT w_epss_det-ip_start_date_p2.

*     Check for Other/External Earnings to duduct from the EPSS P2 payments .
      lv_other_earnings = lv_ext_earnings = 0.
      LOOP AT lt_epss_earn INTO ls_epss_earn WHERE begda LE w_epss_det-epss_end_date_p2
                                               AND endda GE w_epss_det-epss_start_date_p2
                                               AND inactive = abap_false.
        " Skip when earning gets expired.
        IF ls_epss_earn-endda LT lv_rundate.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.

        CASE ls_epss_earn-earning_type.
          WHEN lc_other_earnings. " NSWPF Other Earnings
            ADD ls_epss_earn-earn_amount TO lv_other_earnings.
          WHEN lc_ext_earnings. "  External Earnings
            ADD ls_epss_earn-earn_amount TO lv_ext_earnings.
          WHEN OTHERS.
        ENDCASE.
      ENDLOOP.

*     Other / External Earnings
      w_epss_det-other_earn_p2 = lv_other_earnings.
      w_epss_det-ext_earn_p2 = lv_ext_earnings.

*     Get the Max Monthly EPSS P2 Cap
      lo_t511k = cl_hrpayxx_t511k=>get_instance( iv_molga = lc_molga
                                                 iv_date  = sy-datum ).
      ls_t511k  = lo_t511k->read( iv_konst = lc_max_cap_epss ).
      w_epss_det-wkly_epss_cap = ls_t511k-kwert.

      w_epss_det-bs_date = w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1.
*     Calculate the 75% of Base Salary +17% yearly, monthly, fortnightly and weekly.

      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_EPSS_P2_CALC_SUPLEM_WKL_AMT'
        EXPORTING
          i_pernr            = p_pernr
          i_date             = w_epss_det-estimated_end_date_p1
        IMPORTING
          e_yearly_base      = w_epss_det-yrly_base
          e_weekly_base_75   = w_epss_det-wkly_75
          e_yearly_base_75   = w_epss_det-yrly_75
          e_yearly_base_17pc = w_epss_det-yrly_17
          e_weekly_amount_p2 = w_epss_det-wkly_est_pay_p2
          e_cap_util_level   = lv_cap_util_level
          e_planned_hours    = w_epss_det-plan_hours_p2.

      IF w_epss_det-yrly_75 EQ 0.
        w_epss_det-mthly_75 = w_epss_det-wkly_75 = w_epss_det-frly_75 = w_epss_det-hrly_75 = 0.
      ELSE.
        w_epss_det-yrly_17 = w_epss_det-yrly_17 + w_epss_det-yrly_base.

        w_epss_det-mthly_75 = w_epss_det-yrly_75 / 12.
        w_epss_det-frly_75 = w_epss_det-yrly_75 / '26.08929'.

        IF w_epss_det-plan_hours_p2 EQ 0.
          w_epss_det-hrly_75 = 0.
        ELSE.
          " Base IT8 use Infotype 7 for part time prorata
          w_epss_det-hrly_75 = w_epss_det-frly_75 / w_epss_det-plan_hours_p2.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

*     Calculate the estimated EPSS payments for Period 2
      w_epss_det-wkly_est_pay_p2 = ( w_epss_det-wkly_75 - ( w_epss_det-wkly_wc_plan_p2 + w_epss_det-other_earn_p2 + w_epss_det-ext_earn_p2 ) ).
      IF w_epss_det-wkly_est_pay_p2 LE 0.
        w_epss_det-wkly_est_pay_p2 = w_epss_det-frly_est_pay_p2 = w_epss_det-mthly_est_pay_p2 = 0.
      ELSE.
        w_epss_det-frly_est_pay_p2 = w_epss_det-wkly_est_pay_p2 * 2.
        w_epss_det-mthly_est_pay_p2 = ( w_epss_det-wkly_est_pay_p2 * '52.143' ) / 12.
      ENDIF.

*     Overwrite the EPSS P2 start/end dates into ZIM_IN_CM as they are dynamically
*     calculated based on the Return to Duty entries if a new change was made.
      IF lv_new_r2d_record EQ abap_true.
        UPDATE zim_in_cm FROM zim_in_cm.
      ENDIF.

    ELSE. " Off-duty
*     Off-duty claim is ot applicable for EPSS, we just want to display the dates.
*     which start date is day 60 after the disablement date and end date is 3 years after start date.
*     In this case there will be no record types nor any amount in the EPSS calculation fields in the EPSS calculation
      zim_in_cm-topup_end_date = zcl_olims_config=>get_ip_begin_date_off( zim_in_cm-disablement_date ).
      zim_in_cm-ip_end_date = zcl_olims_config=>get_epss_p2_end_date_off( zim_in_cm-topup_end_date ) - 1.
      CLEAR: w_epss_det, zim_in_cm-epss_end_date_p1, zim_in_cm-epss_end_date_p2,
             zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p1, zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p2.
    ENDIF. " IF zim_in_cm-duty_det = 'O'.
  ENDIF. " IF w_epss IS NOT INITIAL.

ENDFORM.                    " calculate_epss_dates


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  DETERMINE_UNSWORN_CLAIM
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Determines whether the Claim should be handled as an Unsworn
*       Claim.  By default this will be whether the employee is
*       Sworn or Unsworn; but can be different in certain cases
*       (eg. employee moves from Sworn to Unsworn after the Incident).
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM determine_unsworn_claim
  USING
    p_pernr                   TYPE persno
    p_incident                TYPE zhr_inc_identnr.

  CLEAR w_unsworn_claim.

  IF zim_in_cm-pernr    EQ p_pernr AND
     zim_in_cm-incident EQ p_incident.
*   ZIM_IN_CM already read, so use the value from there.
    w_unsworn_claim = zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim.
  ELSE.
    SELECT SINGLE unsworn_claim
      FROM zim_in_cm
      INTO w_unsworn_claim
      WHERE pernr    EQ p_pernr
        AND incident EQ p_incident.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
*     No ZIM_IN_CLAIM record exists, so default to whether the
*     employee is sworn or unsworn.
      zcl_hr_utilities=>is_person_sworn_unsworn(
        EXPORTING
          i_pernr   = p_pernr
        IMPORTING
          e_unsworn = w_unsworn_claim ).
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " DETERMINE_UNSWORN_CLAIM

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  MAINTAIN_INSURER
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_3165   text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM maintain_insurer  USING  p_action
                              p_ins.

  DATA: l_ins_code       TYPE zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont.


  CLEAR w_code2.
  IF zim_in_mast-insurer IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'An Insurer must be entered'
                      'before a Contact can be Created/Maintained'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p_ins = '1'.
    l_ins_code = zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont.
  ELSE.
    l_ins_code = zim_in_mast-ins_sec_cont.
  ENDIF.

  CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_INSURER'
    EXPORTING
      i_action          = p_action
      i_insurer         = zim_in_mast-insurer
      i_contact         = l_ins_code
    IMPORTING
      e_contact         = l_ins_code
    EXCEPTIONS
      insurer_not_found = 1
      contact_not_found = 2
      invalid_action    = 3
      OTHERS            = 4.

  IF sy-subrc = 1.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Insurer'.
  ELSEIF sy-subrc = 2.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Contact'.
  ELSEIF sy-subrc = 2.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Action'.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_ins_cont
         WHERE insurer = zim_in_mast-insurer
           AND contact = l_ins_code.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
  ENDIF.

  IF p_ins = '1'.
    zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont = l_ins_code.
    w_ins1_name  = zim_ins_cont-name.
    w_ins1_phone = zim_ins_cont-phone.
    w_ins1_fax   = zim_ins_cont-fax.
  ELSE.
    zim_in_mast-ins_sec_cont = l_ins_code.
    w_ins2_name  = zim_ins_cont-name.
    w_ins2_phone = zim_ins_cont-phone.
    w_ins2_fax   = zim_ins_cont-fax.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " MAINTAIN_INSURER

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  MAINTAIN_HEALTH_PROFESSIONAL
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_3165   text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM maintain_health_professional  USING    p_action
                                   CHANGING p_prac_code.

  CLEAR w_code2.

  CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_HEALTH_PROFESSIONAL'
    EXPORTING
      i_action       = p_action
      i_med_prac     = p_prac_code
    IMPORTING
      e_med_prac     = p_prac_code
    EXCEPTIONS
      prof_not_found = 1
      invalid_action = 2
      OTHERS         = 3.

  IF sy-subrc = 1.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Health Professional'.
  ELSEIF sy-subrc = 2.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Action'.
  ENDIF.


  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_med
         WHERE med_prac = p_prac_code.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_med.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " MAINTAIN_HEALTH_PROFESSIONAL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ALV_3417
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM alv_3417 .
  DATA: lv_hist     TYPE boole_d.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF clm_ip_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT clm_ip_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'CLM_IP_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT clm_ip_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = clm_ip_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

    "Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_3417
                FOR clm_ip_alv.

    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    clm_ip_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    clm_ip_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    clm_ip_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    clm_ip_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    clm_ip_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_ip_rec  USING lv_hist
                                CHANGING clm_ip_fieldcat.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES clm_ip_functions.

    CALL METHOD clm_ip_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = clm_ip_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = clm_ip_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = clm_ip_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab3417.

    w_3417_refresh = abap_true.
  ENDIF.


  IF w_3417_refresh = abap_true.
    PERFORM get_dis_ip_dates.
    gr_ip_rec->set_current_record_type( iv_rec_type = w_record_type ).
    PERFORM fill_outtab_3417.

    CLEAR w_3417_refresh.
    CALL METHOD clm_ip_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = clm_ip_layout.
    clm_ip_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    CALL METHOD clm_ip_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = clm_ip_layout.

    CALL METHOD clm_ip_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " ALV_3417
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_IP_RECORDS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Get IP Records
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_ip_records .
* check if existing object is valid
  IF gr_ip_rec IS NOT INITIAL AND
     ( gr_ip_rec->gv_pernr     NE  zim_in_mast-pernr OR
      gr_ip_rec->gv_incident   NE  zim_in_mast-incident ).
*   not valid, invalidate object
    CLEAR gr_ip_rec.
  ENDIF.

* create pawe object for the incident
  IF gr_ip_rec IS INITIAL.
    CREATE OBJECT gr_ip_rec
      EXPORTING
        iv_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
        iv_incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " GET_IP_RECORDS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FILL_OUTTAB_3417
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM fill_outtab_3417 .
  DATA: ls_in_ip    TYPE zim_in_ip,
        lt_rec_type TYPE if_dd_types=>ty_t_domvalues,
        ls_rec_type TYPE if_dd_types=>ty_s_domvalue,
        lv_value    TYPE domvalue_l.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <ls_outtab3417> TYPE gs_outtab_type3417.

  lt_rec_type = cl_domain=>get_fixed_values( domain_name = 'ZIM_IP_RECORD_TYPE').
  SORT lt_rec_type BY value.

*   Outtab for ALV Display
  REFRESH  gt_outtab3417.
  LOOP AT gr_ip_rec->gt_ip  INTO ls_in_ip.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_outtab3417 ASSIGNING <ls_outtab3417>.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_in_ip TO <ls_outtab3417>.
    <ls_outtab3417>-del_icon  = icon_delete.
    <ls_outtab3417>-imp_icon  = icon_import.
    IF zcl_olims_ip_rec=>check_if_doc_exists( ls_in_ip ) = abap_true.
      <ls_outtab3417>-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
    ENDIF.
    lv_value = <ls_outtab3417>-record_type.
    READ TABLE lt_rec_type INTO ls_rec_type WITH KEY value = lv_value BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      <ls_outtab3417>-rec_type_desc = ls_rec_type-description.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT  gt_outtab3417 BY record_type
                         begda  DESCENDING.
ENDFORM.                    " FILL_OUTTAB_3417
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_IP_REC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_ip_rec USING pv_hist       TYPE boole_d
                            CHANGING p_fieldcat TYPE lvc_t_fcat.

  DATA: l_col         TYPE i,
        ls_fieldcat   TYPE lvc_s_fcat,
        lv_top_no_out TYPE boole_d  VALUE abap_true,
        lv_ip_no_out  TYPE boole_d  VALUE abap_true,
        lv_dis_no_out TYPE boole_d  VALUE abap_true,
        lv_no_out     TYPE boole_d,
        lv_action     TYPE sy-ucomm.


* Control fields based on drop-down
  IF w_record_type = zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_ip
    OR w_record_type = zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_epss_p2. " *<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03 - Handling new record type EPSS
    CLEAR lv_ip_no_out.
  ELSEIF w_record_type = zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_top
      OR w_record_type = zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_epss_p1. " *<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03 - Handling new record type EPSS
    CLEAR lv_top_no_out.
  ELSEIF w_record_type = zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_dis.
    CLEAR lv_dis_no_out.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR: lv_ip_no_out, lv_top_no_out, lv_dis_no_out.
  ENDIF.

* history
  IF pv_hist  IS  INITIAL.
    lv_no_out = abap_true.
  ENDIF.

* if called from IP Case
  IF w_claim_type = 'I' .
    lv_action = w_3500_action.
  ELSEIF w_claim_type = 'W' AND
    sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRCM'.
    lv_action = w_3400_action.
  ELSE.
*   disply only for WC
    lv_action = 'DISP'.
  ENDIF.


  REFRESH p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REC_TYPE_DESC'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Record Type'.
  ls_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IP'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'BEGDA'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date From'(h02).
  ls_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IP'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ENDDA'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date To'(h03).
  ls_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IP'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'TOP_UP'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Top-Up'.
  ls_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_top_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IP'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CLAIM_NUMBER'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Claim Number'.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_ip_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IP'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DISABLEMENT_DATE'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date of Disablement'.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_dis_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF lv_action NE 'DISP'.
    IF ( w_record_type = zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_ip
      OR w_record_type = zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_epss_p2 ) " *<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03 - Handling new record type EPSS
      AND sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRCM'.
      CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
      ADD 1 TO l_col.
      ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DEL_ICON'.
      ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
      ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Delete'(h05).
      ls_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
      ls_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
      ls_fieldcat-no_out        = pv_hist.
      ls_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
      APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'IMP_ICON'.
    ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Import'.
    ls_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    ls_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    ls_fieldcat-no_out        = pv_hist.
    ls_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DOC_ICON'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  ls_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  ls_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IP'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'IP_DESC'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Description'(h04).
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IP'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_USER'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Changed By'(h08).
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IP'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_DATE'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Change Date'(h06).
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IP'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_TIME'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Change Time'(h07).
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IP'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INACTIVE'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Record Inactive'(h09).
  ls_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_IP_REC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_3417
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       validate screen 3417
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_3417 .
  DATA: lv_date   TYPE datum,
        lv_char10 TYPE char10,
        lv_disablement_date TYPE datum.

* if fields are set to empty, clear
  IF zim_in_ip-claim_number IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_ip-begda        IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_ip-top_up       IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_ip-ip_desc      IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_ip-disablement_date IS INITIAL.
    IF ( zim_in_ip-record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_top OR
       zim_in_ip-record_type   EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_ip OR
       zim_in_ip-record_type   EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_epss_p1 OR     "Added by AZORBA EPSS BRD 03
       zim_in_ip-record_type   EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_epss_p2 ) AND  "Added by AZORBA EPSS BRD 03
       zim_in_ip-endda         IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR zim_in_ip.
      EXIT.
    ELSEIF zim_in_ip-record_type IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR zim_in_ip.
      MESSAGE s999 WITH 'Record Type must be selected'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_record_type  IS  INITIAL.
    MESSAGE 'Record Type must be selected'  TYPE 'E'.
    SET CURSOR FIELD 'W_RECORD_TYPE'.
  ELSE.
    zim_in_ip-record_type = w_record_type.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_ip-record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_top        "Top record
    OR zim_in_ip-record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_epss_p1.  " *<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03 - Period 1
*  validate fields for top up
  ELSE.
    CLEAR zim_in_ip-top_up.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_ip-record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_ip.        "IP record
*  validate fields for IP
*  get disablement date
    IF zim_in_ip-endda IS  INITIAL.
      lv_date = gr_ip_rec->get_disablement_date( ).
      IF lv_date IS INITIAL.
        lv_date = zim_incident-dateincident.
      ENDIF.
*  set default IP end date
      IF zim_in_cm-duty_det = 'O'.   "End date for On-Duty
        zim_in_ip-endda = zcl_olims_config=>get_ip_end_date_on( lv_date ).
      ELSE.                          "End date for Off-duty
        zim_in_ip-endda = zcl_olims_config=>get_ip_end_date_off( lv_date ).
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR zim_in_ip-claim_number.
  ENDIF.
*<<< AZORBA EPSS BRD 03 - Implement validation for record type E - EPSS
  IF zim_in_ip-record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_epss_p2.        "EPSS P2 record
*  validate fields for EPSS
*  get disablement date
    IF zim_in_ip-endda IS  INITIAL.
      CLEAR lv_disablement_date.
      lv_disablement_date = gr_ip_rec->get_disablement_date( ).
      lv_date = zcl_olims_config=>get_ip_begin_date_on( lv_disablement_date ).

*  set default EPSS end date
      IF zim_in_cm-duty_det = 'O'.   "End date for On-Duty
        zim_in_ip-endda = zcl_olims_config=>get_ip_end_date_on( lv_date ).
      ELSE.                          "End date for Off-duty
        zim_in_ip-endda = zcl_olims_config=>get_epss_p2_end_date_off( lv_date ).
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR zim_in_ip-claim_number.
  ENDIF.
*>>> AZORBA

* Validations for Date of disablement
  IF zim_in_ip-record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_dis.
    IF zim_in_ip-begda IS INITIAL.
      zim_in_ip-begda = zim_in_ip-disablement_date.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_in_ip-ip_desc IS INITIAL.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_IP-IP_DESC'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Description - Reason for Disablement '
                        'Date change must be entered'.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR zim_in_ip-disablement_date.
  ENDIF.

* set end date to highest
  IF zim_in_ip-endda IS INITIAL AND
   zim_in_ip-begda IS NOT INITIAL.
    zim_in_ip-endda = '99991231'.
  ENDIF.
* date to must be greater than date from
  IF zim_in_ip-begda GT zim_in_ip-endda.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From cannot be greater'
                      'than Date To'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_3417

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_4104
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       validate screen 4104
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_4104 .
  DATA: lv_date   TYPE datum,
        lv_char10 TYPE char10,
        lv_days   TYPE zim_epss_no_days_used_p2,
        lt_p2rd   TYPE TABLE OF zim_in_epss_p2rd,
        ls_p2rd   TYPE zim_in_epss_p2rd,
        lv_disablement_date TYPE datum.

  IF w_code2 = 'RD1'.
    IF zim_in_epss_p2rd-begda IS  INITIAL.
      MESSAGE 'Date From must be informed'  TYPE 'E'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_P2RD-BEGDA'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_epss_p2rd-endda IS  INITIAL.
      MESSAGE 'Date To must be informed'  TYPE 'E'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_P2RD-ENDDA'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_epss_p2rd-endda LT zim_in_epss_p2rd-begda.
      MESSAGE 'Date From cannot be greater than Date To'  TYPE 'E'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_P2RD-ENDDA'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

    lv_days = zim_in_epss_p2rd-begda - zim_in_epss_p2rd-endda.
    IF lv_days LT 6. " 6 as statement above does not count endda
      MESSAGE 'Return to Duty period must be 7 or more days'  TYPE 'E'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_P2RD-ENDDA'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  " Check for overlapping records
  REFRESH lt_p2rd.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_epss_p2rd INTO TABLE lt_p2rd
    WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
      AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
  sy-subrc = 4.
  LOOP AT lt_p2rd INTO ls_p2rd
    WHERE ( ( begda = zim_in_epss_p2rd-begda AND endda = zim_in_epss_p2rd-endda )
      OR ( ( begda BETWEEN  zim_in_epss_p2rd-begda AND zim_in_epss_p2rd-endda )
             OR
            ( endda BETWEEN  zim_in_epss_p2rd-begda AND zim_in_epss_p2rd-endda ) ) )
      AND inactive EQ zim_in_epss_p2rd-inactive .
    EXIT.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    MESSAGE 'Return to Duty period cannot overlap'  TYPE 'E'.
    SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_P2RD-ENDDA'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_4104
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_4105
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       validate screen 4105
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_4105 .
  DATA: lv_date   TYPE datum,
        lv_char10 TYPE char10,
        lv_disablement_date TYPE datum.

  IF w_code2 = 'OE1'.
    IF zim_in_epss_earn-begda IS  INITIAL.
      MESSAGE 'Date From must be informed'  TYPE 'E'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_EARN-BEGDA'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_epss_earn-endda IS  INITIAL.
      MESSAGE 'Date To must be informed'  TYPE 'E'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_EARN-ENDDA'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_epss_earn-endda LT zim_in_epss_earn-begda.
      MESSAGE 'Date From cannot be greater than Date To'  TYPE 'E'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_EARN-ENDDA'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_4105
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FREE_IP_REC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Freen IP Record
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM free_ip_rec .
  IF NOT clm_ip_container IS INITIAL.
    " destroy ALV
    CALL METHOD clm_ip_container->free
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_system_error = 1
        cntl_error        = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_system_error = 1
        cntl_error        = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR: clm_ip_container.
    CLEAR: clm_ip_alv.
    CLEAR: gr_ip_rec.     "clear out the object
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " FREE_IP_REC

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_IP_REC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_ip_rec .
  DATA: lt_ip_rec     TYPE TABLE OF zim_in_ip,
        ls_ip_rec     TYPE zim_in_ip,
        lt_ip_rec_n   TYPE TABLE OF zim_in_ip,
        ls_ip_rec_n   TYPE zim_in_ip,
        lt_date_range TYPE zca_date_range_upd_tab,
        lt_ip_upd     TYPE TABLE OF zim_in_ip_upd,
        lv_mc_ben_st  TYPE zim_mc_ben_status,
        ls_endda      TYPE endda,
        lv_60th       TYPE datum,
        ls_ip_upd     TYPE zim_in_ip_upd,
        lv_error      TYPE boole_d,
        l_date        TYPE d.


  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
        <ls_date_range> TYPE zca_date_range_upd.

* check if saving required for IP Record
  IF zim_in_ip-begda IS NOT INITIAL.

* check if current record type is set to the right record
    IF w_record_type NE gr_ip_rec->gv_rec_typ_current.
      gr_ip_rec->set_current_record_type( iv_rec_type = w_record_type ) .
    ENDIF.

* keep relevant records
    lt_ip_rec = gr_ip_rec->gt_ip.
    DELETE lt_ip_rec WHERE endda LT zim_in_ip-begda.
    DELETE lt_ip_rec WHERE begda GT zim_in_ip-endda.

* form the date range table to pass it to zcl_olims_prim_claim=>delimit_date_ranges
    LOOP AT lt_ip_rec  INTO ls_ip_rec.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_date_range ASSIGNING <ls_date_range>.
      <ls_date_range>-source_idx = sy-tabix.
      <ls_date_range>-begda = ls_ip_rec-begda.
      <ls_date_range>-endda = ls_ip_rec-endda.
    ENDLOOP.

* delimit or overwrite records, overlapping not allowed
    CALL METHOD zcl_olims_prim_claim=>add_date_range
      EXPORTING
        iv_begda      = zim_in_ip-begda
        iv_endda      = zim_in_ip-endda
      CHANGING
        ct_date_range = lt_date_range.

* Convert
    CALL METHOD gr_ip_rec->convert_date_range_to_ip_upd
      EXPORTING
        it_date_range = lt_date_range
        it_ip_table   = lt_ip_rec
        is_ip_new     = zim_in_ip
      IMPORTING
        et_ip_upd     = lt_ip_upd.

*tvermeulen
*Change 1
*Validation required when creating Top-up records so that a Top-up record cannot be created with the flag 'Top-up'
*for a period greater than 273 days. Note but this record is allowable for any periods
*including end date 31/12/9999 when the 'Top-up' flag is NOT ticked.
    IF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_top.

      IF zim_in_ip-top_up = 'X'.

        zim_in_cm-day_271 = zcl_olims_config=>get_topup_period_date( zim_in_ip-begda ) + 1.
        l_date = zim_in_cm-day_271 - 1.

        IF zim_in_ip-endda GT l_date.

          CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_DISPLAY_TEXT'
            EXPORTING
              titel        = 'Top-up record'
              textline1    = 'Top-up record cannot be created with the flag Top-up'
              textline2    = 'for a period greater than 273 days'
              start_column = 25
              start_row    = 6.

          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Top-up validation failed'.


        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03
    IF w_epss = abap_true AND
      ( zim_in_cm-transition_to_epss = abap_true OR lw_in_cm-transition_to_epss = abap_true ).

      CALL METHOD gr_ip_rec->deactivate_ip_records( ).

    ENDIF.
*>>> AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03

* Put update table to Object
    CALL METHOD gr_ip_rec->put_ip_upd
      EXPORTING
        it_ip_upd = lt_ip_upd.

* Save changes to DB
    CALL METHOD gr_ip_rec->save_ip_to_db( ).

* Determine if any medical certificates exist for the period
* that could result in changed WC Recoup Benefit records
    IF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_dis.
      lv_mc_ben_st = 'I'.
    ELSEIF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_top.
      lv_mc_ben_st = 'T'.
    ELSEIF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_epss_p1. " AZORBA EPSS BRD 03
      lv_mc_ben_st = '1'.
    ENDIF.



    IF lv_mc_ben_st IS  NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM check_med_cert USING lv_mc_ben_st
                                   zim_in_ip-begda
                                   zim_in_ip-endda.
    ENDIF.

*tvermeulen

* Do not Automatically adjust the IP record when the Top-up has changed.
* Change approved by DC on 30/1/2019
*    IF lv_mc_ben_st = 'T'.
*
*      CLEAR gr_ip_rec.
*
*      IF gr_ip_rec IS INITIAL.
*        CREATE OBJECT gr_ip_rec
*          EXPORTING
*            iv_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
*            iv_incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
*      ENDIF.
*
*      gr_ip_rec->set_current_record_type( iv_rec_type = w_record_type ) .
*
*      lt_ip_rec_n = gr_ip_rec->gt_ip.
*
*      DELETE lt_ip_rec_n WHERE record_type NE zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_top
*      AND top_up EQ 'X'.
*
*      SORT lt_ip_rec_n BY begda.
*
*      IF lt_ip_rec_n IS NOT INITIAL.
*        LOOP AT lt_ip_rec_n INTO ls_ip_rec_n.
*        ENDLOOP.
*      ENDIF.
*
*      lv_60th  = zcl_olims_config=>get_60th_birthday( zim_in_mast-pernr ) - 1.
*
*      IF lv_60th LT ls_ip_rec_n-begda.
*        ls_ip_rec_n-begda = lv_60th.
*      ENDIF.
*
** Save the deletion to the audit log table
*      CLEAR ls_ip_upd.
*      CLEAR lt_ip_upd.
*      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_ip
*             INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF ls_ip_upd
*             WHERE pernr       = zim_in_cm-pernr
*               AND record_type = zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_ip
*               AND incident    = zim_in_cm-incident.
*      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
*        CLEAR ls_ip_upd.
*      ENDIF.
*      ls_ip_upd-updkz = 'D'.
*      APPEND ls_ip_upd TO lt_ip_upd.
*      CALL METHOD gr_ip_rec->put_ip_upd
*        EXPORTING
*          it_ip_upd = lt_ip_upd.
*      gr_ip_rec->save_ip_log_to_db( IMPORTING ev_error = lv_error ).
*
*      UPDATE zim_in_ip SET begda = ls_ip_rec_n-begda
*      WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-pernr AND record_type = zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_ip
*      AND incident = zim_in_cm-incident.
*
*      ls_endda =  zcl_olims_config=>get_ip_end_date_on( ls_ip_rec_n-begda ) - 1.
*
*      IF lv_60th LT ls_endda.
*        ls_endda = lv_60th.
*      ENDIF.
*
*      WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
*
*      UPDATE zim_in_ip SET endda = ls_endda
*      WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-pernr AND record_type = zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_ip
*      AND incident = zim_in_cm-incident.
*
** Save the insertion to the audit log table
*      CLEAR lt_ip_upd.
*      ls_ip_upd-begda = ls_ip_rec_n-begda.
*      ls_ip_upd-endda = ls_endda.
*      ls_ip_upd-updkz = 'I'.
*      APPEND ls_ip_upd TO lt_ip_upd.
*      CALL METHOD gr_ip_rec->put_ip_upd
*        EXPORTING
*          it_ip_upd = lt_ip_upd.
*      gr_ip_rec->save_ip_log_to_db( IMPORTING ev_error = lv_error ).
*
*    ENDIF.


* Refresh ALV data
    CLEAR gr_ip_rec.    "clear out the object
    w_3417_refresh = abap_true.
    CLEAR: zim_in_ip.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE role = 'C'.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR zim_advisor.
    ENDIF.

* Reminder to inform the Case Management Team Leader
* that a top-up/disablement date/IP date has been changed
    PERFORM create_reminder USING 'C'
                                  zim_advisor-ima
                                  '80'
                                  '18'
                                  ' '
                                  '00000000'.

  ENDIF.

* check and create default record
  PERFORM create_ip_defaults.

ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_IP_REC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  USER_COMMAND_3417
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM user_command_3417 .
  CASE w_code2.
    WHEN 'IPREC'.
      PERFORM alv_table_refresh_3417  USING  space.
    WHEN 'IPHIST'.
      PERFORM alv_table_refresh_3417  USING  abap_true.
    WHEN 'DEF'.
      PERFORM create_default_record_type.
    WHEN 'CLR'.
      CLEAR zim_in_ip.
  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.                    " USER_COMMAND_3417
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  USER_COMMAND_4104
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM user_command_4104 .
  CASE w_code2.
    WHEN 'RD1'.
      PERFORM create_return_to_duty_period.
    WHEN 'CLR'.
      CLEAR zim_in_epss_p2rd.
  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.                    " USER_COMMAND_4104
