*&      Form  USER_COMMAND_4105
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM user_command_4105 .
  CASE w_code2.
    WHEN 'OE1'.
      PERFORM create_other_ext_earnings.
    WHEN 'CLR'.
      CLEAR zim_in_epss_earn.
  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.                    " USER_COMMAND_4105



*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ALV_TABLE_REFRESH
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM alv_table_refresh_3417  USING    pv_hist TYPE boole_d.

  gr_ip_rec->set_history( iv_hist = pv_hist
                          iv_rec_type = w_record_type ).

* Outtab for ALV Display
  PERFORM fill_outtab_3417.
  PERFORM build_fieldcat_ip_rec  USING pv_hist
                              CHANGING clm_ip_fieldcat.
* change the fieldcatalog
  clm_ip_alv->set_frontend_fieldcatalog(
    EXPORTING it_fieldcatalog = clm_ip_fieldcat ).

* optimize layout
  clm_ip_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
  CALL METHOD clm_ip_alv->set_frontend_layout
    EXPORTING
      is_layout = clm_ip_layout.

* Refresh ALV
  CALL METHOD clm_ip_alv->refresh_table_display( ).
ENDFORM.                    " ALV_TABLE_REFRESH
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CREATE_DEFAULT_RECORD_TYPE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM create_default_record_type.
  DATA: ls_ip_rec     TYPE zim_in_ip,
        lv_mc_ben_st  TYPE zim_mc_ben_status,
        lv_date       TYPE sy-datum.

  lv_date = gr_ip_rec->get_disablement_date( ).

*tvermeulen
*Change 1
*If DOD already exists in case details tab (table ZIM_IN_CM) and a claim status is changed to 're-opened',
*the date record types in the Top-Up/Disab. Date/IP Dates tab (table ZIM_IN_IP)
*should be updated according to current DOD - it currently defaults to the incident date overriding
*the existing DOD (examples in below thread). This is problematic as WC unit may have
*previously adjusted the DOD and it may differ from incident date.


* Y Chen - the calculations of dates need to be reviewed and adjusted
  IF lv_date IS INITIAL.
    lv_date = zim_in_cm-disablement_date.
  ENDIF.

*  if lv_date is initial.
*    lv_date = zim_incident-dateincident.
*  endif.

  CASE w_record_type.
    WHEN zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_top.
      gr_ip_rec->create_default_top( EXPORTING iv_disablement_date = lv_date
                                               iv_duty_det         = zim_in_cm-duty_det
                                     IMPORTING es_ip_rec = ls_ip_rec ).
      lv_mc_ben_st = 'T'.
    WHEN zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_dis.
      gr_ip_rec->create_default_dis( EXPORTING iv_incident_date = lv_date
                                     IMPORTING es_ip_rec = ls_ip_rec ).
      lv_mc_ben_st = 'I'.
    WHEN zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_ip.
      gr_ip_rec->create_default_ip( EXPORTING iv_disablement_date = lv_date
                                              iv_duty_det = zim_in_cm-duty_det
                                    IMPORTING es_ip_rec = ls_ip_rec ).
*<<< AZORBA EPSS BRD 03 - Adding record type EPSS
    WHEN zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_epss_p1 OR zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_epss_p2.
      gr_ip_rec->create_default_epss( EXPORTING iv_disablement_date = lv_date
                                              iv_duty_det = zim_in_cm-duty_det
                                    IMPORTING es_ip_rec = ls_ip_rec ).
*>>> AZORBA
    WHEN OTHERS.
      MESSAGE 'Record Type must be selected before creating a default record' TYPE 'I'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'W_RECORD_TYPE'.
  ENDCASE.

* Determine if any medical certificates exist for the period
* that could result in changed WC Recoup Benefit records
  IF lv_mc_ben_st IS  NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM check_med_cert USING lv_mc_ben_st
                                 ls_ip_rec-begda
                                 ls_ip_rec-endda.
  ENDIF.

* Refresh ALV data
  IF ls_ip_rec IS NOT INITIAL.
    MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Default record has been'
                      'successfully saved'.
    CLEAR gr_ip_rec.    "clear out the object
    w_3417_refresh = abap_true.
    CLEAR: zim_in_ip.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " CREATE_DEFAULT_RECORD_TYPE

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CREATE_RETURN_TO_DUTY_PERIOD
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM create_return_to_duty_period.

  DATA: ls_epss_p2rd TYPE zim_in_epss_p2rd.

  IF zim_in_epss_p2rd IS NOT INITIAL.

    IF zim_in_epss_p2rd-begda IS  INITIAL.
      MESSAGE 'Date From must be informed'  TYPE 'E'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_P2RD-BEGDA'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_epss_p2rd-endda IS  INITIAL.
      MESSAGE 'Date To must be informed'  TYPE 'E'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_P2RD-ENDDA'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_epss_p2rd-endda LT zim_in_epss_p2rd-begda.
      MESSAGE 'Date From cannot be greater than Date To'  TYPE 'E'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_P2RD-ENDDA'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR zim_in_epss_p2rd-seq_no.
    IF zim_in_epss_p2rd-inactive = abap_true.
      CLEAR ls_epss_p2rd.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_epss_p2rd INTO ls_epss_p2rd
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND begda = zim_in_epss_p2rd-begda
           AND endda = zim_in_epss_p2rd-endda.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        zim_in_epss_p2rd-seq_no = ls_epss_p2rd-seq_no.
        zim_in_epss_p2rd-description = ls_epss_p2rd-description.
      ELSE.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'There is no previous period with'
                          'the provided start/end dates'.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.

      CLEAR ls_epss_p2rd.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_epss_p2rd INTO ls_epss_p2rd
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND begda = zim_in_epss_p2rd-begda
           AND endda = zim_in_epss_p2rd-endda.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        zim_in_epss_p2rd-seq_no = ls_epss_p2rd-seq_no.
        zim_in_epss_p2rd-description = ls_epss_p2rd-description.
        CLEAR zim_in_epss_p2rd-inactive.
      ELSE.

        SORT gt_outtab54 BY seq_no DESCENDING.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab54 INTO gr_outtab54 INDEX 1.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          zim_in_epss_p2rd-seq_no = gr_outtab54-seq_no + 1.
        ELSE.
          zim_in_epss_p2rd-seq_no = 1.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    zim_in_epss_p2rd-pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    zim_in_epss_p2rd-incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
    zim_in_epss_p2rd-upd_date = sy-datum.
    zim_in_epss_p2rd-upd_time = sy-uzeit.
    zim_in_epss_p2rd-upd_user = sy-uname.

    MODIFY zim_in_epss_p2rd FROM zim_in_epss_p2rd.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      COMMIT WORK.
      MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Period has been'
                        'successfully saved'.

      w_4104_refresh = abap_true.
      CLEAR: zim_in_epss_p2rd.

*     Re-calculate EPSS dates
      PERFORM calculate_epss_dates USING zim_in_mast-pernr zim_in_mast-incident.
*     Update the (new) IP Estimated END DATE in the case management table - ZIM_IN_CM
      PERFORM update_in_cm_table.

    ELSE.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF. " if ZIM_IN_EPSS_P2RD is not INITIAL.

ENDFORM.                    " CREATE_RETURN_TO_DUTY_PERIOD

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  create_other_ext_earnings
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM create_other_ext_earnings.

  DATA: ls_epss_earn TYPE zim_in_epss_earn.

  IF zim_in_epss_earn IS NOT INITIAL.

    IF zim_in_epss_earn-begda IS  INITIAL.
      MESSAGE 'Date From must be informed'  TYPE 'E'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_EARN-BEGDA'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_epss_earn-endda IS  INITIAL.
      MESSAGE 'Date To must be informed'  TYPE 'E'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_EARN-ENDDA'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_epss_earn-endda LT zim_in_epss_earn-begda.
      MESSAGE 'Date From cannot be greater than Date To'  TYPE 'E'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_EARN-ENDDA'.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR zim_in_epss_earn-seq_no.
    IF zim_in_epss_earn-inactive = abap_true.
      CLEAR ls_epss_earn.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_epss_earn INTO ls_epss_earn
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND begda = zim_in_epss_earn-begda
           AND endda = zim_in_epss_earn-endda
           AND earning_type = zim_in_epss_earn-earning_type.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        zim_in_epss_earn-seq_no = ls_epss_earn-seq_no.
        zim_in_epss_earn-description = ls_epss_earn-description.
      ELSE.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'There is no previous period with'
                          'the provided start/end dates'.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      SORT gt_outtab55 BY seq_no DESCENDING.
      READ TABLE gt_outtab55 INTO gr_outtab55 INDEX 1.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        zim_in_epss_earn-seq_no = gr_outtab55-seq_no + 1.
      ELSE.
        zim_in_epss_earn-seq_no = 1.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    zim_in_epss_earn-pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    zim_in_epss_earn-incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
    zim_in_epss_earn-upd_date = sy-datum.
    zim_in_epss_earn-upd_time = sy-uzeit.
    zim_in_epss_earn-upd_user = sy-uname.

    MODIFY zim_in_epss_earn FROM zim_in_epss_earn.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      COMMIT WORK.
      MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Other/External Earning has '
                        'been successfully saved'.

      w_4106_refresh = abap_true.
      CLEAR: zim_in_epss_earn.

*     Re-calculate EPSS dates
      PERFORM calculate_epss_dates USING zim_in_mast-pernr zim_in_mast-incident.

    ELSE.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF. " if ZIM_IN_EPSS_earn is not INITIAL.

ENDFORM.                    " create_other_ext_earnings

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CREATE_IP_DEFAULTS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM create_ip_defaults .

  DATA: ls_ip_rec   TYPE zim_in_ip.

  IF zim_in_cm-wc_status NE 'C' OR
     ( zim_in_cm-ip_status NE 'C' AND
       zim_in_cm-ip_status IS NOT INITIAL ).

* create defaults for new claims

*  if zim_in_cm-disablement_date is initial and
    IF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim    IS INITIAL.
      PERFORM get_ip_records.
*   If any history record exist, don't create default records
      IF zim_in_cm-disablement_date IS NOT INITIAL.
        IF gr_ip_rec->get_ip_including_inactive( ) IS INITIAL.
* create default date of disablement from date of incident
          w_ip_dates_create = 'X'.
          IF gr_ip_rec->get_ip_dis( )   IS  INITIAL.
            gr_ip_rec->create_default_dis( EXPORTING iv_incident_date = zim_in_cm-disablement_date
                                           IMPORTING es_ip_rec = ls_ip_rec ).
          ENDIF.

          IF w_epss IS INITIAL. " AZORBA EPSS BRD 03
*   create default IP dates and Top-Up records
            IF gr_ip_rec->get_ip_ip( ) IS INITIAL.
*     default disablement date is date of incident
              w_ip_dates_create = 'X'.
              gr_ip_rec->create_default_ip( EXPORTING iv_disablement_date = zim_in_cm-disablement_date
                                                      iv_duty_det = zim_in_cm-duty_det
                                            IMPORTING es_ip_rec = ls_ip_rec ).
            ENDIF.

            IF gr_ip_rec->get_ip_top( ) IS  INITIAL.
*     default disablement date is date of incident
              w_ip_dates_create = 'X'.
              gr_ip_rec->create_default_top( EXPORTING iv_disablement_date = zim_in_cm-disablement_date
                                                       iv_duty_det         = zim_in_cm-duty_det
                                             IMPORTING es_ip_rec = ls_ip_rec ).
            ENDIF.

*<<< AZORBA EPSS BRD 03 - Adding logic for EPSS record type
          ELSE. " EPSS
*   create default EPSS dates
            IF gr_ip_rec->get_ip_epss( iv_period = '1' ) IS INITIAL.
              w_epss_dates_create = 'X'.
              gr_ip_rec->create_default_epss( EXPORTING iv_disablement_date = zim_in_cm-disablement_date
                                                        iv_duty_det = zim_in_cm-duty_det
                                              IMPORTING es_ip_rec = ls_ip_rec ).
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

          CLEAR gr_ip_rec.
          PERFORM get_ip_records.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " CREATE_IP_DEFAULTS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  UPDATE_IN_CM_TABLE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM update_in_cm_table .


  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm
         INTO w_in_cm
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    w_action           = 'I'.
    INSERT zim_in_cm.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_CM'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM update_in_cm_log USING w_action.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    IF zim_in_cm NE w_in_cm.
      w_action           = 'U'.
      UPDATE zim_in_cm.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_CM'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_in_cm_log USING w_action.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " UPDATE_IN_CM_TABLE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_EPSS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_epss .

  DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
  DATA: lt_p0003 TYPE p0003 OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE.
  DATA: lt_hrpy_rgdir TYPE TABLE OF hrpy_rgdir.
  DATA: ls_hrpy_rgdir TYPE hrpy_rgdir,
        lv_operation            LIKE  pspar-actio,
        lv_p2_start_date    TYPE datum,
        lv_update_it0003    TYPE boole_d,
        lv_dialog(1)            TYPE c.
  DATA: ls_bapiret                 TYPE bapireturn1,
        ls_bapi_key                TYPE bapipakey.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_p0003> LIKE LINE OF lt_p0003.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_epss
         INTO w_in_epss
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    zim_in_epss-pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr.
    zim_in_epss-incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
    zim_in_epss-disablement_date = zim_in_cm-disablement_date.
    CLEAR zim_in_epss-commence_p2_payment.  " EPSS Phase 4 - Commence Period 2 flag - DEVK9HRGPL
    INSERT zim_in_epss.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_EPSS'
                        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR zim_in_epss_log.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_in_epss TO zim_in_epss_log.
      zim_in_epss_log-date_processed = sy-datum.
      zim_in_epss_log-time_processed = sy-uzeit.
      zim_in_epss_log-user_processed = sy-uname.
      INSERT zim_in_epss_log.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when inserting ZIM_IN_EPSS_LOG'
                          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
*<<< EPSS Phase 4 - Commence Period 2 flag - DEVK9HRGPL
  ELSE.
* EPSS incident already created, check whether commence p2 payment flag has changed and requires table update.


    IF zim_in_epss-commence_p2_payment NE w_epss_det-commence_p2_payment.

      IF zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p2 IS INITIAL.
        lv_p2_start_date = zim_in_cm-epss_init_start_date_p2.
      ELSE.
        lv_p2_start_date = zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p2.
      ENDIF.

      lv_update_it0003 = abap_true.
*     If p2 has not commenced yet and there are NOT any pay results, forcing retro is not required.
      IF sy-datum LT lv_p2_start_date.
        REFRESH lt_hrpy_rgdir.

        SELECT * FROM hrpy_rgdir INTO TABLE lt_hrpy_rgdir
          WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
            AND fpend GE lv_p2_start_date.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_update_it0003 = abap_false.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF. " IF sy-datum LT zim_in_epss-commence_p2_payment.


      IF lv_update_it0003 = abap_true.
        w_error = abap_false.
        PERFORM lock_employee USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                    '0003'.

        IF w_error = abap_true.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The employee is currently'
                            'being updated in PA30'
                            'by another user'.
        ENDIF.

*     These functions need to be called to refresh the buffers.
        CALL FUNCTION 'HR_PSBUFFER_INITIALIZE'.
        CALL FUNCTION 'HRPAD00_FREE_PLOG_TAB'.
        CALL FUNCTION 'HR_PSA_CLEAR_BUFFER'.

        " Read Earliest MD Change field from infotype 0003
        CLEAR lv_subrc.
        REFRESH lt_p0003.
        CALL FUNCTION 'HR_READ_INFOTYPE'
          EXPORTING
            pernr           = zim_in_mast-pernr
            infty           = '0003'
            begda           = lv_p2_start_date
            endda           = lv_p2_start_date
          IMPORTING
            subrc           = lv_subrc
          TABLES
            infty_tab       = lt_p0003
          EXCEPTIONS
            infty_not_found = 1
            OTHERS          = 2.

        IF lv_subrc IS INITIAL.
          READ TABLE lt_p0003 ASSIGNING <fs_p0003> WITH KEY pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr.
          IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
            IF <fs_p0003>-rrdat GT lv_p2_start_date
            OR <fs_p0003>-rrdat IS INITIAL.
              " Update Earliest MD Change field in infotype 0003 to the first day of EPSS week 40
              <fs_p0003>-rrdat = lv_p2_start_date.

              lv_operation = 'MOD'.
              lv_dialog    = '1'.

              CALL FUNCTION 'HR_INFOTYPE_OPERATION'
                EXPORTING
                  infty         = '0003'
                  number        = <fs_p0003>-pernr
                  subtype       = <fs_p0003>-subty
                  lockindicator = <fs_p0003>-sprps
                  validityend   = <fs_p0003>-endda
                  validitybegin = <fs_p0003>-begda
                  recordnumber  = <fs_p0003>-seqnr
                  objectid      = <fs_p0003>-objps
                  record        = <fs_p0003>
                  operation     = lv_operation
                  dialog_mode   = lv_dialog
                IMPORTING
                  return        = ls_bapiret
                  key           = ls_bapi_key.

              IF ls_bapiret-type = 'E' OR
                 ls_bapiret-type = 'A'.
                ROLLBACK WORK.
                MESSAGE ID     ls_bapiret-id
                        TYPE   ls_bapiret-type
                        NUMBER ls_bapiret-number
                        WITH   ls_bapiret-message_v1
                               ls_bapiret-message_v2
                               ls_bapiret-message_v3
                               ls_bapiret-message_v4.
                w_error = abap_true.
              ENDIF.


              PERFORM unlock_employee USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                        '0003'.

            ENDIF. " IF <fs_p0003>-rrdat GT zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p2
          ENDIF. " READ TABLE lt_p0003 ASSIGNING <fs_p0003> WITH KEY pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr.
        ENDIF. " HR_READ_INFOTYPE IT0003
      ENDIF. " IF lv_update_it0003 = abap_true.

      IF w_error = abap_false.

        zim_in_epss-commence_p2_payment = w_epss_det-commence_p2_payment.

        " No Errors
        UPDATE zim_in_epss.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          w_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_EPSS'
                            'Return code ' w_subrc.
        ELSE.
          CLEAR zim_in_epss_log.
          MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_in_epss TO zim_in_epss_log.
          zim_in_epss_log-date_processed = sy-datum.
          zim_in_epss_log-time_processed = sy-uzeit.
          zim_in_epss_log-user_processed = sy-uname.
          INSERT zim_in_epss_log.
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
            w_subrc = sy-subrc.
            ROLLBACK WORK.
            MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Error occurred when inserting ZIM_IN_EPSS_LOG'
                              'Return code ' w_subrc.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF. "UPDATE zim_in_epss.
      ENDIF. " IF w_error = abap_false.
    ENDIF. " IF zim_in_epss-commence_p2_payment NE w_epss_det-commence_p2_payment.
  ENDIF. " SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_epss
*>>> EPSS Phase 4 - Commence Period 2 flag - DEVK9HRGPL


ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_EPSS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  DETERMINE_IF_EPSS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM determine_if_epss USING p_pernr         TYPE pernr_d
                             p_incident      TYPE zhr_inc_identnr.

  DATA: lv_epss_go_live        TYPE datum,
        lv_transition_to_epss  TYPE zim_transition_to_epss,
        lv_disablement_date    TYPE datum.
  CONSTANTS: c_subapplication  TYPE p_appl VALUE '9EP1'.

* Implementing the EPSS logic into OLIMS.
* Set w_epss flag which will drive the logic to EPSS
* EPSS is when disablement date is GE 01.10.2024 or incident has been transitioned to EPSS.
  CLEAR: lv_disablement_date, lv_epss_go_live.

  w_epss = abap_false.
  SELECT SINGLE transition_to_epss FROM zim_in_cm INTO lv_transition_to_epss
    WHERE pernr = p_pernr
      AND incident = p_incident.
  IF lv_transition_to_epss = abap_true.
    w_epss = abap_true.
  ELSE.

    " Fetch disablement date from OLIMS - case details
    SELECT SINGLE disablement_date FROM zim_in_cm INTO lv_disablement_date
           WHERE pernr = p_pernr
             AND incident = p_incident.
    IF lv_disablement_date IS INITIAL.
      " Disablement date not set in ZIM_IN_CM yet, fetch it from Certificate
      SELECT SINGLE date_from FROM zim_in_cert INTO lv_disablement_date
             WHERE pernr = p_pernr
               AND incident = p_incident.

    ENDIF.

    " Fetch EPSS go live date
    SELECT SINGLE begda FROM t596d INTO lv_epss_go_live
              WHERE appl  EQ c_subapplication
                AND molga EQ zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_molga_aust.
    IF lv_disablement_date GE lv_epss_go_live
     AND lv_epss_go_live IS NOT INITIAL.
      w_epss = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " DETERMINE_IF_EPSS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_NUMBER_OF_DAYS_IN_7YEARS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Calculate the total number of days for P2 = 7 years
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_P_START  text
*      -->P_P_END  text
*      <--P_LV_TOT_DAYS  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_number_of_days_in_7years  USING    p_start_date
                                   CHANGING p_tot_days TYPE zim_epss_no_days_used_p2 .

  DATA: ls_dur         TYPE psen_duration,
        lv_7years_date TYPE datum,
        lv_tot_days_p  TYPE p.

  CLEAR: lv_tot_days_p, lv_7years_date, p_tot_days.

  ls_dur-duryy = 7.

  CALL FUNCTION 'HR_99S_DATE_ADD_SUB_DURATION'
    EXPORTING
      im_date     = p_start_date
      im_operator = '+'
      im_duration = ls_dur
    IMPORTING
      ex_date     = lv_7years_date.


  CALL FUNCTION '/SDF/CMO_DATETIME_DIFFERENCE'
    EXPORTING
      date1            = p_start_date
      date2            = lv_7years_date
    IMPORTING
      datediff         = lv_tot_days_p
    EXCEPTIONS
      invalid_datetime = 1
      OTHERS           = 2.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    CLEAR p_tot_days.
  ELSE.
    MOVE lv_tot_days_p TO p_tot_days.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_NUMBER_OF_DAYS_IN_7YEARS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_RETURN_TO_DUTY_DAYS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_BEGDA  text
*      -->P_ENDDA  text
*      -->P_KDATE  text
*      <--P_LV_NO_R2D_DAYS_PAST  text
*      <--P_LV_NO_R2D_DAYS_FUTURE  text
*      <--P_NEW_RECORD   text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_return_to_duty_days  USING    p_pernr
                                       p_incident
                                       p_begda
                                       p_endda
                                       p_kdate
                              CHANGING p_tot_r2d_days_past
                                       p_tot_r2d_days_future
                                       p_new_record.

  DATA: lv_no_r2d_days_past  TYPE zim_epss_no_days_used_p2,
        lv_no_r2d_days_future TYPE zim_epss_no_days_used_p2,
        lt_epss_p2rd         TYPE TABLE OF zim_in_epss_p2rd,
        ls_epss_p2rd         TYPE zim_in_epss_p2rd.

* When an employee returns to duty the clock stops for the EPSS period 2.
* The return to duty periods are manually entered via the EPSS Period 2 - Return to Duty tab.
* Here the logic checks whether a R2D record spans within the period of period 2 as sometimes
* a record can start before or/and end after (and vice-versa) the period 2 period.
* In addition to that, the logic is separating the number of days of return to duty past and future based
* on the current date(kdate). This is to display the correct number of days remaining/used of period 2.

  CLEAR: lt_epss_p2rd[], p_new_record, p_tot_r2d_days_past, p_tot_r2d_days_future.

  SELECT * FROM zim_in_epss_p2rd INTO TABLE lt_epss_p2rd
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.

  SORT lt_epss_p2rd BY seq_no.
  lv_no_r2d_days_past = p_tot_r2d_days_past = p_tot_r2d_days_future = lv_no_r2d_days_future = 0.

  LOOP AT lt_epss_p2rd INTO ls_epss_p2rd WHERE begda LE p_endda
                                           AND endda GE p_begda.


    IF ls_epss_p2rd-upd_date EQ sy-datum.
      " New record meaning the start/end dates of Period 2 will change and needs to be updated in the ZIM_IN_CM table.
      p_new_record = abap_true.
    ENDIF.

    IF ls_epss_p2rd-inactive = abap_true.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.
    " Period starts and ends in the past
    IF ls_epss_p2rd-begda LT p_kdate AND
       ls_epss_p2rd-endda LE p_kdate.

      IF ls_epss_p2rd-begda LT p_begda.

        lv_no_r2d_days_past = p_begda - ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
        lv_no_r2d_days_past = lv_no_r2d_days_past + 1. " +1 to include end date
        IF lv_no_r2d_days_past LT 7.
          " Skip record as Return to duty only applies if period is more 7 days
          CONTINUE.
        ELSE.
          IF p_begda LT ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
            " Record starts before and ends after period 2 period.
            " Only consider the number of days within Period 2
            lv_no_r2d_days_past = p_begda - p_endda.
            lv_no_r2d_days_past = lv_no_r2d_days_past + 1. " +1 to include end date
          ELSE.
            " Only consider the number of days starting from the start date of Period 2
            lv_no_r2d_days_past = p_begda - ls_epss_p2rd-endda .
            lv_no_r2d_days_past = lv_no_r2d_days_past + 1. " +1 to include end date
          ENDIF. " IF p_begda LT ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
        ENDIF. " IF lv_no_r2d_days_past LE 7.

      ELSE.
        " Record starts after P2 start date
        IF p_endda LT ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
          " Record end date is after period 2 end date.
          lv_no_r2d_days_past = ls_epss_p2rd-begda - p_endda.
          lv_no_r2d_days_past = lv_no_r2d_days_past + 1. " +1 to include end date
        ELSE.
          " Record starts and ends within Period 2 period.
          lv_no_r2d_days_past = ls_epss_p2rd-begda - ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
          lv_no_r2d_days_past = lv_no_r2d_days_past + 1. " +1 to include end date

        ENDIF. " IF p_endda LT ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
      ENDIF. " IF ls_epss_p2rd-begda LT p_begda.


      " Period starts and ends in the future
    ELSEIF ls_epss_p2rd-begda GT p_kdate AND
           ls_epss_p2rd-endda GT p_kdate.

      IF ls_epss_p2rd-begda LT p_begda.

        lv_no_r2d_days_future = p_begda - ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
        lv_no_r2d_days_future = lv_no_r2d_days_future + 1. " +1 to include end date

        IF lv_no_r2d_days_future LT 7.
          " Skip record as Return to duty only applies if period is more 7 days
          CONTINUE.
        ELSE.
          IF p_endda LT ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
            " Record starts before and ends after period 2 period.
            " Only consider the number of days within Period 2
            lv_no_r2d_days_future = p_begda - p_endda.
            lv_no_r2d_days_future = lv_no_r2d_days_future + 1. " +1 to include end date
          ELSE.
            " Only consider the number of days starting from the start date of Period 2
            lv_no_r2d_days_future = p_begda - ls_epss_p2rd-endda .
            lv_no_r2d_days_future = lv_no_r2d_days_future + 1. " +1 to include end date
          ENDIF. " IF p_begda LT ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
        ENDIF. " IF lv_no_r2d_days_past LE 7.

      ELSE.
        " Record starts after P2 start date
        IF p_endda LT ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
          " Record end date is after period 2 end date.
          lv_no_r2d_days_future = ls_epss_p2rd-begda - p_endda.
          lv_no_r2d_days_future = lv_no_r2d_days_future + 1. " +1 to include end date
        ELSE.
          " Record starts and ends within Period 2 period.
          lv_no_r2d_days_future = ls_epss_p2rd-begda - ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
          lv_no_r2d_days_future = lv_no_r2d_days_future + 1. " +1 to include end date

        ENDIF. " IF p_endda LT ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
      ENDIF. " IF ls_epss_p2rd-begda LT p_begda.

      " Period starts in the past and ends in the future
    ELSEIF ls_epss_p2rd-begda LT p_kdate AND
           ls_epss_p2rd-endda GT p_kdate.

      lv_no_r2d_days_past = ls_epss_p2rd-begda - p_kdate.
      lv_no_r2d_days_past = lv_no_r2d_days_past + 1.  " +1 to include current date
      lv_no_r2d_days_future = ls_epss_p2rd-endda - p_kdate.

      IF ls_epss_p2rd-begda LT p_begda.

        lv_no_r2d_days_past = p_begda - p_kdate.
        lv_no_r2d_days_past = lv_no_r2d_days_past + 1. " +1 to include end date
        IF lv_no_r2d_days_past LT 7.
          " Skip record as Return to duty only applies if period is more 7 days
          CONTINUE.
        ELSE.
          IF p_endda LT ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
            " Record starts before and ends after period 2 period.
            " Only consider the number of days within Period 2
            lv_no_r2d_days_past = p_begda - p_kdate.
            lv_no_r2d_days_past = lv_no_r2d_days_past + 1. " +1 to include end date

            lv_no_r2d_days_future = p_kdate - p_endda.
            lv_no_r2d_days_future = lv_no_r2d_days_future + 1. " +1 to include end date

          ELSE.
            " Only consider the number of days starting from the start date of Period 2
            lv_no_r2d_days_past = p_begda - p_kdate .
            lv_no_r2d_days_past = lv_no_r2d_days_past + 1. " +1 to include end date

            lv_no_r2d_days_future = p_kdate - ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
            lv_no_r2d_days_future = lv_no_r2d_days_past + 1. " +1 to include end date

          ENDIF. " IF p_begda LT ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
        ENDIF. " IF lv_no_r2d_days_past LE 7.

      ELSE. " ls_epss_p2rd-begda GE p_begda.

        " Record starts after P2 start date
        IF p_endda LT ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
          " Record end date is after period 2 end date.
          lv_no_r2d_days_past = ls_epss_p2rd-begda - p_kdate.
          lv_no_r2d_days_past = lv_no_r2d_days_past + 1. " +1 to include end date

          lv_no_r2d_days_future = p_kdate - p_endda.
          lv_no_r2d_days_future = lv_no_r2d_days_future + 1. " +1 to include end date

        ELSE.
          " Only consider the number of days starting from the start date of Period 2
          lv_no_r2d_days_past = ls_epss_p2rd-begda - p_kdate .
          lv_no_r2d_days_past = lv_no_r2d_days_past + 1. " +1 to include end date

          lv_no_r2d_days_future = p_kdate - ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
          lv_no_r2d_days_future = lv_no_r2d_days_past + 1. " +1 to include end date

        ENDIF. " IF p_endda LT ls_epss_p2rd-endda.
      ENDIF. " IF ls_epss_p2rd-begda LT p_begda.

    ENDIF. " IF ls_epss_p2rd-begda LT p_kdate AND ls_epss_p2rd-endda LE p_kdate.

    ADD lv_no_r2d_days_past TO p_tot_r2d_days_past.
    ADD lv_no_r2d_days_future TO p_tot_r2d_days_future.
    CLEAR: lv_no_r2d_days_past, lv_no_r2d_days_future.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_RETURN_TO_DUTY_DAYS
